local function model(partN)
    for _, v in pairs(Map:GetDescendants()) do
        if v.Name == partN and v:IsA("BasePart") then
            return v.Color
        end
    end
end

local function FrostNotify(Message)
    OrionLib:Notification({
        Name = "Frost Client",
        Content = Message,
        Image = "rbxassetid://16570630989",
        Time = 3
    })
end

local function GetPlayerCharacter()
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
        return char
    end
end

local function GetCharacter()
    return localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
end

local function PLCF()
    local c = GetCharacter()
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and c.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
end

local function HRP()
    local char = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    return char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
end

local function GetRoot(character)
    local char = character or localPlayer.Character
    return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function getLocalHum()
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function isDeveloper(userId)
    return developerDESU[userId] == true
end

local function lookAt(origin, player)
    local forward = (player - origin).Unit
    
    local right = forward:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)).Unit
    
    local up = right:Cross(forward)
    
    return CFrame.fromMatrix(origin, right, up)
end

local function CNWOSHIPOPl(pler)
    if typeof(pler) == "Instance" and pler:IsA("Player") and pler.Character then
        local head = pler.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local ownerValue = head and head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
        if ownerValue and ownerValue.Value == localPlayer.Name then
            return ownerValue
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function ROS(part, ignoreDistance)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end

    local character = GetCharacter()
    if not character then return end

    local root = character.HumanoidRootPart
    local distance = (root.Position - part.Position).Magnitude

    if ignoreDistance or distance <= 30 then
        pcall(function()
            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(
                part,
                lookAt(root.Position, part.Position)
            )
        end)
    end
end

local function getCharInfo()
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return char, hrp, hum
end

local function CheckAndBuyToy(toyName)
    local ToyContents = PlayerGui:WaitForChild("MenuGui"):WaitForChild("Menu"):WaitForChild("TabContents"):WaitForChild("Toys"):WaitForChild("Contents")
    
    if not ToyContents:FindFirstChild(toyName) then
        local success = BuyToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(toyName)
        
        if not success then
            if not Config.notifiedNoCoins then
                FrostNotify("You did not have enough coins to purchase ".. toyName ..".")
                Config.notifiedNoCoins = true
            end
            return false
        end
    end
    return true
end
local function TeleportToPlayerBehind(playerRootCFrame)
    if typeof(playerRootCFrame) ~= "CFrame" then return end

    local char = GetPlayerCharacter()
    if not char or _G.TPNET then return end
    
    local root = char.HumanoidRootPart
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not humanoid then return end
    
    local playerCFrame = CFrame.new(playerRootCFrame.Position - playerRootCFrame.lookVector * Config.FEDOSE)
    
    root.CFrame = root.CFrame.Rotation + playerCFrame.Position
    
    if humanoid.SeatPart == nil or tostring(humanoid.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
        humanoid.Sit = false
    end
end
local function IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(player)
    return player:FindFirstChild("InPlot") and player.InPlot.Value
end
local function GrabParts(model)
    if model.Name ~= "GrabParts" then return end
    
    pcall(function()
        local grabPart = model:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 2)
        if not grabPart then return end
        
        local weld = grabPart:FindFirstChild("WeldConstraint")
        local playerPart = weld and weld.Part1
        if not playerPart then return end

        local playerCharacter = playerPart.Parent
        local playerHumanoid = playerCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if Config.DeathGrabToggle and playerHumanoid then
            task.spawn(function()
                while playerCharacter.Parent and Config.DeathGrabToggle do
                    local Player = service.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(playerCharacter)

                    if CNWOSHIPOPl(Player) then
                        playerHumanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                        playerHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                        playerHumanoid.Jump = true
                        playerHumanoid.Sit = false

                        if playerHumanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) then
                            DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(playerPart)
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        end

        if Config.skyGrabToggle or Config.supersonicGrabToggle then
            local speed = Config.supersonicGrabToggle and 1000 or 100
            local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, speed, 0)
            bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
            bv.Parent = grabPart
        end

		if Config.flingGrabToggle and playerPart then
            local localPlayer = service.Players.LocalPlayer
            local myCharacter = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
            local myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if myCharacter and myRoot then
                local BV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                BV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                BV.P = 1250

                local playerTarget = service.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(playerCharacter)
                if playerTarget then
                    BV.Velocity = myRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 2000 + Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)
                else
                    BV.Velocity = myRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 4000 + Vector3.new(0, 2000, 0)
                end
                
                BV.Parent = playerPart

                task.spawn(function()
                    model.Destroying:Wait()
                    if BV and BV.Parent then
                        BV:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        if Config.spinGrabToggle and playerPart then
            local bav = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
            bav.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)
            bav.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
            bav.Parent = playerPart
        end

        if Config.chaosGrabToggle then
            local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition", grabPart)
            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro", grabPart)
            bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)

            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.chaosGrabToggle and grabPart.Parent do
                    bp.Position = grabPart.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-50,50), math.random(-50,50), math.random(-50,50))
                    bg.CFrame = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.random(-180,180)), math.rad(math.random(-180,180)), math.rad(math.random(-180,180)))
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                if bp then bp:Destroy() end
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
            end)
        end

        if Config.crazyGrabToggle and playerPart then
            local bav = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
            bav.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(5000, 5000, 5000)
            bav.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            bav.Parent = playerPart

            local localPlayer = service.Players.LocalPlayer
            local myCharacter = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
            local myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            
            local startY = myRoot and myRoot.Position.Y or playerPart.Position.Y
            local targetY = startY + 120

            local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
            bp.P = 10000
            bp.Position = Vector3.new(playerPart.Position.X, targetY, playerPart.Position.Z)
            bp.Parent = playerPart

            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.crazyGrabToggle and playerPart.Parent do
                    local randomOffset = Vector3.new(
                        math.random(-500, 500),
                        math.random(-5, 5),
                        math.random(-500, 500)
                    )
                    
                    bp.Position = Vector3.new(playerPart.Position.X, targetY, playerPart.Position.Z) + randomOffset
                    
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
                if bp then bp:Destroy() end
                if bav then bav:Destroy() end
            end)
        end

        if Config.noclipGrabToggle and not playerPart.Anchored then
            task.spawn(function()
                if playerCharacter:IsA("Model") then
                    local allParts = playerCharacter:GetDescendants()
                    local originalCollisionState = {}

                    for _, obj in pairs(allParts) do
                        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                            originalCollisionState[obj] = obj.CanCollide
                        end
                    end

                    while Config.noclipGrabToggle and grabPart.Parent do
                        for _, obj in pairs(allParts) do
                            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                                obj.CanCollide = false
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait(0.2)
                    end

                    for _, obj in pairs(allParts) do
                        if obj and obj:IsA("BasePart") and originalCollisionState[obj] ~= nil then
                            obj.CanCollide = originalCollisionState[obj]
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        if Config.freezeGrabToggle then
            local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition", grabPart)
            bp.Position = grabPart.Position
            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        end

        if Config.masslessGrabToggle then
            local dp = model:FindFirstChild("DragPart")
            if dp then
                local ap = dp:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition")
                local ao = dp:FindFirstChild("AlignOrientation")
                if ap then
                    ap.Responsiveness = 200
                    ap.MaxForce = math.huge
                    ap.MaxVelocity = math.huge
                end
                if ao then
                    ao.Responsiveness = 200
                    ao.MaxTorque = math.huge
                end
            end
        end

    end)
end

local function whitelistBlob(Player)
    if isDeveloper(Player.UserId) then return true end
    
    if table.find(Config.Manualwhite, Player.UserId) then return true end

    if Config.WhitelistFriendsBLOB then
        return localPlayer:IsFriendsWith(Player.UserId)
    end
    return false
end

local function CanAuraTarget(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == localPlayer then return false end
    if not targetPlayer.Character then return false end
    if isDeveloper(targetPlayer.UserId) then return false end
    if Config.WhitelistFriends and localPlayer:IsFriendsWith(targetPlayer.UserId) then
        return false
    end
    if table.find(kickAuraWhitelist or {}, targetPlayer.Name) then
        return false
    end
    if table.find(Config.Manualwhite, targetPlayer.Name) then
        return false
    end
    local hum = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return hum and hum.Health > 0
end

local function getBlobman()
    local inv = SpawnedInToys
    local v = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
    if not v then
        for _, p in ipairs(PlotItems:GetChildren()) do
            local m = p:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
            if m and m:FindFirstChild("PlayerValue") and m.PlayerValue.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                v = m
                break
            end
        end
    end
    return v
end

local function blobGrab(blob, target, side)
    if not blob then return end
    local detector = blob:FindFirstChild(side .. "Detector")
    if not detector then return end
    local weld = detector:FindFirstChild(side .. "Weld")
    if not weld then return end
    local script = blob:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript", true)
    if not script then return end
    local remote = script:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
    if remote then
        pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer(detector, target, weld)
        end)
    end
end

local function spawnBlobmanAtPlayer()
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end

    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end

    local pos = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

    return SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
        "CreatureBlobman",
        pos,
        Vector3.new(0, 59.667, 0)
    )
end

local function spawnBLob2()
    local char = GetCharacter()
    local tFolder = service.Workspace:WaitForChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
    local existing = tFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
    if existing then 
        return existing 
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() 
            SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", char.Head.CFrame, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)) 
        end)
    end)
    local blobman
    for _ = 1, 50 do
        blobman = tFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
        if blobman then 
            break 
        end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    return blobman
end

local function sitOnBlobman(blobman)
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end

    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not root or not humanoid then return end

    if not blobman then
        spawnBlobmanAtPlayer()
        blobman = getBlobman() 
    end

    if blobman then
        local seat = blobman:FindFirstChildOfClass("VehicleSeat") or blobman:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
        
        if seat and humanoid.SeatPart ~= seat then
            seat:Sit(humanoid)
        end
    end
end

service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if Config.AutoBlobSit then
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end

        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return end

        currentBlobman = getBlobman()

        if not currentBlobman then
            spawnBlobmanAtPlayer()
            currentBlobman = getBlobman()
        end

        if currentBlobman then
            local seat = currentBlobman:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
            if seat and humanoid.SeatPart ~= seat then
                seat:Sit(humanoid)
                Config.blobSeat = seat
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function GetPlot()
    local plots = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end

    local folder = nil
    if Config.SelectedPlot == "Green-House" then
        folder = "Plot1"
    elseif Config.SelectedPlot == "Red-House" then
        folder = "Plot2"
    elseif Config.SelectedPlot == "Purple-House" then
        folder = "Plot3"
    elseif Config.SelectedPlot == "Blue-House" then
        folder = "Plot4"
    elseif Config.SelectedPlot == "Chinese-House" then
        folder = "Plot5"
    end

    if folder then
        return plots:FindFirstChild(folder)
    end
end
local function GetPlayerListForDropdown()
    local list = {}
    for _, v in pairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if v ~= localPlayer then
            table.insert(list, string.format("%s (ID: %s)", v.DisplayName, v.Name))
        end
    end
    return list
end
local function CSV(part)
    if not part:FindFirstChild("SkyVelocity") then
        local velocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", part)
        velocity.Name = "SkyVelocity"
        velocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 1e14, 0)
        velocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
    end
end

local function TPPlayer(targetCFrame)
    local char = GetCharacter()
    if char and typeof(targetCFrame) == "CFrame" then
        local root = char.HumanoidRootPart
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        root.CFrame = root.CFrame.Rotation + targetCFrame.Position
        if hum.SeatPart == nil or tostring(hum.SeatPart.Parent) ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
            hum.Sit = false
        end
    end
end

local function NoclipON()
    if not Config.noclipConnection then
        Config.Noclipoff = false
        Config.noclipConnection = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not Config.Noclipoff and localPlayer.Character then
                for _, part in pairs(localPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide and part.Name ~= (floatName or "") then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function NoclipOFF()
    if not _G.NoclipToggle then
        if Config.noclipConnection then
            Config.noclipConnection:Disconnect()
            Config.noclipConnection = nil
        end
        Config.Noclipoff = true
    end
end

local function IsValidTarget(target)
    if typeof(target) == "Instance" and target ~= localPlayer and target.Character then
        local char = target.Character
        if char:IsDescendantOf(service.Workspace) and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
local function CanAttack(target)
    if IsValidTarget(target) then
        if isDeveloper(target.UserId) or (Config.WhitelistFriends2 and localPlayer:IsFriendsWith(target.UserId)) then
            return false
        end
        if not (InPlot and InPlot.Value) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

    local productInfo = service.MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
    if productInfo and productInfo.Name then
        Config.GameName = productInfo.Name
    end
end)

iroiro:AddToggle({
    Name = "Walk Speed",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.walkSpeedToggle = Value
        task.spawn(function()
            while Config.walkSpeedToggle do
                local char = localPlayer.Character
                if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local hrp = char.HumanoidRootPart
                    local moveDir = char.Humanoid.MoveDirection
                    hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + moveDir * (Config.walkSpeed / 10)
                end
                task.wait()
            end

            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                char.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
            end
        end)
    end
})

    Name = "Infinite Jump",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.infiniteJumpToggle = Value

        if Config.infiniteJumpToggle then
            if Config.jumpConnection then Config.jumpConnection:Disconnect() end
            Config.jumpConnection = service.UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
                local char = localPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end
            end)
        else
            if Config.jumpConnection then
                Config.jumpConnection:Disconnect()
                Config.jumpConnection = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

ag:AddToggle({
    Name = "Ragdoll",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        permRagdollT = Value

        if permRagdollT and not permRagdollRunningS then
            coroutine.wrap(function()
                permRagdollRunningS = true
                
                while permRagdollT do
                    local char = localPlayer.Character
                    if char then
                        local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
                        
                        if hrp then
                            RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, 1)
                            if hum then
                                hum.PlatformStand = true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0)
                end
                
                permRagdollRunningS = false
                
                local char = localPlayer.Character
                if char then
                    local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
                    
                    if hrp then
                        RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, 0)
                        if hum then
                            hum.PlatformStand = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)()
        elseif not permRagdollT then
            permRagdollRunningS = false
        end
    end
})

		else

				Config.spincconnn:Disconnect()
				Config.spincconnn = nil
			end
		end
	end

deletepartss:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Delete Part",
    Default = "Right Leg",
    Options = {"Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg"},
    Callback = function(selected)
        Config.selectedPart = selected
    end

deletepartss:AddButton({
    Name = "Delete",
    Callback = function()
        if Config.feetTPDeleteEnabled then return end
        Config.feetTPDeleteEnabled = true
        if Config.legsEnabled or Config.obeyPartEnabled or Config.obeyAllEnabled or Config.feetTPDeleteEnabled then
            Config.ragdollRunning = true
        else
            Config.ragdollRunning = false
        end
        if Config.ragdollRunning then
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.ragdollRunning do
                    pcall(function()
                        local char = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                        local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso"))
                        if hrp then 
                            RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, 1) 
                        end
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end)
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local char = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                if not char then return end
                local function getRepPart(character, partKey)
                    if not character then return nil end
                    local names = {}
                    if partKey == "Left Arm" then 
                        names = {"LeftHand", "LeftUpperArm", "Left Arm", "LeftLowerArm"}
                    elseif partKey == "Right Arm" then 
                        names = {"RightHand", "RightUpperArm", "Right Arm", "RightLowerArm"}
                    elseif partKey == "Left Leg" then 
                        names = {"LeftFoot", "LeftLowerLeg", "Left Leg", "LeftUpperLeg"}
                    elseif partKey == "Right Leg" then 
                        names = {"RightFoot", "RightLowerLeg", "Right Leg", "RightUpperLeg"}
                    end
                    for _, n in ipairs(names) do
                        local p = character:FindFirstChild(n)
                        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then 
                            return p 
                        end
                    end
                    return nil
                end
                local targetPart = getRepPart(char, Config.selectedPart)
                local savedPos = {}
                local otherParts = {"Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg"}
                for _, p in pairs(otherParts) do
                    if p ~= Config.selectedPart then
                        local part = getRepPart(char, p)
                        if part then 
                            savedPos[p] = part.CFrame 
                        end
                    end
                end
                if targetPart then 
                    for _, child in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                        if child:IsA("Motor6D") or child:IsA("JointInstance") then
                            if child.Part0 == targetPart or child.Part1 == targetPart then
                                child:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    targetPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 999999999, 0) 
                end

                task.wait(0.1)
                if targetPart then 
                    targetPart:Destroy() 
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
                for k, v in pairs(savedPos) do
                    local part = getRepPart(char, k)
                    if part then 
                        part.CFrame = v 
                    end
                end
                Config.feetTPDeleteEnabled = false
                
                if Config.legsEnabled or Config.obeyPartEnabled or Config.obeyAllEnabled or Config.feetTPDeleteEnabled then
                    Config.ragdollRunning = true
                else
                    Config.ragdollRunning = false
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
})

local function getplot()
    for i = 1, 5 do
        local plot = workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild("Plot"..i)
        local value = plot.PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:FindFirstChild("Value")
        if plot and value and value.Value:find(localPlayer.Name) then
            return plot
        end
    end
end

local function spawntoy(toy, cf)
    if not localPlayer.CanSpawnToy.Value then
        localPlayer.CanSpawnToy.Changed:Wait()
    end
    local t
    local toyadded
    toyadded = SpawnedInToys.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
        if c.Name == toy then
            t = c
            toyadded:Disconnect()
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
            toy,
            cf,
            Vector3.new(0,0,0)
        )
    end)
    local time = tick() + 1
    repeat task.wait() until t or tick() > time
    if t then
        return t
    else
        local plot = getplot()
        if plot then
            return workspace.PlotItems[plot.Name]:FindFirstChild(toy) or workspace.PlotItems[plot.Name]:WaitForChild(toy, 0.5)
        end
    end
end

grab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Ragdoll Grab",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            local pal, pal2
            pal2 = localPlayer.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy.Contents.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
                if c.Name == "PalletLightBrown" then
                    pal = c
                    task.wait()
                    pal2:Disconnect()
                    pal2 = nil
                end
            end)
			local ragd = spawntoy("PalletLightBrown", HRP().CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 5, 20))
            local partt = ragd:WaitForChild("SoundPart", 0.1)
            ragd.Name = "ragdoll"
            spawn(function()
                task.wait(1)
                local ragdoll = pal.ViewItemButton.NewMessage:Clone()
                ragdoll.Name = "Ragdoll"
                ragdoll.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                ragdoll.Text = "Ragdoll Grab"
                ragdoll.Visible = true
                ragdoll.Parent = pal.ViewItemButton
            end)
            repeat SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(partt) task.wait() until partt:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
            partt.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
            spawn(function()
                for i,v in pairs(ragd:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v:IsA("Part") then
                        v.Transparency = 1
                        v.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end)
            Config.Contuuti["rgarab1"] = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
                if c.Name == "GrabParts" then
                    local part = c:FindFirstChild("GrabPart") or c:WaitForChild("GrabPart", 3)
                    if part then
                        local obj = part.WeldConstraint.Part1
                        while workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts") and task.wait() do
                            if obj then
                                if obj.Parent and obj.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and obj.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and obj.Parent.Humanoid:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") and obj.Parent.Humanoid.Ragdolled.Value == false then
                                    spawn(function()
                                        partt.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
                                        partt.CFrame = obj.Parent.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                        task.wait(0.05)
                                        partt.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 1e9, 0)
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if Config.Contuuti["rgarab1"] then Config.Contuuti["rgarab1"]:Disconnect() end
        end
    end
})

alllll:AddDropdown({
    Name = "All mode",
    Default = "Kill",
    Options = {"Kill", "Teleport"},
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.BlobAllmode = Value
        if Config.BloballToggle then
            Config.blobmanAllRung = false
            task.wait(0.1)
            
            Config.blobmanAllRung = Config.BloballToggle
            if Config.BloballToggle then
                if Config.BlobAllmode == "Kill" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local hpToggle = false
                        while Config.blobmanAllRung do
                            task.wait(0.01)
                            local char, hrp = getCharInfo()
                            if not hrp then continue end
                            
                            local blobman = spawnBLob2()
                            if not blobman then continue end
                            
                            sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                            local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                            local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                            local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                            if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                            
                            local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                            if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                            
                            hpToggle = not hpToggle
                            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if not Config.blobmanAllRung then break end
                                if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                    
                                    if Config.Igplottaisyo and IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(plr) then continue end
                                    
                                    local c = plr.Character
                                    local thrp, hum = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if thrp and hum and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                            Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            hum.Health = hpToggle and 0 or 100
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)

                    task.spawn(function()
                        while Config.blobmanAllRung do
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            local char, hrp = getCharInfo()
                            if not hrp then continue end
                            
                            local blobman = spawnBLob2()
                            if not blobman then continue end
                            
                            sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if not Config.blobmanAllRung then break end 
                                if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                    
                                    if Config.Igplottaisyo and IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(plr) then continue end
                                    
                                    local c = plr.Character
                                    local thrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if thrp then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            hrp.CFrame = thrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
                                        end)
                                        task.wait(0.2)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)

                elseif Config.BlobAllmode == "Teleport" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while Config.blobmanAllRung do
                            task.wait(0.01)
                            local char, hrp = getCharInfo()
                            if not hrp then continue end
                            
                            local blobman = spawnBLob2()
                            if not blobman then continue end
                            
                            sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                            local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                            local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                            local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                            if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                            
                            local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                            if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                            
                            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if not Config.blobmanAllRung then break end
                                if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                    
                                    if Config.Igplottaisyo and IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(plr) then continue end
                                    
                                    local c = plr.Character
                                    local thrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if thrp and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                            Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            thrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 20, 0)
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)

                    task.spawn(function()
                        while Config.blobmanAllRung do
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            local char, hrp = getCharInfo()
                            if not hrp then continue end
                            
                            local blobman = spawnBLob2()
                            if not blobman then continue end
                            
                            sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if not Config.blobmanAllRung then break end 
                                if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                    
                                    if Config.Igplottaisyo and IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(plr) then continue end
                                    
                                    local c = plr.Character
                                    local thrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if thrp then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            hrp.CFrame = thrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
                                        end)
                                        task.wait(0.2)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

tblo:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Mode",
    Default = "localPlayer",
    Options = {"localPlayer", "server"},
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.TPblobmode = Value
    end    
})

tblo:AddButton({
    Name = "Blobman TP",
    Callback = function()
        local character = localPlayer.Character
        if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then 
            return 
        end
        
        local myRoot = character.HumanoidRootPart
        local targetFound = false

        if Config.TPblobmode == "localPlayer" then
            local folderName = localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys"
            local targetFolder = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)
            
            if targetFolder then
                local blobman = targetFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman")
                if blobman and blobman:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    myRoot.CFrame = blobman.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                    targetFound = true
                elseif blobman and blobman:IsA("BasePart") then
                    myRoot.CFrame = blobman.CFrame
                    targetFound = true
                end
            end
            
        elseif Config.TPblobmode == "server" then
            for _, obj in pairs(service.Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                    if obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        myRoot.CFrame = obj.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        targetFound = true
                        break
                    elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                        myRoot.CFrame = obj.CFrame
                        targetFound = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if targetFound then
        else
            FrostNotify("I can't find Blobman ❌️")
        end
    end
})

rgbblob:AddToggle({
    Name = "BlobmanRGB",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AIRNJIAD = Config.AIRNJIAD or {}
        
        if Config.BLESP == Value then return end
        Config.BLESP = Value

        local function BLBOESP(model)
            if not Config.BLESP then return end
            if model:FindFirstChild("BlobmanESP") then return end

            local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = "BlobmanESP"
            highlight.FillTransparency = 0.3
            highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            highlight.Adornee = model
            highlight.Parent = model

            table.insert(Config.AIRNJIAD, highlight)
        end

        local function isValidBlobman(obj)
            return obj:IsA("Model")
                and obj.Name == "CreatureBlobman"
                and obj.Parent
                and obj.Parent.Name:find("SpawnedInToys")
        end

        local function SOKDOI()
            if Config.RDESDG then return end
            Config.RDESDG = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                local hue = tick() % 5 / 5
                for i = #Config.AIRNJIAD, 1, -1 do
                    local hl = Config.AIRNJIAD[i]
                    if not hl or not hl.Parent then
                        table.remove(Config.AIRNJIAD, i)
                    else
                        hl.FillColor = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local function GASGS()
            if Config.RDESDG then
                Config.RDESDG:Disconnect()
                Config.RDESDG = nil
            end
        end

        local function GWGEVwgwg()
            for i = #Config.AIRNJIAD, 1, -1 do
                local hl = Config.AIRNJIAD[i]
                if hl then
                    hl:Destroy()
                end
                table.remove(Config.AIRNJIAD, i)
            end
        end

        local function scan()
            for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if isValidBlobman(v) then
                    BLBOESP(v)
                end
            end
        end

        if Value then
            if Config.BlobmanConnection then 
                Config.BlobmanConnection:Disconnect() 
            end
            
            Config.BlobmanConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if isValidBlobman(obj) then
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    BLBOESP(obj)
                end
            end)

            scan()
            SOKDOI()
        else
            if Config.BlobmanConnection then
                Config.BlobmanConnection:Disconnect()
                Config.BlobmanConnection = nil
            end
            GASGS()
            GWGEVwgwg()
        end
    end
})

aurabklo:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Select Aura",
    Default = "KillAura",
    Options = {"KillAura", "Kick Aura", "Gucci Destroy", "Speed/Jump Controls"},
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AuraMods = Value
        if Config.blobauradeesu then
            blobmanAuraRunning = false
            task.wait(0.1)
            
            blobmanAuraRunning = Config.blobauradeesu
            if Config.blobauradeesu then
                if Config.AuraMods == "KillAura" then
                    task.spawn(function() 
                        local hpToggle = false
                        while blobmanAuraRunning do
                            task.wait(0.01)
                            local char = localPlayer.Character
                            local hrp = GetRoot(char)
                            if not hrp then continue end
                            local blobman = getBlobman()
                            if not blobman then continue end
                            sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                            
                            local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                            local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                            local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                            if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                            local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                            if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                            hpToggle = not hpToggle
                            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if not blobmanAuraRunning then break end
                                if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                    local c = plr.Character
                                    local thrp, hum = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if thrp and hum and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                            Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            hum.Health = hpToggle and 0 or 100 
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                elseif Config.AuraMods == "Kick Aura" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        Config.AuraKickPlay = {}
                        local auraSide = "Left"
                        while blobmanAuraRunning do
                            task.wait(0.01)
                            local char = localPlayer.Character
                            local myRoot = GetRoot(char)
                            if not myRoot then continue end
                            local blob = getBlobman()
                            if not blob then continue end
                            sitOnBlobman(blob)
                            
                            local currentTime = tick()
                            for playerId, kickTime in pairs(Config.AuraKickPlay) do
                                if currentTime - kickTime >= 1.5 then
                                    Config.AuraKickPlay[playerId] = nil
                                end
                            end
                            
                            local nearbyPlayers = {}
                            for _, plr in service.Players:GetPlayers() do
                                if plr == localPlayer then continue end
                                if Config.AuraKickPlay[plr.UserId] then continue end
                                
                                if WhitelistFriendsBLOB then
                                    local isFriend = false
                                    pcall(function()
                                        isFriend = plr:IsFriendsWith(localPlayer.UserId)
                                    end)
                                    if isFriend then continue end
                                end
                                
                                local c = plr.Character
                                if not c then continue end
                                local root = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if not root then continue end
                                local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
                                
                                local playerY = root.Position.Y
                                if playerY < -25.072832107543945 then continue end
                                
                                local shouldIgnore = false
                                for _, ignoreHeight in ipairs(Config.HeightAurakick) do
                                    if math.abs(playerY - ignoreHeight) < 0.1 then
                                        shouldIgnore = true
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                                if shouldIgnore then continue end
                                
                                local dist = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < 50 then
                                    table.insert(nearbyPlayers, {plr = plr, root = root, dist = dist})
                                end
                            end
                            
                            table.sort(nearbyPlayers, function(a, b)
                                return a.dist < b.dist
                            end)
                            
                            if #nearbyPlayers > 0 then
                                local target = nearbyPlayers[1]
                                local targetHum = target.root.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                
                                myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                for _, part in blob:GetDescendants() do
                                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                        part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    end
                                end
                                
                                blobGrab(blob, myRoot, auraSide)
                                task.wait(0.11)
                                
                                if SetNetworkOwner then
                                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(target.root, GetRoot().CFrame)
                                end
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                
                                local oldPos = target.root.CFrame
                                target.root.CFrame = oldPos + Vector3.new(0, Config.Amountdesukick, 0)
                                task.wait(0.08)
                                
                                DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(target.root)
                                blobGrab(blob, target.root, auraSide)
                                
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                local healthAfter = targetHum and targetHum.Health or 0
                                if healthAfter <= 0 then
                                    Config.AuraKickPlay[target.plr.UserId] = nil
                                end
                                
                                auraSide = (auraSide == "Left") and "Right" or "Left"
                                task.wait(Config.Kickaurabyou)
                            end
                        end
                        Config.AuraKickPlay = {}
                    end)
                elseif Config.AuraMods == "Gucci Destroy" then
                    task.spawn(function() 
                        local hpToggle = false
                        while blobmanAuraRunning do
                            task.wait(0.01)
                            local char = localPlayer.Character
                            local hrp = GetRoot(char)
                            if not hrp then continue end
                            local blobman = getBlobman()
                            if not blobman then continue end
                            sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                            
                            local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                            local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                            local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                            if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                            local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                            if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                            hpToggle = not hpToggle
                            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if not blobmanAuraRunning then break end
                                if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                    local c = plr.Character
                                    local thrp, hum = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if thrp and hum and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                            Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                elseif Config.AuraMods == "Speed/Jump Controls" then
                    task.spawn(function() 
                        while blobmanAuraRunning do
                            task.wait(0.01)
                            local char = localPlayer.Character
                            local hrp = GetRoot(char)
                            if not hrp then continue end
                            local blobman = getBlobman()
                            if not blobman then continue end
                            sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                            
                            local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                            local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                            local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                            if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                            local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                            if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                            
                            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if not blobmanAuraRunning then break end
                                if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                    local c = plr.Character
                                    local thrp, hum = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if thrp and hum and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                            Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                            hum.WalkSpeed = targetWalkSpeed
                                            hum.JumpPower = targetJumpPower
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

aurabklo:AddToggle({
    Name = "Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.blobauradeesu = Value
        
        if not Value then
            blobmanAuraRunning = false
        end

        blobmanAuraRunning = Config.blobauradeesu
        if Config.blobauradeesu then
            if Config.AuraMods == "KillAura" then
                task.spawn(function() 
                    local hpToggle = false
                    while blobmanAuraRunning do
                        task.wait(0.01)
                        local char = localPlayer.Character
                        local hrp = GetRoot(char)
                        if not hrp then continue end
                        local blobman = getBlobman()
                        if not blobman then continue end
                        sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                        
                        local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                        local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                        local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                        if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                        local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                        if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                        hpToggle = not hpToggle
                        for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if not blobmanAuraRunning then break end
                            if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                local c = plr.Character
                                local thrp, hum = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if thrp and hum and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                        Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                        Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                        hum.Health = hpToggle and 0 or 100 
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            elseif Config.AuraMods == "Kick Aura" then
                task.spawn(function()
                    Config.AuraKickPlay = {}
                    local auraSide = "Left"
                    while blobmanAuraRunning do
                        task.wait(0.01)
                        local char = localPlayer.Character
                        local myRoot = GetRoot(char)
                        if not myRoot then continue end
                        local blob = getBlobman()
                        if not blob then continue end
                        sitOnBlobman(blob)
                        
                        local currentTime = tick()
                        for playerId, kickTime in pairs(Config.AuraKickPlay) do
                            if currentTime - kickTime >= 1.5 then
                                Config.AuraKickPlay[playerId] = nil
                            end
                        end
                        
                        local nearbyPlayers = {}
                        for _, plr in service.Players:GetPlayers() do
                            if plr == localPlayer then continue end
                            if Config.AuraKickPlay[plr.UserId] then continue end
                            
                            if WhitelistFriendsBLOB then
                                local isFriend = false
                                pcall(function()
                                    isFriend = plr:IsFriendsWith(localPlayer.UserId)
                                end)
                                if isFriend then continue end
                            end
                            
                            local c = plr.Character
                            if not c then continue end
                            local root = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if not root then continue end
                            local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
                            
                            local playerY = root.Position.Y
                            if playerY < -25.072832107543945 then continue end
                            
                            local shouldIgnore = false
                            for _, ignoreHeight in ipairs(Config.HeightAurakick) do
                                if math.abs(playerY - ignoreHeight) < 0.1 then
                                    shouldIgnore = true
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                            if shouldIgnore then continue end
                            
                            local dist = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < 50 then
                                table.insert(nearbyPlayers, {plr = plr, root = root, dist = dist})
                            end
                        end
                        
                        table.sort(nearbyPlayers, function(a, b)
                            return a.dist < b.dist
                        end)
                        
                        if #nearbyPlayers > 0 then
                            local target = nearbyPlayers[1]
                            local targetHum = target.root.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            
                            myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            for _, part in blob:GetDescendants() do
                                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                end
                            end
                            
                            blobGrab(blob, myRoot, auraSide)
                            task.wait(0.11)
                            
                            if SetNetworkOwner then
                                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(target.root, GetRoot().CFrame)
                            end
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            
                            local oldPos = target.root.CFrame
                            target.root.CFrame = oldPos + Vector3.new(0, Config.Amountdesukick, 0)
                            task.wait(0.08)
                            
                            DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(target.root)
                            blobGrab(blob, target.root, auraSide)
                            
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            local healthAfter = targetHum and targetHum.Health or 0
                            if healthAfter <= 0 then
                                Config.AuraKickPlay[target.plr.UserId] = nil
                            end
                            
                            auraSide = (auraSide == "Left") and "Right" or "Left"
                            task.wait(Config.Kickaurabyou)
                        end
                    end
                    Config.AuraKickPlay = {}
                end)
            elseif Config.AuraMods == "Gucci Destroy" then
                task.spawn(function() 
                    local hpToggle = false
                    while blobmanAuraRunning do
                        task.wait(0.01)
                        local char = localPlayer.Character
                        local hrp = GetRoot(char)
                        if not hrp then continue end
                        local blobman = getBlobman()
                        if not blobman then continue end
                        sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                        
                        local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                        local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                        local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                        if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                        local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                        if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                        hpToggle = not hpToggle
                        for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if not blobmanAuraRunning then break end
                            if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                local c = plr.Character
                                local thrp, hum = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if thrp and hum and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                        Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                        Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            elseif Config.AuraMods == "Speed/Jump Controls" then
                task.spawn(function() 
                    while blobmanAuraRunning do
                        task.wait(0.01)
                        local char = localPlayer.Character
                        local hrp = GetRoot(char)
                        if not hrp then continue end
                        local blobman = getBlobman()
                        if not blobman then continue end
                        sitOnBlobman(blobman)
                        
                        local scriptFolder = blobman:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                        local detector = blobman:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector")
                        local weld = detector and detector:FindFirstChild("LeftWeld")
                        if not (scriptFolder and detector and weld) then continue end
                        local Grab, Release, Drop = scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureRelease"), scriptFolder:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                        if not (Grab and Release and Drop) then continue end
                        
                        for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if not blobmanAuraRunning then break end
                            if plr ~= localPlayer and not whitelistBlob(plr) then
                                local c = plr.Character
                                local thrp, hum = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                if thrp and hum and (hrp.Position - thrp.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        Grab:FireServer(detector, thrp, weld)
                                        Release:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                        Drop:FireServer(weld, thrp)
                                        hum.WalkSpeed = targetWalkSpeed
                                        hum.JumpPower = targetJumpPower
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
})
