sett:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti Fling",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(aiueo)
        if aiueo then
            if not Config.blnoclipCor then
                Config.blnoclip = true
                Config.blnoclipCor = coroutine.create(function()
                    while Config.blnoclip do

                        if toys then
                            local creatureBlobmanCount = 0

                            for _, item in ipairs(SpawnedInToys:GetChildren()) do
                                if item.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                                    if creatureBlobmanCount < 4 then
                                        creatureBlobmanCount = creatureBlobmanCount + 1

                                        for _, descendant in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
                                            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                                                descendant.CanQuery = false
                                                descendant.CanTouch = false
                                                descendant.CanCollide = false
                                            end
                                        end
                                    else
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        wait()
                    end
                end)
                coroutine.resume(Config.blnoclipCor)
            end
        else
            Config.blnoclip = false
            if Config.blnoclipCor then
                coroutine.resume(Config.blnoclipCor)
                Config.blnoclipCor = nil
            end

            if toys then
                local creatureBlobmanCount = 0

                for _, item in ipairs(SpawnedInToys:GetChildren()) do
                    if item.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                        if creatureBlobmanCount < 4 then
                            creatureBlobmanCount = creatureBlobmanCount + 1

                            for _, descendant in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
                                if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    descendant.CanQuery = true
                                    descendant.CanTouch = true
                                    descendant.CanCollide = true
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

sett:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Sit",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AutoBlobSit = Value
        if not Value and Config.blobSeat then
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char then
                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.Sit = false
                end
            end
            Config.blobSeat = nil
        end
    end
})

sett:AddToggle({
    Name = "walk Speed",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.BlobSpeedToggle = Value
        
        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.BlobSpeedToggle do
                    local char = localPlayer.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    
                    if hum then
                        local seat = hum.SeatPart
                        local isRiding = seat and seat.Parent and seat.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman"
                        
                        if isRiding then
                            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if hrp then
                                local moveDir = hum.MoveDirection
                                hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + moveDir * (Config.BlobSpeedValue / 10)
                            end
                        else
                            hum.WalkSpeed = 16
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
                
                local char = localPlayer.Character
                if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                    char.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})

sett:AddSlider({
    Name = "Speed",
    Min = 1,
    Max = 100,
    Default = 16,
    Increment = 1,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.BlobSpeedValue = Value

        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            local seat = hum.SeatPart
            local isRiding = seat and seat.Parent and seat.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman"
            
            if isRiding then
                hum.WalkSpeed = Value
            else
                hum.WalkSpeed = 16
            end
        end
    end
})

sett:AddSlider({
    Name = "HipHeight",
    Min = 1,
    Max = 500,
    Increment = 10,
    Default = 0,
    Callback = function(value)
        local function updateHipHeight(targetValue)
            if not SpawnedInToys then return end

            local creatureBlobmanCount = 0
            for _, item in ipairs(SpawnedInToys:GetChildren()) do
                if item.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                    if creatureBlobmanCount < 4 then
                        creatureBlobmanCount = creatureBlobmanCount + 1
                        for _, descendant in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
                            if descendant:IsA("Humanoid") then
                                if Config.BlobHipHeight == nil then
                                    Config.BlobHipHeight = descendant.HipHeight
                                end
                                descendant.HipHeight = targetValue
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        updateHipHeight(value)
    end
})

anitgrab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Grab",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(value)
        Config.AntiGrabToggle = value
        
        if value then
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.AntiGrabToggle do
                    pcall(function()
                        Struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
                    end)
                    
                    Config.ragdollCount = 0
                    
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)

            local function AntiGrabT(character)
                local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
                local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 10)
                
                if not (hrp and hum and head) then return end

                head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                    if child.Name ~= "PartOwner" or not Config.AntiGrabToggle then return end
                    
                    hum.Sit = false
                    
                    if Config.ragdollCount < 100 then
                        RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, 0)
                        Config.ragdollCount = Config.ragdollCount + 1
                    end

                    if not Config.antiGrabProce then
                        Config.antiGrabProce = true
                        hrp.Anchored = true

                        local isHeld = localPlayer:WaitForChild("IsHeld", 5)
                        while Config.AntiGrabToggle and (not isHeld or not isHeld.Value) do 
                            task.wait() 
                        end

                        local renderConnection = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                            hrp.CFrame += hum.MoveDirection * 0.3
                        end)

                        while Config.AntiGrabToggle and isHeld and isHeld.Value do 
                            task.wait() 
                        end
                        
                        renderConnection:Disconnect()
                        hrp.Anchored = false
                        Config.antiGrabProce = false
                    end
                end)
            end

            if localPlayer.Character then task.spawn(AntiGrabT, localPlayer.Character) end
            localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(AntiGrabT)
        end
    end,
})

anitgrab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto-Attacker",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        Config.autoatakka = enabled
        if enabled then
            Config.AutoAttackerToggle = coroutine.create(function()
                while Config.autoatakka do
                    task.wait(0.02)

                    local character = localPlayer.Character
                    local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local localHRP = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not (character and head and localHRP) then continue end

                    local partOwner = head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
                    if not partOwner then continue end

                    local attacker = service.Players:FindFirstChild(partOwner.Value)
                    if not (attacker and attacker.Character) then continue end

                    local targetChar = attacker.Character
                    local hrp = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local torso = targetChar:FindFirstChild("Torso")

                    if not hrp or not hum then continue end

                    Struggle:FireServer()

                    pcall(function()
                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(hrp, hrp.CFrame)
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.05)

                    if Config.SelectAutoatakka == "Death" then
                        pcall(function()
                            for _, bp in ipairs(targetChar:GetChildren()) do
                                if bp:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    bp.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -1e9, 0)
                                    bp.CanCollide = false
                                end
                            end
                            hum.Health = 0
                            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                            CSV(hrp)
                            if ReplicatedStorage.GrabEvents:FindFirstChild("DestroyGrabLine") then
                                DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(hrp)
                            end
                        end)

                    elseif Config.SelectAutoatakka == "knock-back" then
                        local knockback = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                        knockback.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        knockback.Velocity = (localHRP.CFrame.LookVector * 200) + Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
                        knockback.Parent = hrp
                        service.Debris:AddItem(knockback, 0.5)

                    elseif Config.SelectAutoatakka == "air-Suspend" and torso then
                        local velocity = torso:FindFirstChild("l") or Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                        velocity.Name = "l"
                        velocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 5000, 0)
                        velocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                        velocity.Parent = torso
                        service.Debris:AddItem(velocity, 1)

                    elseif Config.SelectAutoatakka == "Freeze" then
                        local freezeVel = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                        freezeVel.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        freezeVel.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                        freezeVel.Parent = hrp
                        service.Debris:AddItem(freezeVel, 2)

                    elseif Config.SelectAutoatakka == "Fling" then
                        local flingVel = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                        flingVel.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        flingVel.P = 1250
                        flingVel.Velocity = localHRP.CFrame.LookVector * 2000 + Vector3.new(0, 1000, 0)
                        flingVel.Parent = hrp
                        service.Debris:AddItem(flingVel, 3)
                    end
                end
            end)
            coroutine.resume(Config.AutoAttackerToggle)
        else
            if Config.AutoAttackerToggle then
                coroutine.close(Config.AutoAttackerToggle)
                Config.AutoAttackerToggle = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

anitgrab:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Mode",
    Options = {"Death", "knock-back", "air-Suspend", "Freeze", "Fling"},
    Default = "Death",
    Callback = function(value)
        Config.SelectAutoatakka = value
    end
})

giuu:AddToggle({ 
    Name = "Gucci", 
    Default = false, 
    Callback = function(v) 
        Config.GucciToggle = v
        Config.ToyName = "CreatureBlobman"
        if v then 
            local _, hrp, _ = getCharInfo()
            if hrp then 
                Config.defPosgucci = hrp.CFrame 
            end
            if Config.GucciConn then 
                Config.GucciConn:Disconnect() 
            end
            Config.GucciConn = SpawnedInToys.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                task.spawn(function()
                    if child.Name ~= Config.ToyName then 
                        return 
                    end
                    Config.gucciInst = child
                    local _, hrpInfo, hum = getCharInfo()
                    if not (hrpInfo and hum) then 
                        return 
                    end
                    local seat = child:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 2) or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true)
                    if seat and hum then
                        pcall(function()
                            seat:Sit(hum)
                        end)
                        local start = tick()
                        while tick() - start < Config.guccidura and Config.GucciToggle do
                            pcall(function()
                                RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrpInfo, 0)
                                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                            end)
                            service.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                        local primary = child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) or child:FindFirstChild("Part", true)
                        if primary and Config.GucciToggle then
                            pcall(function()
                                if primary.SetNetworkOwner then 
                                    primary:SetNetworkOwner(localPlayer) 
                                end
                                sethiddenproperty(primary, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                            end)
                        end
                        if Config.defPosgucci then 
                            hrpInfo.CFrame = Config.defPosgucci 
                        end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                        if seat then 
                            seat:Destroy() 
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end)
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.GucciToggle do
                    if not Config.gucciInst or not Config.gucciInst.Parent then
                        pcall(function() 
                            SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(Config.ToyName, CFrame.new(Config.guccispawn), Vector3.new(0, -15.716, 0)) 
                        end)
                    end
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end)
        else 
            if Config.GucciConn then 
                Config.GucciConn:Disconnect() 
            end
            local _, hrp, hum = getCharInfo()
            if hrp and hum then
                pcall(function()
                    RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, 0)
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end)
            end
            Config.gucciInst = nil
            Config.defPosgucci = nil 
        end 
    end 
})

giuu:AddToggle({ 
    Name = "Gucci2", 
    Default = false, 
    Callback = function(v) 
        Config.GucciToggle = v
        Config.ToyName = "TractorRed"
        if v then 
            local _, hrp, _ = getCharInfo()
            if hrp then 
                Config.defPosgucci = hrp.CFrame 
            end
            if Config.GucciConn then 
                Config.GucciConn:Disconnect()

            Config.GucciConn = SpawnedInToys.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                task.spawn(function()
                    if child.Name ~= Config.ToyName then 
                        return 
                    end
                    Config.gucciInst = child
                    local _, hrpInfo, hum = getCharInfo()
                    if not (hrpInfo and hum) then 
                        return 
                    end
                    local seat = child:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 2) or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true)
                    if seat and hum then
                        pcall(function()
                            seat:Sit(hum)
                        end)
                        local start = tick()
                        while tick() - start < Config.guccidura and Config.GucciToggle do
                            pcall(function()
                                RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrpInfo, 0)
                                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                            end)
                            service.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                        local primary = child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) or child:FindFirstChild("Part", true)
                        if primary and Config.GucciToggle then
                            pcall(function()
                                if primary.SetNetworkOwner then 
                                    primary:SetNetworkOwner(localPlayer) 
                                end
                                sethiddenproperty(primary, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
                            end)
                        end
                        if Config.defPosgucci then 
                            hrpInfo.CFrame = Config.defPosgucci 
                        end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                        if seat then 
                            seat:Destroy() 
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end)
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.GucciToggle do
                    if not Config.gucciInst or not Config.gucciInst.Parent then
                        pcall(function() 
                            SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(Config.ToyName, CFrame.new(Config.guccispawn), Vector3.new(0, -15.716, 0)) 
                        end)
                    end
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end)
        else 
            if Config.GucciConn then 
                Config.GucciConn:Disconnect() 
            end
            local _, hrp, hum = getCharInfo()
            if hrp and hum then
                pcall(function()
                    RagdollRemote:FireServer(hrp, 0)
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end)
            end
            Config.gucciInst = nil
            Config.defPosgucci = nil 
        end 
    end 
})

deeefef:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Burn",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        local mapHole = workspace:WaitForChild("Map"):WaitForChild("Hole"):WaitForChild("PoisonBigHole")
        
        local holePosition = mapHole:GetPivot().Position
        
        local extPart = mapHole:FindFirstChild("ExtinguishPart")

        if not extPart then
            extPart = Instance.new("Part")
            extPart.Name = "ExtinguishPart"
            extPart.Size = Vector3.new(4, 1, 4)
            extPart.Anchored = true
            extPart.CanCollide = false
            extPart.Transparency = 1
            extPart.Position = holePosition
            extPart.Parent = mapHole
        end

        if enabled then
            if not Config.AntiBurnndada then
                Config.AntiBurnndada = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local char = localPlayer.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    
                    if hrp then
                        local fireLight = hrp:FindFirstChild("FireLight")
                        local fireEmitter = hrp:FindFirstChild("FireParticleEmitter")

                        if fireLight or fireEmitter then
                            extPart.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
                        else
                            extPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(holePosition)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        else
            if Config.AntiBurnndada then
                Config.AntiBurnndada:Disconnect()
                Config.AntiBurnndada = nil
            end
            if extPart then
                extPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(holePosition)
            end
        end
    end
})

    Name = "Anti Banana & Snowball",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.antibananaSit = Value
        
        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.antibananaSit do
                    local character = localPlayer.Character
                    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

                    if hum and hrp and hum.Health > 0 then
                        hum.Sit = false
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                        
                        local vec = camera.CFrame.LookVector
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                            hrp.Position,
                            hrp.Position + Vector3.new(vec.X, 0, vec.Z)
                        )
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        end
    end

deeefef:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Paint",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local function setTouchQuery(s)
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char then
                for _, v in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("BasePart") then
                        v.CanTouch = s
                        v.CanQuery = s
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if state then
            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name == "PaintPlayerPart" then
                    local clone = obj:Clone()
                    clone.Archivable = true
                    Config.paintPartsBackup[obj:GetDebugId()] = {
                        clone = clone,
                        parent = obj.Parent
                    }
                    obj:Destroy()
                end
            end

            local conn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name == "PaintPlayerPart" then
                    task.defer(function()
                        if obj and obj.Parent then
                            local clone = obj:Clone()
                            clone.Archivable = true
                            Config.paintPartsBackup[obj:GetDebugId()] = {
                                clone = clone,
                                parent = obj.Parent
                            }
                            obj:Destroy()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(Config.paintConnections, conn)

            setTouchQuery(false)
        else
            for _, conn in ipairs(Config.paintConnections) do
                if conn.Connected then conn:Disconnect() end
            end
            Config.paintConnections = {}

            for id, data in pairs(Config.paintPartsBackup) do
                if data.clone and data.parent then
                    data.clone.Parent = data.parent
                end
            end
            Config.paintPartsBackup = {}

            setTouchQuery(true)
        end
    end
})

deeefef:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Network (OwnerShip)",
    Default = false, 
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AntiNetTOgl = Value
        
        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                Config.ToyName = "FoodHamburger"
                while Config.AntiNetTOgl do
                    local char = GetCharacter()
                    local hrp = HRP()
                    
                    if not hrp then 
                        service.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() 
                        continue 
                    end
                    
                    local burger = SpawnedInToys and SpawnedInToys:FindFirstChild(Config.ToyName)
                    
                    if not burger and Config.AntiNetTOgl then
                        pcall(function() 
                            SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(Config.ToyName, hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)) 
                        end)
                        burger = SpawnedInToys and SpawnedInToys:FindFirstChild(Config.ToyName)
                    end
                    
                    if burger and Config.AntiNetTOgl then
                        local holdPart = burger:FindFirstChild("HoldPart", true)
                        if holdPart then
                            local hRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
                            local dRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
                            local hPlayer = holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldingPlayer")
                            
                            if hPlayer and hPlayer.Value and hPlayer.Value ~= localPlayer then
                                if dRemote then 
                                    pcall(function() 
                                        dRemote:InvokeServer(burger, hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50000, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)) 
                                    end) 
                                end
                                burger:Destroy()
                            elseif hRemote and dRemote then
                                pcall(function()
                                    hRemote:InvokeServer(burger, char)
                                    dRemote:InvokeServer(burger, hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50000, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        else
            Config.ToyName = "FoodHamburger"
            local burger = SpawnedInToys and SpawnedInToys:FindFirstChild(Config.ToyName)
            if burger then
                DestroyToy:FireServer(burger)
            end
        end
    end
})

deeefef:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Network 2",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.Antinet2 = state
        if state then
            Config.hbConn = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not Config.Antinet2 then return end

                local character = localPlayer.Character
                local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")

                if not Config.coco or not Config.coco.Parent then
                    local toysFolder = workspace:WaitForChild(localPlayer.Name.."SpawnedInToys", 2)
                    if toysFolder then
                        SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
                            "FoodCoconut",
                            CFrame.new(-72.93, -5.97, -265.54),
                            Vector3.new(0,90,0)
                        )

                        Config.coco = toysFolder:WaitForChild("FoodCoconut", 2)
                        if Config.coco then
                            local holdPart = Config.coco:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 1)
                            if holdPart then
                                Config.holdRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
                                Config.dropRemote = holdPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return
                end

                if not Config.holdRemote or not Config.dropRemote or not head then return end

                pcall(function()
                    Config.holdRemote:InvokeServer(Config.coco, character)
                    Config.dropRemote:InvokeServer(
                        Config.coco,
                        CFrame.new(head.Position + Vector3.new(0,10,0)),
                        Vector3.zero
                    )
                end)
            end)
        else
            if Config.hbConn then
                Config.hbConn:Disconnect()
                Config.hbConn = nil
            end

            if Config.coco and Config.coco.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    if DestroyToy.FireServer then
                        DestroyToy:FireServer(Config.coco)
                    elseif DestroyToy.InvokeServer then
                        DestroyToy:InvokeServer(Config.coco)
                    end
                end)
            end

            Config.coco = nil
            Config.holdRemote = nil
            Config.dropRemote = nil
        end
    end
})

deeefef:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Network 3",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.Antiinput = Value
        if Config.Antiinput then 
            local char = GetCharacter()
            local hrp = HRP()
            local burger = SpawnedInToys:FindFirstChild("FoodMayonnaise")
            local HoldPart = burger and burger:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
            local DropItem, HoldItem
            while Config.Antiinput and task.wait() do 

                if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") then continue end

                HoldPart = burger and burger.Parent and burger:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
                DropItem, HoldItem = HoldPart and HoldPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction"), HoldPart and HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")

                if not HoldPart or not (DropItem and HoldItem) then   
                    for _, v in pairs(SpawnedInToys:GetChildren()) do  
                        if v.Name == "FoodMayonnaise" then  
                            DestroyToy:FireServer(v)
                        end
                    end
                    
                    local ToyName = "FoodMayonnaise"
                    
                    if InPlot.Value and not InOwnedPlot.Value then 
                        InPlot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
                    end 
                    if not CanSpawnToy.Value then 
                        CanSpawnToy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
                    end

                    local SpawnCF = (PLCF() or hrp.CFrame) * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
                    
                    local Container = nil
                    if InOwnedPlot.Value then
                        if Config.LastHouse == nil or Config.LastPlotOwner == nil or Config.LastPlotOwner.Parent == nil then 
                            for i = 1, 5 do 
                                local Plot = workspace.Plots["Plot"..i]
                                for _, v in pairs(Plot.PlotSign["ThisPlotsOwners"]:GetChildren()) do 
                                    if v.Value == plr.Name then 
                                        Config.LastHouse = workspace.PlotItems["Plot"..i]
                                        Config.LastPlotOwner = v 
                                        Container = Config.LastHouse
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                                if Container then break end
                            end
                        else
                            Container = Config.LastHouse
                        end
                    else
                        Container = SpawnedInToys
                    end

                    if Container then
                        local spawnedObject = nil
                        local connection
                        connection = Container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                            if child.Name == ToyName then
                                spawnedObject = child
                            end
                        end)

                        task.spawn(function()
                            pcall(function()
                                SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(ToyName, SpawnCF, Vector3.zero)
                            end)
                        end)

                        local start = tick()
                        repeat task.wait() until spawnedObject or (tick() - start) > 2.5

                        connection:Disconnect()
                        burger = spawnedObject
                    else
                        burger = nil
                    end

                    if not burger then continue end 
                    
                    HoldPart = burger:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") or burger:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 1)
                    
                    DropItem = HoldPart and (HoldPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction") or HoldPart:WaitForChild("DropItemRemoteFunction", 1))
                    HoldItem = HoldPart and (HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction") or HoldPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 1))
                end

                task.spawn(function()
                    HoldItem:InvokeServer(burger, char)
                    while HoldPart and not HoldPart.RigidConstraint.Attachment1 do task.wait() end 
                    
                    if HoldPart and HoldPart.RigidConstraint.Attachment1 ~= char["Left Arm"].LeftGripAttachment then 
                        for _, v in pairs(SpawnedInToys:GetChildren()) do  
                            if v.Name == "FoodMayonnaise" then  
                                DestroyToy:FireServer(v)
                            end
                        end
                        burger = nil
                    end
                end)
                task.wait(0.1)
                task.spawn(DropItem.InvokeServer, DropItem, burger, char:GetPivot() * CFrame.new(0, 500, 0), Vector3.zero)
            end
        end
    end
})
deeefef:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Packet",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            if Config.connection then return end

            Config.connection = ExtendGrabLine.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
                local args = { ... }

                local totalBytes = 0
                for _, v in ipairs(args) do
                    if typeof(v) == "string" then
                        totalBytes += #v
                    end
                end

                if totalBytes < Config.hold then return end
                if tick() - Config.lastNotify < Config.COOLDOWN then return end
                Config.lastNotify = tick()

                local sender = nil
                for _, v in ipairs(args) do
                    if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("Player") then
                        sender = v
                        break
                    end
                    if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("Model") then
                        local plr = service.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(v)
                        if plr then 
                            sender = plr 
                            break 
                        end
                    end
                    if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local model = v:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                        if model then
                            local plr = service.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
                            if plr then 
                                sender = plr 
                                break 
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if typeof(v) == "Instance" and v:IsA("Tool") then
                        local model = v:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                        if model then
                            local plr = service.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
                            if plr then 
                                sender = plr 
                                break 
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not sender then
                    sender = localPlayer
                end

                local senderName = sender.DisplayName 
                local mbSize = totalBytes / (1024 * 1024)

                local seen = {}
                local summarized = {}

                for _, v in ipairs(args) do
                    local key

                    if typeof(v) == "string" then
                        local str = v
                        if #str <= 80 then
                            key = "str:"..str
                        else
                            key = "str:"..str:sub(1, 80) .. "... (+" .. (#str - 80) .. " chars)"
                        end
                    elseif typeof(v) == "Instance" then
                        key = "inst:"..v.ClassName.."("..v.Name..")"
                    elseif typeof(v) == "table" then
                        local preview = {}
                        local count = 0
                        for _, val in pairs(v) do
                            count += 1
                            if count <= 5 then
                                table.insert(preview, tostring(val))
                            end
                        end
                        key = "tbl:table["..count.."] { "..table.concat(preview, ", ").." ... }"
                    else
                        key = typeof(v)..":"..tostring(v)
                    end

                    seen[key] = (seen[key] or 0) + 1
                end

                for k, count in pairs(seen) do
                    if count > 1 then
                        table.insert(summarized, k.." x"..count)
                    else
                        table.insert(summarized, k)
                    end
                end

                FrostNotify(string.format(
                    "Sender: %s\nSize: %.3f MB\nArgs:\n%s",
                    senderName,
                    mbSize,
                    table.concat(summarized, "\n")
                ))
            end)
        else
            if Config.connection then
                Config.connection:Disconnect()
                Config.connection = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

    Name = "Anti-Loop",

deeefef:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Ragdoll",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AntiragdollToggle = Value
        if not Config.connections then Config.connections = {} end

        local function clearConnections()
            for _, conn in ipairs(Config.connections) do
                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            end
            Config.connections = {}
        end

        local function protectHumanoid(humanoid)
            humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            humanoid.AutoRotate = true
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false

            table.insert(Config.connections, humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
                if Config.AntiragdollToggle and health <= 0 then
                    humanoid.Health = 1
                end
            end))

            table.insert(Config.connections, humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AutoRotate"):Connect(function()
                if Config.AntiragdollToggle and humanoid.AutoRotate == false then
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end))

            table.insert(Config.connections, humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlatformStand"):Connect(function()
                if Config.AntiragdollToggle and humanoid.PlatformStand == true then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                end
            end))

            table.insert(Config.connections, service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if Config.AntiragdollToggle then
                    if humanoid.Sit and humanoid.SeatPart == nil then
                        humanoid.Sit = false
                    end
                end
            end))
        end

        local function onCharacter(char)
            local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if humanoid and Config.AntiragdollToggle then
                protectHumanoid(humanoid)
            end
        end

        if Config.AntiragdollToggle then
            local character = localPlayer.Character
            if character then
                onCharacter(character)
            end

            Config.charAddedConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacter)
        else
            clearConnections()
            if Config.charAddedConn then
                Config.charAddedConn:Disconnect()
                Config.charAddedConn = nil
            end
        end
    end
})
