    Name = "Anti-Lag",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        if CharacterAndBeamMove then
            CharacterAndBeamMove.Disabled = state
        end
    end
})

antikickpcld:AddButton({
    Name = "Break PCLD",
    Callback = function()
        local serverPos = CFrame.new(-272.2197265625, -7.350403785705566, 475.0108947753906)
        workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0

        local storedJoints = {}
        local root
        local conn

        local char1 = localPlayer.Character
        if char1 then
            root = char1:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            for _, v in ipairs(char1:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("Motor6D") then
                    storedJoints[v] = v.Part0
                    v.Part0 = nil
                end
            end

            root.CFrame = serverPos

            conn = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if root and root.Parent then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end)
        end

        task.wait(0.12)

        if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
        for m, p0 in pairs(storedJoints) do
            if m and m.Parent then m.Part0 = p0 end
        end
        storedJoints = {}
        localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Once(function()
            task.wait(0.25)

            local char2 = localPlayer.Character
            if char2 then
                root = char2:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                for _, v in ipairs(char2:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v:IsA("Motor6D") then
                        storedJoints[v] = v.Part0
                        v.Part0 = nil
                    end
                end

                root.CFrame = serverPos

                conn = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    if root and root.Parent then
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end)
            end

            task.wait(0.12)

            if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
            for m, p0 in pairs(storedJoints) do
                if m and m.Parent then m.Part0 = p0 end
            end
            storedJoints = {}
        end)
        
    end
})

Config.AntiKickToggle = antikick:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Kick",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AntikickT = Value

        local function ClearKunai()
            local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
            if inv and DestroyToy then
                for _, v in pairs(inv:GetChildren()) do
                    if v.Name == "AntiKick" or v.Name == "NinjaShuriken" then
                        pcall(function()
                            DestroyToy:FireServer(v)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                local function getHRP()
                    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        return localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    else
                        local character = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                        return character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    end
                end

                local function CheckForHome()
                    if not service.Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) then
                        return false
                    end
                    for _, v in pairs(service.Workspace.Plots:GetChildren()) do
                        local sign = v:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                        local owners = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
                        if owners then
                            for _, b in pairs(owners:GetChildren()) do
                                if b.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                                    local folder = service.Workspace.PlotItems:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
                                    if folder then
                                        return true, folder
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return false
                end

                local function StickKunai(kunai)
                    if not kunai or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                        return
                    end

                    local currentHRP = getHRP()
                    if not currentHRP then
                        return
                    end

                    if kunai:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
                        if not kunai.SoundPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or kunai.SoundPart.PartOwner.Value ~= localPlayer.Name then
                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(kunai.SoundPart, kunai.SoundPart.CFrame)
                        end
                    end

                    local firePart = currentHRP:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") or currentHRP:WaitForChild("FirePlayerPart", 5)
                    if firePart then
                        StickyPartEvent:FireServer(
                            kunai.StickyPart,
                            firePart,
                            CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), math.rad(90))
                        )
                    end

                    for _, obj in pairs(kunai:GetChildren()) do
                        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                            obj.CanTouch = false
                            obj.CanCollide = false
                            obj.CanQuery = false
                            obj.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end
                end

                local function SpawnToy(name)
                    local t = tick()
                    while not CanSpawnToy.Value do
                        if not Config.AntikickT or tick() - t > 5 then
                            return nil
                        end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end

                    local currentHRP = getHRP()
                    if currentHRP then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            pcall(function()
                                SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
                                    name,
                                    currentHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 12, 20),
                                    Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                )
                            end)
                        end)
                    end

                    local boolik, house = CheckForHome()
                    local inv = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")

                    if boolik and house then
                        return house:WaitForChild(name, 2)
                    elseif not service.Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) and inv then
                        return inv:WaitForChild(name, 2)
                    end
                    return nil
                end

                while Config.AntikickT do
                    task.wait(0.005)

                    if not localPlayer.Character
                        or not localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        or localPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                        continue
                    end

                    local inv = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                    local kunai = inv and inv:FindFirstChild("NinjaShuriken")

                    if service.Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) then
                        local boolik, house = CheckForHome()
                        if boolik and house and service.Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(house.Name) then
                            local sign = service.Workspace.Plots[house.Name]:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                            if sign and sign.ThisPlotsOwners.Value.TimeRemainingNum.Value > 89 then
                                kunai = SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
                                if kunai == nil then
                                    continue
                                end
                                kunai.Name = "AntiKick"
                                StickKunai(kunai)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if not kunai then
                        if service.Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name) then
                            continue
                        end
                        kunai = SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
                        if kunai == nil then
                            continue
                        end
                        kunai.Name = "AntiKick"
                        if not kunai then
                            continue
                        end
                    end

                    repeat
                        if kunai
                            and kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                            and kunai.StickyPart.CanTouch == true then
                            StickKunai(kunai)
                            kunai.Name = "AntiKick"
                        end
                        task.wait(0.3)
                    until not kunai
                        or not Config.AntikickT
                        or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                        or kunai.StickyPart.CanTouch == false
                        or not localPlayer.Character
                        or not localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                        or (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - kunai.StickyPart.Position).Magnitude >= 20

                    if not kunai
                        or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                        or not localPlayer.Character
                        or not localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        or (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - kunai.StickyPart.Position).Magnitude >= 20 then
                        ClearKunai()
                    end

                    pcall(function()
                        repeat
                            task.wait(0.05)
                        until not Config.AntikickT
                            or not localPlayer.Character
                            or not localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                            or not kunai
                            or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                            or not kunai.StickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")
                            or not kunai.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1

                        if not kunai
                            or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                            or (localPlayer.Character
                                and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                and localPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0)
                            or not kunai.StickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld")
                            or not kunai.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                            ClearKunai()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        else
            Config.AntikickT = false
            ClearKunai()
        end
    end
})

antikick:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto-Reset",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AntikickResetToggle = Value
        
        if Config.AntikickResetToggle then
            Config.Contuuti['GameNotify'] = GameCorrectionsNotify.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(actionType)
                if actionType == "Flying" then
                    Struggle:FireServer(localPlayer)
                    
                    local parent = localPlayer.Character
                    local childName = "Humanoid"
                    local humanoid = parent:FindFirstChild(childName) or parent:WaitForChild(childName, nil)
                    
                    if humanoid then
                        humanoid.Health = 0
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            local playname = "GameNotify"
            for connectionName, connection in Config.Contuuti do
                if connectionName:find(playname) then
                    connection:Disconnect()
                    connection = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

antikick:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto-Enable",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AutoTurnOnAntiKick = Value
        
        if Config.AutoTurnOnAntiKick then
            Config.Contuuti['AntiKickAutoOn'] = GameCorrectionsNotify.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(actionType)
                if actionType == "Flying" then
                    if not Config.antikick and Config.AntiKickToggle then
                        Config.AntiKickToggle:Set(true)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if Config.Contuuti['AntiKickAutoOn'] then
                Config.Contuuti['AntiKickAutoOn']:Disconnect()
                Config.Contuuti['AntiKickAutoOn'] = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

weat:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Water (Walk-Water)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local oceanPath = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
            and workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Ocean")
            and workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Ocean:FindFirstChild("Object")
            and workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects.Ocean.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")

        if oceanPath then
            for _, part in pairs(oceanPath:GetChildren()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name == "Ocean" then
                    part.CanCollide = state
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

AntikickES:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Kick ESP",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            for _,pl in service.Players:GetPlayers() do
                if pl~=plr then 
                    for i,v in workspace[pl.Name.."SpawnedInToys"]:GetChildren() do
                        if v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                            local high = Instance.new("Highlight", v)
                            high.Adornee = v
                            if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(192, 173, 0)
                            else
                                high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 194, 0)
                            end
                            v.StickyPart.StickyWeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Part1"):Connect(function()
                                if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(192, 173, 0)
                                else
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 194, 0)
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                    Config.Contuuti["antikickesp"..pl.Name] = workspace[pl.Name.."SpawnedInToys"].ChildAdded:Connect(function(v)
                        task.wait(0.4)
                        if v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                            local high = Instance.new("Highlight", v)
                            high.Adornee = v
                            if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(192, 173, 0)
                            else
                                high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 194, 0)
                            end
                            v.StickyPart.StickyWeld:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Part1"):Connect(function()
                                if v.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1 then
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(192, 173, 0)
                                else
                                    high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 194, 0)
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        else
            for i,v in service.Players:GetPlayers() do
                if v~=plr then
                    if Config.Contuuti["antikickesp"..v.Name] then Config.Contuuti["antikickesp"..v.Name]:Disconnect() Config.Contuuti["antikickesp"..v.Name] = nil end
                    for i,v in workspace[v.Name.."SpawnedInToys"]:GetChildren() do
                        if v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                            if v.Parent:FindFirstChild("Highlight") then v.Parent.Highlight:Destroy() end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

ESPPLA:AddToggle({
    Name = "Highlight (ESP)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.ESPhight = Value

        local function ApplyHighlightESP(player)
            if player == localPlayer then return end

            local function setupHighlight(character)
                if not Config.ESPhight then return end
                
                if character:FindFirstChild("ESP_Highlight") then
                    character.ESP_Highlight:Destroy()
                end

                local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                highlight.Name = "ESP_Highlight"
                highlight.FillColor = Config.HighlightFill
                highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
                highlight.OutlineColor = Config.HighlightOutline
                highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
                highlight.Adornee = character
                highlight.Parent = character

                task.spawn(function()
                    while Config.ESPhight and character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and highlight.Parent do
                        if highlight.FillColor ~= Config.HighlightFill then highlight.FillColor = Config.HighlightFill end
                        if highlight.OutlineColor ~= Config.HighlightOutline then highlight.OutlineColor = Config.HighlightOutline end
                        task.wait(1)
                    end
                    if highlight.Parent then
                        highlight:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
            end

            player.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupHighlight)
            if player.Character then 
                task.spawn(setupHighlight, player.Character) 
            end
        end

        for _, player in pairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if Config.ESPhight then
                ApplyHighlightESP(player)
            else
                if player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("ESP_Highlight") then
                    player.Character.ESP_Highlight:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end

        service.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
            if Config.ESPhight then
                ApplyHighlightESP(player)
            end
        end)
    end    
})

ESPPLA:AddToggle({
    Name = "ESP Box",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(value)
        Config.ESPBox = value
        
        for _, p in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= localPlayer and p.Character then
                local root = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if Config.ESPBox and root then
                    if not root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                        local char = p.Character
                        if not char:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                            task.delay(1, function()
                                local r = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if r and not r:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                                    local box = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                                    box.Name = "BoxESP"
                                    box.Size = Vector3.new(4, 6, 2)
                                    box.Adornee = r
                                    box.AlwaysOnTop = true
                                    box.ZIndex = 5
                                    box.Transparency = 0.5
                                    box.Color3 = Config.ESPBoxColor
                                    box.Parent = r
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    else
                        root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP").Color3 = Config.ESPBoxColor
                    end
                elseif root and root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                    root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP"):Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

ESPPLA:AddToggle({
    Name = "Tracer ESP",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.TracerT = Value
        if Value then
            for _, p in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= localPlayer and not Config.actresa[p] then
                    local line = Drawing.new("Line")
                    line.Thickness = 1
                    line.Color = Config.TracerColor
                    line.Visible = false
                    Config.actresa[p] = line
                end
            end
            if not Config.Connections["Tracer"] then
                Config.Connections["Tracer"] = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    for player, line in pairs(Config.actresa) do
                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            local hrp = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                            local vector, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
                            if onScreen and Config.TracerT then
                                line.From = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y)
                                line.To = Vector2.new(vector.X, vector.Y)
                                line.Color = Config.TracerColor
                                line.Visible = true
                            else
                                line.Visible = false
                            end
                        else
                            line.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not Config.PlayerAddedConns["Tracer"] then
                Config.PlayerAddedConns["Tracer"] = service.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
                    if p ~= localPlayer and not Config.actresa[p] then
                        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
                        line.Thickness = 1
                        line.Color = Config.TracerColor
                        line.Visible = false
                        Config.actresa[p] = line
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not Config.Connections["TracerRemoving"] then
                Config.Connections["TracerRemoving"] = service.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
                    if Config.actresa[p] then
                        Config.actresa[p]:Destroy()
                        Config.actresa[p] = nil
                    end
                end)
            end
        else
            if Config.Connections["Tracer"] then Config.Connections["Tracer"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["Tracer"] = nil end
            if Config.PlayerAddedConns["Tracer"] then Config.PlayerAddedConns["Tracer"]:Disconnect() Config.PlayerAddedConns["Tracer"] = nil end
            if Config.Connections["TracerRemoving"] then Config.Connections["TracerRemoving"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["TracerRemoving"] = nil end
            for p, l in pairs(Config.actresa) do
                l:Destroy()
                Config.actresa[p] = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

ESPPLA:AddToggle({
    Name = "Skeleton ESP",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.SkeletonT = Value
        if Value then
            for _, p in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= localPlayer and not Config.Acskeleton[p] then
                    local bones = {}
                    for i = 1, 6 do
                        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
                        line.Thickness = 1
                        line.Color = Config.SkeletonColor
                        line.Visible = false
                        bones[i] = line
                    end
                    Config.Acskeleton[p] = bones
                end
            end
            if not Config.Connections["Skeleton"] then
                Config.Connections["Skeleton"] = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    for player, bones in pairs(Config.Acskeleton) do
                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                            local char = player.Character
                            local upperTorso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                            local leftArm = char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
                            local rightArm = char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                            local leftLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
                            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")

                            if upperTorso and head and leftArm and rightArm and leftLeg and rightLeg and Config.SkeletonT then
                                local parts = {head, upperTorso, leftArm, rightArm, leftLeg, rightLeg}
                                local locs = {}
                                local allOnScreen = true

                                for i, part in ipairs(parts) do
                                    local vec, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                                    if not onScreen then allOnScreen = false break end
                                    locs[i] = Vector2.new(vec.X, vec.Y)
                                end

                                if allOnScreen then
                                    bones[1].From = locs[1] bones[1].To = locs[2]
                                    bones[2].From = locs[2] bones[2].To = locs[3]
                                    bones[3].From = locs[2] bones[3].To = locs[4]
                                    bones[4].From = locs[2] bones[4].To = locs[5]
                                    bones[5].From = locs[2] bones[5].To = locs[6]
                                    bones[6].From = locs[3] bones[6].To = locs[4]

                                    for _, line in ipairs(bones) do
                                        line.Color = Config.SkeletonColor
                                        line.Visible = true
                                    end
                                else
                                    for _, line in ipairs(bones) do line.Visible = false end
                                end
                            else
                                for _, line in ipairs(bones) do line.Visible = false end
                            end
                        else
                            for _, line in ipairs(bones) do line.Visible = false end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not Config.PlayerAddedConns["Skeleton"] then
                Config.PlayerAddedConns["Skeleton"] = service.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
                    if p ~= localPlayer and not Config.Acskeleton[p] then
                        local bones = {}
                        for i = 1, 6 do
                            local line = Drawing.new("Line")
                            line.Thickness = 1
                            line.Color = Config.SkeletonColor
                            line.Visible = false
                            ESPTable.insert(bones, line)
                        end
                        Config.Acskeleton[p] = bones
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not Config.Connections["SkeletonRemoving"] then
                Config.Connections["SkeletonRemoving"] = service.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
                    if Config.Acskeleton[p] then
                        for _, line in pairs(Config.Acskeleton[p]) do line:Destroy() end
                        Config.Acskeleton[p] = nil
                    end
                end)
            end
        else
            if Config.Connections["Skeleton"] then Config.Connections["Skeleton"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["Skeleton"] = nil end
            if Config.PlayerAddedConns["Skeleton"] then Config.PlayerAddedConns["Skeleton"]:Disconnect() Config.PlayerAddedConns["Skeleton"] = nil end
            if Config.Connections["SkeletonRemoving"] then Config.Connections["SkeletonRemoving"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["SkeletonRemoving"] = nil end
            for p, bones in pairs(Config.Acskeleton) do
                for _, line in ipairs(bones) do line:Destroy() end
                Config.Acskeleton[p] = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

ESPPLA:AddToggle({
    Name = "Info ESP",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.InfoT = Value
        if Value then
            local function initInfo(p)
                if p == localPlayer or Config.AcInfos[p] then return end
                local bgui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                bgui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 100)
                bgui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                bgui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                bgui.Adornee = p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                tl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                tl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                tl.TextColor3 = Config.InfoColor
                tl.TextSize = 14
                tl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                tl.Parent = bgui
                bgui.Parent = service.CoreGui
                Config.AcInfos[p] = {Gui = bgui, Label = tl}
                Config.CharAddedConns["Info"..p.Name] = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    if Config.AcInfos[p] then Config.AcInfos[p].Gui.Adornee = char:FindFirstChild("Head") end
                end)
            end
            for _, p in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do initInfo(p) end
            if not Config.Connections["Info"] then
                Config.Connections["Info"] = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    for player, data in pairs(Config.AcInfos) do
                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                            local head = player.Character.Head
                            local hum = player.Character.Humanoid
                            data.Gui.Adornee = head
                            if Config.InfoT then
                                local dist = math.floor((Camera.CFrame.Position - head.Position).Magnitude)
                                data.Label.Text = string.format("Name: %s\nDist: %d\nHP: %d/%d", player.Name, dist, math.floor(hum.Health), math.floor(hum.MaxHealth))
                                data.Label.TextColor3 = Config.InfoColor
                                data.Gui.Enabled = true
                            else
                                data.Gui.Enabled = false
                            end
                        else
                            data.Gui.Enabled = false
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not Config.PlayerAddedConns["Info"] then
                Config.PlayerAddedConns["Info"] = service.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(initInfo)
            end
            if not Config.Connections["InfoRemoving"] then
                Config.Connections["InfoRemoving"] = service.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
                    if Config.AcInfos[p] then
                        Config.AcInfos[p].Gui:Destroy()
                        Config.AcInfos[p] = nil
                    end
                    if Config.CharAddedConns["Info"..p.Name] then Config.CharAddedConns["Info"..p.Name]:Disconnect() Config.CharAddedConns["Info"..p.Name] = nil end
                end)
            end
        else
            if Config.Connections["Info"] then Config.Connections["Info"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["Info"] = nil end
            if Config.PlayerAddedConns["Info"] then Config.PlayerAddedConns["Info"]:Disconnect() Config.PlayerAddedConns["Info"] = nil end
            if Config.Connections["InfoRemoving"] then Config.Connections["InfoRemoving"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["InfoRemoving"] = nil end
            for p, data in pairs(Config.AcInfos) do
                data.Gui:Destroy()
                Config.AcInfos[p] = nil
                if Config.CharAddedConns["Info"..p.Name] then Config.CharAddedConns["Info"..p.Name]:Disconnect() Config.CharAddedConns["Info"..p.Name] = nil end
            end
        end
    end
})

ESPPLA:AddToggle({
    Name = "Friend ESP",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.FriendT = Value
        if Value then
            local function initFriend(p)
                if p == localPlayer or Config.AcFriends[p] then return end
                if localPlayer:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) then
                    local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
                    hl.FillColor = Config.FriendColor
                    hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    hl.FillTransparency = 0.5
                    hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
                    hl.Adornee = p.Character
                    hl.Parent = service.CoreGui
                    Config.AcFriends[p] = hl
                    Config.CharAddedConns["Friend"..p.Name] = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        if Config.AcFriends[p] then Config.AcFriends[p].Adornee = char end
                    end)
                end
            end
            for _, p in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do initFriend(p) end
            if not Config.Connections["Friend"] then
                Config.Connections["Friend"] = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    for player, hl in pairs(Config.AcFriends) do
                        if player.Character and Config.FriendT then
                            hl.Adornee = player.Character
                            hl.FillColor = Config.FriendColor
                            hl.Enabled = true
                        else
                            hl.Enabled = false
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not Config.PlayerAddedConns["Friend"] then
                Config.PlayerAddedConns["Friend"] = service.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(initFriend)
            end
            if not Config.Connections["FriendRemoving"] then
                Config.Connections["FriendRemoving"] = service.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
                    if Config.AcFriends[p] then
                        Config.AcFriends[p]:Destroy()
                        Config.AcFriends[p] = nil
                    end
                    if Config.CharAddedConns["Friend"..p.Name] then Config.CharAddedConns["Friend"..p.Name]:Disconnect() Config.CharAddedConns["Friend"..p.Name] = nil end
                end)
            end
        else
            if Config.Connections["Friend"] then Config.Connections["Friend"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["Friend"] = nil end
            if Config.PlayerAddedConns["Friend"] then Config.PlayerAddedConns["Friend"]:Disconnect() Config.PlayerAddedConns["Friend"] = nil end
            if Config.Connections["FriendRemoving"] then Config.Connections["FriendRemoving"]:Disconnect() Config.Connections["FriendRemoving"] = nil end
            for p, hl in pairs(Config.AcFriends) do
                hl:Destroy()
                Config.AcFriends[p] = nil
                if Config.CharAddedConns["Friend"..p.Name] then Config.CharAddedConns["Friend"..p.Name]:Disconnect() Config.CharAddedConns["Friend"..p.Name] = nil end
            end
        end
    end
})

ESPPLA:AddToggle({
    Name = "Icon ESP",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        Config.ESPICON = enabled
            if enabled then
            local espIconTemplate = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            local imageButton = Instance.new("ImageButton")
            local uiCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            local uiTextSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
            local uiAspectRatioConstraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
                
            espIconTemplate.Name = "ESP"
            espIconTemplate.Parent = nil
            espIconTemplate.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            espIconTemplate.Active = true
            espIconTemplate.Adornee = nil
            espIconTemplate.AlwaysOnTop = true
            espIconTemplate.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
            espIconTemplate.Size = UDim2.new(3, 50, 3, 45)
            
            imageButton.Name = "UserImage"
            imageButton.Parent = espIconTemplate
            imageButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            imageButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            imageButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageButton.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            imageButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
            imageButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.300000012, 0)
            imageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0.5, 5)
            imageButton.Image = ""
            
            uiCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(2, 0)
            uiCorner.Parent = imageButton
                
            textLabel.Name = "Username"
            textLabel.Parent = espIconTemplate
            textLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            textLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            textLabel.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            textLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
            textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.75999999, 0)
            textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 5, 0.340000004, 5)
            textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
            textLabel.Text = ""
            textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            textLabel.TextScaled = true
            textLabel.TextSize = 35
            textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            textLabel.TextWrapped = true
                
            uiTextSizeConstraint.Parent = textLabel
            uiTextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = 35
            uiTextSizeConstraint.MinTextSize = 15
                
            uiAspectRatioConstraint.Parent = espIconTemplate
            uiAspectRatioConstraint.AspectRatio = 1.043

            local connections = {}
            local playersService = service.Players
                
            local playerAddedConnection = playersService.PlayerAdded:Connect(
                function(newPlayer)
                    if newPlayer ~= localPlayer then
                        local character = newPlayer.Character or newPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                        if character then
                            local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 1)
                            if not character:FindFirstChild("ESP") and head then
                                local espClone = espIconTemplate:Clone()
                                espClone.Parent = character
                                espClone.Adornee = head
                                espClone.Username.Text = newPlayer.Name
                                espClone.UserImage.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. newPlayer.UserId .. "&width=420&height=420&format=png"
                                    
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    while character.Parent and Config.ESPICON do
                                        task.wait(0.25)
                                    end
                                    espClone:Destroy()
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                            
                        connections[#connections + 1] = newPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(
                            function(character)
                                if character then
                                    local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 1)
                                    if not character:FindFirstChild("ESP") and head then
                                        local espClone = espIconTemplate:Clone()
                                        espClone.Parent = character
                                        espClone.Adornee = head
                                        espClone.Username.Text = newPlayer.Name
                                        espClone.UserImage.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. newPlayer.UserId .. "&width=420&height=420&format=png"
                                            
                                        task.spawn(function()
                                            while character.Parent and Config.ESPICON do
                                                task.wait(0.25)
                                            end
                                            espClone:Destroy()
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        )
                    end
                end
            )
                
            local nextFunc, tableRef, currentKey = pairs(playersService:GetPlayers())
            while true do
                local Player
                currentKey, Player = nextFunc(tableRef, currentKey)
                if currentKey == nil then
                    break
                end

                if Player ~= localPlayer then
                    local character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    if character then
                        local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 1)
                        if not character:FindFirstChild("ESP") and head then
                            local espClone = espIconTemplate:Clone()
                            espClone.Parent = character
                            espClone.Adornee = head
                            espClone.Username.Text = Player.Name
                            espClone.UserImage.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. Player.UserId .. "&width=420&height=420&format=png"
                                
                            task.spawn(function()
                                while character.Parent and Config.ESPICON do
                                    task.wait(0.25)
                                end
                                espClone:Destroy()
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                        
                    connections[#connections + 1] = Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(
                        function(character)
                            if character then
                                local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 1)
                                if not character:FindFirstChild("ESP") and head then
                                    local espClone = espIconTemplate:Clone()
                                    espClone.Parent = character
                                    espClone.Adornee = head
                                    espClone.Username.Text = Player.Name
                                    espClone.UserImage.Image = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId=" .. Player.UserId .. "&width=420&height=420&format=png"
                                        
                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        while character.Parent and Config.ESPICON do
                                            task.wait(0.25)
                                        end
                                        espClone:Destroy()
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    )
                end
            end
                
            while Config.ESPICON do
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
                
            playerAddedConnection:Disconnect()
                
            local dNextFunc, dTableRef, dCurrentKey = pairs(connections)
            while true do
                local connection
                dCurrentKey, connection = dNextFunc(dTableRef, dCurrentKey)
                if dCurrentKey == nil then
                    break
                end
                if typeof(connection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    connection:Disconnect()
               end
           end
           table.clear(connections)
       end
   end
})

PCES:AddToggle({
    Name = "PCLD",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.PCLDToggle = Value

        local partname = {
            "partesp",
            "playercharacterlocationdetector"
        }

        local function isTarget(obj)
            if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
            for _, name in ipairs(partname) do
                if string.lower(obj.Name) == string.lower(name) then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function CRESP(obj)
            if Config.PCLDBOX[obj] then return end

            local container = Instance.new("Folder")
            container.Name = "ESP_Container"
            
            local lineBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
            lineBox.Name = "LineBox"
            lineBox.Adornee = obj
            lineBox.LineThickness = 0.01
            lineBox.Color3 = Config.PCLDLine
            lineBox.SurfaceColor3 = Config.PCLDSurface
            lineBox.SurfaceTransparency = 0.95
            lineBox.Transparency = 0
            lineBox.Parent = container

            local fillBox = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
            fillBox.Name = "FillBox"
            fillBox.Adornee = obj
            fillBox.Size = obj.Size
            fillBox.Color3 = Config.PCLDFill
            fillBox.Transparency = 0.9
            fillBox.AlwaysOnTop = true
            fillBox.ZIndex = 5
            fillBox.Parent = container

            container.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            Config.PCLDBOX[obj] = container

            obj.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent and Config.PCLDBOX[obj] then
                    Config.PCLDBOX[obj]:Destroy()
                    Config.PCLDBOX[obj] = nil
                end
            end)
        end

        local function delectedallPCLD()
            if Config.PCLDCONN then
                Config.PCLDCONN:Disconnect()
                Config.PCLDCONN = nil
            end

            for obj, container in pairs(Config.PCLDBOX) do
                if container then
                    container:Destroy()
                end
            end
            Config.PCLDBOX = {}
        end

        if Config.PCLDToggle then
            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if isTarget(obj) then
                    CRESP(obj)
                end
            end

            Config.PCLDCONN = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if isTarget(obj) then
                    task.wait(0.1) 
                    if Config.PCLDToggle and obj:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                        CRESP(obj)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            delectedallPCLD()
        end
    end
})

Blsck:AddToggle({
    Name = "BlackHole ESP",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.blackHoleESP = Value
        if Value then
            for _, obj in ipairs(service.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if obj.Name == "BlackHoleKick" then
                    if not Config.BlackholeSelectionB[obj] then
                        local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                        selectionBox.Adornee = obj
                        selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.05
                        selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 191, 255)
                        selectionBox.Parent = obj
                        Config.BlackholeSelectionB[obj] = selectionBox
                        local holePart = obj:FindFirstChild("Hole")
                        if holePart and holePart:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local billboardGui = holePart:FindFirstChildOfClass("BillboardGui")
                            if billboardGui then
                                billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                            end
                            
                            local localChar = LocalPlayer.Character
                            if localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                local localHrp = localChar.HumanoidRootPart
                                
                                local holeAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                                holeAttachment.Parent = holePart
                                
                                local localAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                                localAttachment.Parent = localHrp
                                
                                local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                                beam.Attachment0 = localAttachment
                                beam.Attachment1 = holeAttachment
                                beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(0, 191, 255))
                                beam.Width0 = 0.1
                                beam.Width1 = 0.1
                                beam.Parent = holePart
                                
                                Config.BlackholeBeam[obj] = {
                                    beam = beam,
                                    holeAttachment = holeAttachment,
                                    localAttachment = localAttachment
                                }
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            
            if Config.BlackHoleConnection then Config.BlackHoleConnection:Disconnect() end
            Config.BlackHoleConnection = service.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if Config.blackHoleESP and child.Name == "BlackHoleKick" then
                    if not Config.BlackholeSelectionB[child] then
                        local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                        selectionBox.Adornee = child
                        selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.05
                        selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 191, 255)
                        selectionBox.Parent = child
                        Config.BlackholeSelectionB[child] = selectionBox
                        
                        local holePart = child:FindFirstChild("Hole")
                        if holePart and holePart:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local billboardGui = holePart:FindFirstChildOfClass("BillboardGui")
                            if billboardGui then
                                billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                            end
                            
                            local localChar = LocalPlayer.Character
                            if localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                local localHrp = localChar.HumanoidRootPart
                                
                                local holeAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                                holeAttachment.Parent = holePart
                                
                                local localAttachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                                localAttachment.Parent = localHrp
                                
                                local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                                beam.Attachment0 = localAttachment
                                beam.Attachment1 = holeAttachment
                                beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(0, 191, 255))
                                beam.Width0 = 0.1
                                beam.Width1 = 0.1
                                beam.Parent = holePart
                                
                                Config.BlackholeBeam[child] = {
                                    beam = beam,
                                    holeAttachment = holeAttachment,
                                    localAttachment = localAttachment
                                }
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if Config.BlackHoleConnection then
                Config.BlackHoleConnection:Disconnect()
                Config.BlackHoleConnection = nil
            end
            for blackHole in pairs(Config.BlackholeSelectionB) do
                if Config.BlackholeSelectionB[blackHole] then
                    Config.BlackholeSelectionB[blackHole]:Destroy()
                    Config.BlackholeSelectionB[blackHole] = nil
                end
                
                if Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole] then
                    if Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole].beam then Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole].beam:Destroy() end
                    if Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole].holeAttachment then Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole].holeAttachment:Destroy() end
                    if Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole].localAttachment then Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole].localAttachment:Destroy() end
                    Config.BlackholeBeam[blackHole] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

ESPColor:AddColorpicker({
    Name = "Highlight Fill Color",

ESPColor:AddColorpicker({
    Name = "Box Color",
    Default = Config.ESPBoxColor,
    Callback = function(value)
        Config.ESPBoxColor = value
        
        for _, p in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= localPlayer and p.Character then
                local root = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if Config.ESPBox and root then
                    if not root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                        local char = p.Character
                        if not char:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                            task.delay(1, function()
                                local r = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if r and not r:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                                    local box = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                                    box.Name = "BoxESP"
                                    box.Size = Vector3.new(4, 6, 2)
                                    box.Adornee = r
                                    box.AlwaysOnTop = true
                                    box.ZIndex = 5
                                    box.Transparency = 0.5
                                    box.Color3 = Config.ESPBoxColor
                                    box.Parent = r
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    else
                        root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP").Color3 = Config.ESPBoxColor
                    end
                elseif root and root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP") then
                    root:FindFirstChild("BoxESP"):Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

EmoteTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Play Animation",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(on)
        Config.AnimmetiSta = on
        
        if on then
            local char = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if not animator then
                    animator = Instance.new("Animator")
                    animator.Parent = hum
                end

                if Config.cutell then
                    Config.cutell:Stop()
                    Config.cutell = nil
                end
                
                local animationsTable = {
                    ["idle"] = "rbxassetid://27744947",
                    ["Hugs"] = "rbxassetid://1119816160",
                    ["Dance"] = "rbxassetid://161099825",
                    ["Dance2"] = "rbxassetid://182435998",
                    ["Dance3"] = "rbxassetid://248263260",
                    ["Crazy"] = "rbxassetid://248263260",
                    ["Insane"] = "rbxassetid://35654637",
                    ["Collapse"] = "rbxassetid://35154961",
                    ["Zombie"] = "rbxassetid://33796059",
                }
                
                local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
                anim.AnimationId = animationsTable[Config.animaselect]
                
                Config.cutell = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
                Config.cutell.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                Config.cutell.Looped = true
                Config.cutell:Play()
            end
        else
            if Config.cutell then
                Config.cutell:Stop()
                Config.cutell = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

Inlff:AddToggle({
    Name = "Inf Line",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.InfLineToggle = state

        if state then
            local oldGui = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):FindFirstChild("ExtendControlGui")
            if oldGui then oldGui:Destroy() end

            local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            gui.Name = "ExtendControlGui"
            gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            gui.Parent = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
            gui.Enabled = service.UserInputService.TouchEnabled

            local CAG = localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ContextActionGui")
            if CAG then
                CAG.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
                    if Config.InfLineToggle and descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
                        local actionIcon = descendant:WaitForChild("ActionIcon", 2)
                        if actionIcon and (actionIcon.Image == "rbxassetid://9603826756" or actionIcon.Image == "rbxassetid://9603831913") then
                            actionIcon.Parent.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end

            local imageButton = Instance.new("ImageButton")
            imageButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
            imageButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -259)
            imageButton.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"
            imageButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageButton.ImageTransparency = 0.2
            imageButton.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
            imageButton.Visible = false
            imageButton.Parent = gui

            local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            imageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            imageLabel.Image = "rbxassetid://9603831913"
            imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageLabel.Parent = imageButton

            local imageButtonDe = Instance.new("ImageButton")
            imageButtonDe.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
            imageButtonDe.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, -211)
            imageButtonDe.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"
            imageButtonDe.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageButtonDe.ImageTransparency = 0.2
            imageButtonDe.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
            imageButtonDe.Visible = false
            imageButtonDe.Parent = gui

            local imageLabelDe = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            imageLabelDe.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            imageLabelDe.Image = "rbxassetid://9603826756"
            imageLabelDe.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageLabelDe.Parent = imageButtonDe

            local function toggleButtonState(buttonState)
                local isVisible = buttonState and Config.InfLineToggle
                imageButton.Visible = isVisible
                imageButton.Active = isVisible
                imageButtonDe.Visible = isVisible
                imageButtonDe.Active = isVisible
            end

            local function adjustDistance(amount)
                if not Config.InfLineToggle then return end

                Config.PcDistance = Config.PcDistance + amount
                if Config.PcDistance < Config.MinDistance then
                    Config.PcDistance = Config.MinDistance
                end

                if senv and senv.distance then
                    senv.distance = Config.PcDistance
                end
            end

            local function startLongPress(mode)
                while Config.IsTouching do
                    if mode == "increase" then
                        adjustDistance(Config.LineDistance)
                    else
                        adjustDistance(-Config.LineDistance)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end

            local function endTouch(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    Config.IsTouching = false
                end
            end

            Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == "GrabParts" and child:IsA("Model") then
                    if Config.InfLineToggle and (service.UserInputService.MouseEnabled or service.UserInputService.TouchEnabled) then
                        local grabPart = child:WaitForChild("GrabPart")
                        local dragPart = child:WaitForChild("DragPart")
                        
                        local bodyPos = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                        bodyPos.MaxForce = Vector3.new(275000, 275000, 275000)
                        bodyPos.P = 20000
                        bodyPos.D = 950
                        bodyPos.Position = grabPart.Position
                        bodyPos.Parent = grabPart
                        
                        Config.PcDistance = (grabPart.Position - service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                        dragPart:WaitForChild("AlignPosition").Enabled = false
                        
                        task.spawn(function()
                            while child.Parent and Config.InfLineToggle do
                                bodyPos.Position = service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + (service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector * Config.PcDistance)
                                task.wait()
                            end
                            Config.PcDistance = 0
                            bodyPos:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end
                    toggleButtonState(true)
                end
            end)

            service.Workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == "GrabParts" and child:IsA("Model") then
                    toggleButtonState(false)
                end
            end)

            imageButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
                if not processed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    Config.IsTouching = true
                    task.spawn(startLongPress, "increase")
                end
            end)

            imageButtonDe.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
                if not processed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    Config.IsTouching = true
                    task.spawn(startLongPress, "decrease")
                end
            end)

            imageButton.InputEnded:Connect(endTouch)
            imageButtonDe.InputEnded:Connect(endTouch)

            service.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if not Config.InfLineToggle then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
                    if Config.PcDistance < 11 then Config.PcDistance = 11 end
                    local direction = input.Position.Z > 0 and 1 or -1
                    adjustDistance(direction * Config.LineDistance)
                end
            end)

        else
            local targetGui = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):FindFirstChild("ExtendControlGui")
            if targetGui then
                targetGui:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end,
})

Inlff:AddSlider({
    Name = "Line Distance",
    Min = Config.MinExtendLine,
    Max = Config.MaxExtendLine,
    Default = 3,
    Increment = 1,
    Callback = function(value)
        Config.LineDistance = value
    end,
})

line:AddToggle({
    Name = "Rainbow Line",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.ToggleState = Value

        if Value then
            if not Config.originalColors then
                if UpdateLineColorsEvent then
                    Config.originalColors = {}
                    for i = 1, 10 do
                        table.insert(Config.originalColors, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
                    end
                end
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                Config.hueOffset = 0
                while Config.ToggleState do
                    if UpdateLineColorsEvent then
                        Config.hueOffset = (Config.hueOffset + 0.01) % 1
                        
                        local cs = ColorSequence.new{
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   Color3.fromHSV((0   + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1, Color3.fromHSV((0.1 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, Color3.fromHSV((0.2 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, Color3.fromHSV((0.3 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, Color3.fromHSV((0.4 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromHSV((0.5 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, Color3.fromHSV((0.6 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, Color3.fromHSV((0.7 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, Color3.fromHSV((0.8 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.9, Color3.fromHSV((0.9 + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1)),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   Color3.fromHSV((1   + Config.hueOffset)%1,1,1))
                        }

                        UpdateLineColorsEvent:FireServer(
                            cs,
                            cs.Keypoints[1].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[2].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[3].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[4].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[5].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[6].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[7].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[8].Value,
                            cs.Keypoints[9].Value
                        )
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        else
            if Config.originalColors then
                pcall(function()
                    UpdateLineColorsEvent:FireServer(
                        Config.originalColors[1],
                        Config.originalColors[2],
                        Config.originalColors[3],
                        Config.originalColors[4],
                        Config.originalColors[5],
                        Config.originalColors[6],
                        Config.originalColors[7],
                        Config.originalColors[8],
                        Config.originalColors[9],
                        Config.originalColors[10]
                    )
                end)
            end
        end
    end
})

local AAGGG = TeleportTab:AddPlayersDropdown({
    Name = "Select Player",
    Default = "",
    Options = {""},
    Callback = function(selection)
        local namePart = string.match(selection, "ID:%s*([^%)]+)")
        if namePart then
            Config.PlayerToTeleport = namePart
        else
            Config.PlayerToTeleport = selection
        end
    end
})

TeleportTab:AddButton({
    Name = "Teleport",
    Callback = function()
        local target = service.Players:FindFirstChild(Config.PlayerToTeleport or "")
        if target and target.Character then
            local targetRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if targetRoot then
                local calculatedCFrame
                local direction = Config.TeleportOffsetDirection
                local offsetDistance = 4

                if direction == "Front" then
                    calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -offsetDistance)
                elseif direction == "Right" then
                    calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(offsetDistance, 0, 0)
                elseif direction == "Left" then
                    calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(-offsetDistance, 0, 0)
                elseif direction == "Head" then
                    calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)
                elseif direction == "Rotate" then
                    local cosX = math.clamp(math.cos(0), -1, 1)
                    local sinZ = math.clamp(math.sin(0), -1, 1)
                    calculatedCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetRoot.Position + Vector3.new(cosX, 0, sinZ) * offsetDistance, targetRoot.Position)
                else
                    calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, offsetDistance)
                end

                local localChar = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                if localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local myRoot = localChar.HumanoidRootPart
                    myRoot.CFrame = myRoot.CFrame.Rotation + calculatedCFrame.Position
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

TeleportTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Loop Teleport",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.LoopPlayerTP = state
        Config.RotateAngle = 0
        
        while Config.LoopPlayerTP do
            local target = service.Players:FindFirstChild(Config.PlayerToTeleport or "")
            if target and target.Character then
                local targetRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if targetRoot then
                    local calculatedCFrame
                    local direction = Config.TeleportOffsetDirection
                    local offsetDistance = 4

                    if direction == "Front" then
                        calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -offsetDistance)
                    elseif direction == "Right" then
                        calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(offsetDistance, 0, 0)
                    elseif direction == "Left" then
                        calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(-offsetDistance, 0, 0)
                    elseif direction == "Head" then
                        calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)
                    elseif direction == "Rotate" then
                        Config.RotateAngle = Config.RotateAngle + 0.2
                        local cosX = math.clamp(math.cos(Config.RotateAngle), -1, 1)
                        local sinZ = math.clamp(math.sin(Config.RotateAngle), -1, 1)
                        
                        calculatedCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetRoot.Position + Vector3.new(cosX, 0, sinZ) * offsetDistance, targetRoot.Position)
                    else
                        calculatedCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, offsetDistance)
                    end

                    local localChar = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                    if localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local myRoot = localChar.HumanoidRootPart
                        myRoot.CFrame = myRoot.CFrame.Rotation + calculatedCFrame.Position
                    end
                end
            else
                Config.LoopPlayerTP = false
                if PlayerLoopTeleport then PlayerLoopTeleport:Set(false) end
            end
            task.wait()
        end
    end
})

TeleportTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Lock Camera",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.LockCameraOnPlayer = state
        if not state then return end

        local connection
        connection = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not Config.LockCameraOnPlayer then
                connection:Disconnect()
                return
            end

            local target = service.Players:FindFirstChild(Config.PlayerToTeleport or "")
            if target and target.Character and Camera then
                local targetRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if targetRoot then
                    Camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Camera.CFrame.Position, targetRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
                end
            else
                Config.LockCameraOnPlayer = false
                if PlayerLockCamera then PlayerLockCamera:Set(false) end
            end
        end)
    end
})

TeleportTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "View",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.ViewCameraOnPlayer = state
        local originalSubject = Camera.CameraSubject

        if state then
            while Config.ViewCameraOnPlayer do
                local target = service.Players:FindFirstChild(Config.PlayerToTeleport or "")
                if target and target.Character then
                    if Config.CameraTargetPart == "Humanoid" then
                        local humanoid = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if humanoid then Camera.CameraSubject = humanoid end
                    else
                        local targetPart = target.Character:FindFirstChild(Config.CameraTargetPart)
                        if targetPart and targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                            Camera.CameraSubject = targetPart
                        else
                            local humanoid = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if humanoid then Camera.CameraSubject = humanoid end
                        end
                    end
                else
                    Config.ViewCameraOnPlayer = false
                    if PlayerViewCamera then PlayerViewCamera:Set(false) end
                end
                task.wait()
            end
            Camera.CameraSubject = originalSubject
        end
    end
})
