NotifyTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Kick (BlackHole)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            Config.KickCor = service.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if obj.Name == "BlackHoleKick" then
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    local pos = nil
                    
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pos = obj.Position
                    elseif obj:IsA("Model") and obj.PrimaryPart then
                        pos = obj.PrimaryPart.Position
                    elseif obj:IsA("Model") then
                        pos = obj:GetPivot().Position
                    end

                    if not pos then return end

                    local closestPlr = nil
                    local closestDist = math.huge

                    for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if plr ~= localPlayer and plr.Character then
                            local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if hrp then
                                local dist = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
                                if dist < closestDist then
                                    closestDist = dist
                                    closestPlr = plr
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if closestPlr then
                        local message = closestPlr.DisplayName .. " (" .. closestPlr.Name .. ") has been kicked."
                        FrostNotify(message)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if Config.KickCor then
                Config.KickCor:Disconnect()
                Config.KickCor = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

NotifyTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Kick",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.antikickNotifyToggle = Value
        if Value then
            if Config.antikickConnection then Config.antikickConnection:Disconnect() end
            Config.antikickConnection = service.Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(v)
                if not Config.antikickNotifyToggle then return end
                if v.Name == "NinjaKunai" or v.Name == "NinjaShuriken" then 
                    task.wait() 
                    if not v.Parent then return end
                    local name = v.Parent.Name:gsub("SpawnedInToys$", "")
                    local plr = service.Players:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if plr and plr ~= service.Players.LocalPlayer and plr ~= Config.lasninja then  
                        Config.lasninja = plr
                        FrostNotify("[Anti-Kick]: " .. plr.Name .. " (" .. plr.DisplayName .. ")")
                        task.delay(5, function()
                            if Config.lasninja == plr then
                                Config.lasninja = nil
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if Config.antikickConnection then
                Config.antikickConnection:Disconnect()
                Config.antikickConnection = nil
            end
            Config.lasninja = nil
        end
    end
})

    Name = "Click TP (Z)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.ClickTPfutuu = Value

        if Config.TPZcon then Config.TPZcon:Disconnect(); Config.TPZcon = nil end
        if Config.TPZconall then Config.TPZconall:Disconnect(); Config.TPZconall = nil end
        if Config.TPZholdCn then Config.TPZholdCn:Disconnect(); Config.TPZholdCn = nil end
        if Config.MobileTPDownCon then Config.MobileTPDownCon:Disconnect(); Config.MobileTPDownCon = nil end
        if Config.MobileTPUpCon then Config.MobileTPUpCon:Disconnect(); Config.MobileTPUpCon = nil end
        if Config.MobileTPLeaveCon then Config.MobileTPLeaveCon:Disconnect(); Config.MobileTPLeaveCon = nil end
        if Config.MobileTPClickCon then Config.MobileTPClickCon:Disconnect(); Config.MobileTPClickCon = nil end

            Config.TeleportGuiInstance:Destroy()
            Config.TeleportGuiInstance = nil
        end
        Config.holdtoggletp = false

            tpbutton.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            tpbutton.BorderSizePixel = 0

            tpbutton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 70)
            tpbutton.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"

            tpbutton.Visible = false
            tpbutton.Active = false

            tpLabel.Parent = tpbutton
            tpLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            tpLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1.000
            tpLabel.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

            tpLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

            if isMobile then
                tpbutton.Visible = true
                tpbutton.Active = true
                
                Config.MobileTPDownCon = tpbutton.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                    if Config.TPMode == "ClickTP (Hold)" and Config.ClickTPfutuu then
                        Config.holdtoggletp = true
                        if not Config.TPZholdCn then
                            Config.TPZholdCn = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                                if Config.holdtoggletp and Config.ClickTPfutuu then
                                    local CharacterTP = Config.TPZContro or localPlayer.Character
                                    if CharacterTP then
                                        local originPartName = Config.TPZContro and "Head" or (localPlayer.Character and "CamPart" or nil)
                                        if originPartName and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild(originPartName) then
                                            local hitPart, hitPosition = service.Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(
                                                Ray.new(CharacterTP[originPartName].Position, localPlayer.Character.CamPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5000),
                                                { CharacterTP }
                                            )
                                            if hitPart and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                                CharacterTP.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(hitPosition.X, hitPosition.Y + 3, hitPosition.Z)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    if Config.TPZholdCn then Config.TPZholdCn:Disconnect(); Config.TPZholdCn = nil end
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
                
                Config.MobileTPUpCon = tpbutton.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
                    if Config.TPMode == "ClickTP (Hold)" then
                        Config.holdtoggletp = false
                        if Config.TPZholdCn then Config.TPZholdCn:Disconnect(); Config.TPZholdCn = nil end
                    end
                end)
                
                Config.MobileTPLeaveCon = tpbutton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    if Config.TPMode == "ClickTP (Hold)" then
                        Config.holdtoggletp = false
                        if Config.TPZholdCn then Config.TPZholdCn:Disconnect(); Config.TPZholdCn = nil end
                    end
                end)
                
                Config.MobileTPClickCon = tpbutton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    if Config.TPMode == "ClickTP" and Config.ClickTPfutuu then
                        local CharacterTP = Config.TPZContro or localPlayer.Character
                        if CharacterTP then
                            local originPartName = Config.TPZContro and "Head" or (localPlayer.Character and "CamPart" or nil)
                            if originPartName and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild(originPartName) then
                                local hitPart, hitPosition = service.Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(
                                    Ray.new(CharacterTP[originPartName].Position, localPlayer.Character.CamPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5000),
                                    { CharacterTP }
                                )
                                if hitPart and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                    CharacterTP.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(hitPosition.X, hitPosition.Y + 3, hitPosition.Z)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                
            else
                tpbutton.Visible = false
                tpbutton.Active = false
                
                Config.TPZcon = service.UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                    if gameProcessed then return end
                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z and Config.ClickTPfutuu then
                        if Config.TPMode == "ClickTP" then
                            local CharacterTP = Config.TPZContro or localPlayer.Character
                            if CharacterTP then
                                local originPartName = Config.TPZContro and "Head" or (localPlayer.Character and "CamPart" or nil)
                                if originPartName and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild(originPartName) then
                                    local hitPart, hitPosition = service.Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(
                                        Ray.new(CharacterTP[originPartName].Position, localPlayer.Character.CamPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5000),
                                        { CharacterTP }
                                    )
                                    if hitPart and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                        CharacterTP.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(hitPosition.X, hitPosition.Y + 3, hitPosition.Z)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        elseif Config.TPMode == "ClickTP (Hold)" then
                            Config.holdtoggletp = true
                            if not Config.TPZholdCn then
                                Config.TPZholdCn = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                                    if Config.holdtoggletp and Config.ClickTPfutuu then
                                        local CharacterTP = Config.TPZContro or localPlayer.Character
                                        if CharacterTP then
                                            local originPartName = Config.TPZContro and "Head" or (localPlayer.Character and "CamPart" or nil)
                                            if originPartName and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild(originPartName) then
                                                local hitPart, hitPosition = service.Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(
                                                    Ray.new(CharacterTP[originPartName].Position, localPlayer.Character.CamPart.CFrame.lookVector * 5000),
                                                    { CharacterTP }
                                                )
                                                if hitPart and CharacterTP:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                                    CharacterTP.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(hitPosition.X, hitPosition.Y + 3, hitPosition.Z)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    else
                                        if Config.TPZholdCn then Config.TPZholdCn:Disconnect(); Config.TPZholdCn = nil end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                
                Config.TPZconall = service.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z then
                        Config.holdtoggletp = false
                        if Config.TPZholdCn then
                            Config.TPZholdCn:Disconnect()
                            Config.TPZholdCn = nil
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end    
})

ancc:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anchor (K)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(ancval)
        if Config.AnchorGuiInstance then
            Config.AnchorGuiInstance:Destroy()
            Config.AnchorGuiInstance = nil
        end
        if Config.anchorConn then
            Config.anchorConn:Disconnect()
            Config.anchorConn = nil
        end

        if ancval then
            local Atchmet = Instance.new("Attachment")
            local function setanchorObject(TPARTY)
                local NilModel = Instance.new("Model", service.Workspace)

                local function anchorobjecteffect(an7)
                    local kurosee = Atchmet:Clone()
                    kurosee.Parent = an7
                    service.Debris:AddItem(kurosee)
                end

                local function unAnchorObject(an9)
                    if typeof(an9) == "Instance" and an9.Parent and (an9.Parent:IsA("Model") or an9.Parent:IsA("Folder")) then
                        local an8 = an9.Parent
                        local Isanh = an8:GetAttribute("IsAnchored")
                        local GluePri = an8:GetAttribute("GluePrimary")
                        if not an8:IsA("Folder") and an8 ~= service.Workspace then
                            an9 = an8
                        end
                        if Config.Anchorparts[an9] and Isanh then
                            local an10 = Config.Anchorparts[an9]
                            an10.BodyPosition.Parent = an9
                            an10.BodyGyro.Parent = an9
                            if not GluePri then
                                    an10.HighLightAnchor.Adornee = NilModel
                            end
                            an10.PartAnchored = nil
                            local an14, an13, an12 = pairs(an10.Connections)
                            while true do
                                local an11
                                an12, an11 = an14(an13, an12)
                                if an12 == nil then
                                    break
                                end
                                an11:Disconnect()
                            end
                            an10.Connections = nil
                            an9:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", false)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if typeof(TPARTY) == "Instance" and TPARTY.Parent and (TPARTY.Parent:IsA("Model") or TPARTY.Parent:IsA("Folder")) then
                    local rootObject = TPARTY.Parent
                    if rootObject:IsA("Folder") or rootObject == service.Workspace then
                        rootObject = TPARTY
                    end
                    local isAnchoredAttr = rootObject:GetAttribute("IsAnchored")
                    local isGlueAttr = rootObject:GetAttribute("Glue")
                    if isGlueAttr then
                        isGlueAttr = not rootObject:GetAttribute("GluePrimary")
                    end
                    if isAnchoredAttr or isGlueAttr then
                        unAnchorObject(TPARTY)
                    else
                        local bodyPosition =
                            rootObject:FindFirstChild("AnchorPositionBody") or
                            (TPARTY:FindFirstChild("AnchorPositionBody") or Instance.new("BodyPosition"))
                        local bodyGyro =
                            rootObject:FindFirstChild("AnchorGyroBody") or
                            (TPARTY:FindFirstChild("AnchorGyroBody") or Instance.new("BodyGyro"))
                        local highlight = rootObject:FindFirstChild("AnchorHighlight") or Instance.new("Highlight", rootObject)
                        local connections = {}
                        local maxForceVector = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        local zeroVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        local initialPosition = TPARTY.Position
                        local partOwnerInstance = nil
                        local colorGreen = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                        local colorYellow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 58)
                        local colorOrange = Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 58)
                        local colorNavy = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                        bodyPosition.Name = "AnchorPositionBody"
                        bodyPosition.Position = TPARTY.Position
                        bodyPosition.Parent = TPARTY
                        bodyGyro.Name = "AnchorGyroBody"
                        bodyGyro.Parent = TPARTY
                        bodyGyro.CFrame = TPARTY.CFrame
                        bodyGyro.D = 950
                        bodyGyro.P = 40000
                        bodyPosition.P = 40000
                        bodyPosition.D = 950
                        highlight.Name = "AnchorHighlight"
                        highlight.Parent = rootObject
                        highlight.Adornee = rootObject
                        highlight.FillTransparency = 0.7
                        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
                        highlight.Enabled = true
                        local function updateVisuals()
                            if rootObject:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") or rootObject:GetAttribute("Glue") then
                                bodyGyro.MaxTorque = maxForceVector
                                bodyPosition.MaxForce = maxForceVector
                            end
                            if rootObject:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") or rootObject:GetAttribute("GluePrimary") then
                                highlight.FillColor = colorGreen
                                highlight.OutlineColor = colorGreen
                            elseif rootObject:GetAttribute("Glue") then
                                highlight.OutlineColor = colorYellow
                                highlight.FillColor = colorOrange
                            else
                                highlight.FillColor = colorNavy
                                highlight.OutlineColor = colorNavy
                            end
                        end
                        local function resetVisualsAndOwnership()
                            bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new()
                            bodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new()
                            highlight.FillColor = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                            highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                            rootObject:SetAttribute("AnchorOwnership", false)
                        end
                        connections[1] =
                            rootObject.DescendantAdded:Connect(
                            function(descendant)
                                if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" then
                                    if descendant.Value ~= localPlayer.Name then
                                        resetVisualsAndOwnership()
                                    else
                                        partOwnerInstance = descendant
                                        updateVisuals()
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        )
                        connections[2] =
                            rootObject.DescendantRemoving:Connect(
                            function(descendant)
                                if descendant.Name == "PartOwner" and (descendant.Value == localPlayer.Name and descendant.Value == localPlayer.Name) then
                                    partOwnerInstance = nil
                                    updateVisuals()
                                end
                            end
                        )
                        task.spawn(
                            function()
                                while bodyPosition.Parent and not rootObject:GetAttribute("Glue") do
                                    if rootObject:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
                                        bodyGyro.MaxTorque = maxForceVector
                                        bodyPosition.MaxForce = maxForceVector
                                    else
                                        bodyGyro.MaxTorque = zeroVector
                                        bodyPosition.MaxForce = zeroVector
                                    end
                                    bodyPosition.Position = initialPosition + Vector3.new(0, 0.001, 0)
                                    task.wait()
                                    bodyPosition.Position = initialPosition
                                end
                            end
                        )
                        local anchordeta = {
                            BodyPosition = bodyPosition,
                            BodyGyro = bodyGyro,
                            PartAnchored = TPARTY,
                            HighLightAnchor = highlight,
                            Connections = connections,
                            Model = rootObject
                        }
                        Config.Anchorparts[rootObject] = anchordeta
                        anchorobjecteffect(TPARTY)
                        rootObject:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", true)
                        updateVisuals()
                    end
                end
            end

            local gui2 = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            gui2.ResetOnSpawn = false
            gui2.Name = "CAG2"
            if service.UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
                gui2.Parent = localPlayer.PlayerGui
            end
            
            local imageButtonAnchor = Instance.new("ImageButton")
            imageButtonAnchor.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50)
            imageButtonAnchor.Position = UDim2.new(1, -325, 1, -80)
            imageButtonAnchor.Image = "rbxassetid://97166444"
            imageButtonAnchor.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageButtonAnchor.ImageTransparency = 0.2
            imageButtonAnchor.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 142)
            imageButtonAnchor.Parent = gui2
            
            local imageKLabelDe = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            imageKLabelDe.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            imageKLabelDe.Image = "rbxassetid://3040311268"
            imageKLabelDe.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            imageKLabelDe.Parent = imageButtonAnchor

            Config.AnchorGuiInstance = gui2

            imageButtonAnchor.Visible = true
            imageButtonAnchor.Active = true
            
            service.ContextActionService:BindAction("AnchorK", function(untii, eumuu, _)
                if untii == "AnchorK" and eumuu == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
                    local GBPT = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
                    if GBPT then
                        local guraankaa = GBPT.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1
                        if guraankaa and not (guraankaa:IsDescendantOf(service.Workspace.Map) or guraankaa.Anchored) then
                            setanchorObject(guraankaa)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end, false, Enum.KeyCode.K)

            Config.anchorConn = imageButtonAnchor.InputBegan:Connect(function(an5, an6)
                if not an6 and (service.UserInputService.TouchEnabled and an5.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    local GBPT = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
                    if GBPT then
                        local guraankaa = GBPT.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1
                        if guraankaa and not (guraankaa:IsDescendantOf(service.Workspace.Map) or guraankaa.Anchored) then
                            setanchorObject(guraankaa)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            service.ContextActionService:UnbindAction("AnchorK")
        end
    end,
})

ancc:AddButton({
    Name = "Compile Parts",
    Callback = function()
        local chatgptwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwww___ = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("GrabParts")
        local ugokasu = nil
        if chatgptwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwww___ then
            local ewewewew = chatgptwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwwww___.GrabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1
            if ewewewew then
                for _, anchordeta in pairs(Config.Anchorparts) do
                    local model = anchordeta.Model
                    if model and ewewewew:IsDescendantOf(model) then
                        ugokasu = model
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local sex = ugokasu

        local function aireadycompiled(mdoel)
            if not mdoel then return false end
            for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(Config.Compiledugokasuyo) do
                if compiledGroup.primaryPart == mdoel then
                    return true
                end
                if compiledGroup.group then
                    for _, groupData in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
                        if groupData.model == mdoel then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        if aireadycompiled(sex) then
            FrostNotify("This toy is already compiled!")
            return
        end

        local sex2 = 0
        local se3x = {}
        
        local function isInCompileGroup(sex4)
            for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(Config.Compiledugokasuyo) do
                if compiledGroup.primaryPart and compiledGroup.group then
                    for _, groupData in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
                        local sex14 = groupData.model
                        if sex14 and (sex14 == sex4 and (sex14:GetAttribute("Glue") or sex14:GetAttribute("GluePrimary"))) and not sex14:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        for _, anchordeta in pairs(Config.Anchorparts) do
            local model = anchordeta.Model
            if model and not isInCompileGroup(model) and not aireadycompiled(model) then
                sex2 = sex2 + 1
            end
        end
        
        if sex2 == 0 then
            FrostNotify("No anchored parts found")
            return
        elseif sex2 == 1 then
            FrostNotify("Needs at least 2 anchored objects")
            return
        else
            if sex then
                FrostNotify("Compiled " .. sex2 .. " Toys together")
                for _, anchordeta in pairs(Config.Anchorparts) do
                    local model = anchordeta.Model
                    if model and not isInCompileGroup(model) then
                        local sex15 = false
                        for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(Config.Compiledugokasuyo) do
                            if compiledGroup.primaryPart and compiledGroup.primaryPart == model and compiledGroup.primaryPart:GetAttribute("IsAnchored") then
                                sex15 = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        
                        if sex15 then
                            for i, compiledGroup in ipairs(Config.Compiledugokasuyo) do
                                if compiledGroup.primaryPart and compiledGroup.primaryPart == model then
                                    for _, constraint in pairs(compiledGroup.Nc_Group) do
                                        constraint:Destroy()
                                    end
                                    for _, connection in pairs(compiledGroup.gC) do
                                        connection:Disconnect()
                                    end
                                    for _, groupData in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
                                        local groupModel = groupData.model
                                        groupModel:SetAttribute("Glue", false)
                                        groupModel:SetAttribute("GluePrimary", false)
                                        groupModel:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", false)
                                    end
                                    table.remove(Config.Compiledugokasuyo, i)
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                local sex16 = {}
                for _, anchordeta in pairs(Config.Anchorparts) do
                    local sex17 = anchordeta.Model
                    local sex18 = anchordeta.BodyPosition
                    local sex19 = anchordeta.BodyGyro
                    
                    if sex17 and not isInCompileGroup(sex17) and not aireadycompiled(sex17) then
                        local sex20 = anchordeta.PartAnchored
                        if sex20 then
                            local sex21 = sex.PrimaryPart.CFrame:toObjectSpace(sex20.CFrame)
                            sex17:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", false)

                            if sex17 == sex then
                                sex19.MaxTorque = Vector3.new()
                                sex18.MaxForce = Vector3.new()
                                sex17:SetAttribute("GluePrimary", true)
                                anchordeta.HighLightAnchor.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                                anchordeta.HighLightAnchor.FillColor = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                            else
                                sex17:SetAttribute("Glue", true)
                                anchordeta.HighLightAnchor.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 58)
                                anchordeta.HighLightAnchor.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 58)
                            end
                            table.insert(
                                sex16,
                                {
                                    model = sex17,
                                    part = sex20,
                                    offset = sex21,
                                    bodypos = sex18,
                                    bodygyro = sex19
                                }
                            )
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                table.insert(
                    Config.Compiledugokasuyo,
                    {
                        primaryPart = sex,
                        group = sex16,
                        Nc_Group = {},
                        gC = se3x
                    }
                )
                local function Compiledugokasu3(sex22)
                    for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(Config.Compiledugokasuyo) do
                        if compiledGroup.primaryPart and compiledGroup.primaryPart == sex22 then
                            for _, groupData in ipairs(compiledGroup.group) do
                                if groupData.model ~= sex22 then
                                    local sex31 = groupData.bodypos
                                    local sex32 = groupData.bodygyro
                                    local sex33 = sex22.PrimaryPart or sex22:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
                                    if sex33 and sex22 then
                                        if sex31 then
                                            sex31.P = 40000
                                            sex31.D = 200
                                            sex31.Position = (sex33.CFrame * groupData.offset).Position
                                            task.wait()
                                            sex31.Position = sex31.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.002, 0)
                                        end
                                        if sex32 then
                                            sex32.P = 40000
                                            sex32.D = 200
                                            sex32.CFrame = sex33.CFrame * groupData.offset
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                for _, compiledGroup in ipairs(Config.Compiledugokasuyo) do
                    if compiledGroup.primaryPart and compiledGroup.primaryPart == sex then
                        for _, groupData1 in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
                            local sex42 = groupData1.model
                            if sex42 == sex then
                                for _, sex46 in ipairs(sex42:GetChildren()) do
                                    if sex46:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        for _, groupData2 in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
                                            local sex51 = groupData2.model
                                            for _, sex55 in ipairs(sex51:GetChildren()) do
                                                if sex55:IsA("BasePart") then
                                                    local sex56 = Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint", sex46)
                                                    sex56.Part0 = sex46
                                                    sex56.Part1 = sex55
                                                    sex56.Enabled = true
                                                    table.insert(compiledGroup.Nc_Group, sex56)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                local sex57 = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(
                    function()
                        Compiledugokasu3(sex)
                    end
                )
                table.insert(se3x, sex57)
            else
                FrostNotify("You need to hold one of your anchored object")
            end
        end
    end
})

ancc:AddButton({
    Name = "Uncompile Parts",
    Callback = function()
        if #Config.Compiledugokasuyo == 0 then
            FrostNotify("No compiled Toys found")
            return
        end
        
        for i, compiledGroup in ipairs(Config.Compiledugokasuyo) do
            for _, constraint in pairs(compiledGroup.Nc_Group) do
                if constraint and constraint.Parent then
                    constraint:Destroy()
                end
            end
            
            for _, connection in pairs(compiledGroup.gC) do
                if connection and connection.Connected then
                    connection:Disconnect()
                end
            end
            
            for _, groupData in pairs(compiledGroup.group) do
                local groupModel = groupData.model
                if groupModel then
                    groupModel:SetAttribute("Glue", false)
                    groupModel:SetAttribute("GluePrimary", false)
                    groupModel:SetAttribute("IsAnchored", false)
					groupModel:SetAttribute("IsCompiled", false)
                    if groupData.bodypos then
                        groupData.bodypos.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        groupData.bodypos.P = 0
                        groupData.bodypos.D = 0
                        groupData.bodypos:Destroy()
                    end
                    if groupData.bodygyro then
                        groupData.bodygyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        groupData.bodygyro.P = 0
                        groupData.bodygyro.D = 0
                        groupData.bodygyro:Destroy()
                    end
                    
                    for _, part in pairs(groupModel:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            part.Anchored = false
                        end
                    end
                    
                    for _, anchordeta in pairs(Config.Anchorparts) do
                        if anchordeta.Model == groupModel then
                            if anchordeta.HighLightAnchor then
                                anchordeta.HighLightAnchor.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                anchordeta.HighLightAnchor.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                anchordeta.HighLightAnchor.Enabled = false
                            end
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        Config.Compiledugokasuyo = {}
        FrostNotify("Uncompiled all Toys")
    end
})

sppalen:AddToggle({
    Name = "Spawn Pallet (Tab)",

    Callback = function(valuee)

            Config.palletConn:Disconnect()

        end
        service.ContextActionService:UnbindAction("Spawn Pallet (TAB)")

        if valuee then
            local function spawnPallet()
                local spawnObjectd = {
                    "PalletLightBrown",

                palletlabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1.000

                service.ContextActionService:BindAction("Spawn Pallet (TAB)", function(palspawn, palspawn2, _)
                    if palspawn == "Spawn Pallet (TAB)" and palspawn2 == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
                        spawnPallet()
                    end
                end, false, Enum.KeyCode.Tab)
                
            end
        end
    end,

    Name = "Track mode",
    Default = "Players",
    Options = {"Players", "Freeze"},
    Callback = function(v)
        Config.TrackMode = v
        if v == "Freeze" then
            local Player = service.Players:FindFirstChild(Config.PlayerobjName) or localPlayer
            local char = Player.Character
            if char then
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                if hrp then
                    Config.FreezePosition = hrp.Position
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

TEkitou:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Objects mode",
    Default = "Rings",
    Options = {"Rings", "Triangle", "Square", "Heart", "Star", "Chaos Cyclone"},
    Callback = function(v)
        Config.CurrentMode = v
    end
})

TEkitou:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Mode",
    Default = "Horizontal",
    Options = {"Horizontal", "Vertical"},
    Callback = function(v)
        Config.SharedMode = v
    end
})

TEkitou:AddToggle({
    Name = "Objects",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        Config.ActiveToggle = v
        if not v then
            if Config.ActiveConn then
                Config.ActiveConn:Disconnect()
                Config.ActiveConn = nil
            end
            
            for _, val in ipairs(Config.ActiveList) do
                local p = val.part
                if p and p.Parent then
                    p.CanCollide = true
                    p.CanTouch = true
                    p.CanQuery = true
                    p.Massless = false
                    if p.Material == Enum.Material.Neon then
                        p.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
                    end
                end
            end

            if Config.HeartPoints then
                for _, point in ipairs(Config.HeartPoints) do
                    if point.part then point.part:Destroy() end
                    if point.assigned then
                        if point.assigned.BG then point.assigned.BG:Destroy() end
                        if point.assigned.BP then point.assigned.BP:Destroy() end
                        if point.assigned.Root then
                            point.assigned.Root.CanCollide = true
                            point.assigned.Root.CanTouch = true
                            point.assigned.Root.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            
            Config.HeartPoints = {}
            Config.HeartAssigned = {}
            Config.ActiveList = {}
            return
        end

        local function createStarPoints()
            local outer = Config.StarSize or 15
            local inner = outer * 0.45
            local points = {}
            for i = 0, 9 do
                local angle = math.rad((i * 36) - 125)
                local radius = (i % 2 == 1) and inner or outer
                local hx = math.cos(angle) * radius
                local hz = math.sin(angle) * radius

                if Config.SharedMode == "Horizontal" then
                    table.insert(points, Vector3.new(hx, Config.StarHeight or 0, hz))
                else
                    table.insert(points, Vector3.new(hx, (Config.StarHeight or 0) + hz, 0))
                end
            end
            return points
        end

        local function lerp(a, b, progress)
            return a + (b - a) * progress
        end

        local function getStarPosition(index, total, time, hrp)
            local points = createStarPoints()
            local progress = ((index / total) + (time * (Config.StarSpeed or 5) * 0.1)) % 1
            local segmentCount = #points
            local segment = math.floor(progress * segmentCount) + 1
            if segment > segmentCount then segment = 1 end

            local nextSegment = segment + 1
            if nextSegment > segmentCount then nextSegment = 1 end

            local segmentProgress = (progress * segmentCount) % 1
            local p1 = points[segment]
            local p2 = points[nextSegment]
            local pos = lerp(p1, p2, segmentProgress)

            local hrpAngle = math.atan2(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
            local rotatedPos = CFrame.Angles(0, hrpAngle, 0) * pos
            return hrp.Position + rotatedPos
        end

        Config.ActiveList = {}
        
        local limit = (Config.CurrentMode == "Chaos Cyclone") and 99999 or (Config.obuzilekutoCount or 50)
        
        local toys = {}
        for _, item in ipairs(service.Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if #toys >= limit then break end
            if item:IsA("Model") and item.Name == "FireworkSparkler" then
                local alreadyAdded = false
                for _, existingToy in ipairs(toys) do 
                    if existingToy == item then alreadyAdded = true; break end 
                end
                if not alreadyAdded then table.insert(toys, item) end
            end
        end
        table.sort(toys, function(a, b) return a.Name < b.Name end)

        for _, d in ipairs(toys) do
            local part = d.PrimaryPart or d:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if not part then
                local potentialParts = {"Handle", "Main", "Part", "Base", "Sparkler", "Firework"}
                for _, partName in ipairs(potentialParts) do
                    local found = d:FindFirstChild(partName)
                    if found and found:IsA("BasePart") then part = found; break end
                end
                if not part then
                    for _, child in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do 
                        if child:IsA("BasePart") then part = child; break end 
                    end
                end
            end

            if part then
                part.CanCollide = false
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.CanQuery = false
                part.Massless = true

                pcall(function()
                    part:SetNetworkOwner(localPlayer)
                end)

                table.insert(Config.ActiveList, {
                    model = d,
                    part = part
                })
            end
        end

        if Config.CurrentMode == "Heart" or Config.CurrentMode == "Chaos Cyclone" then
            Config.HeartToys = Config.ActiveList
            Config.HeartPoints = {}
            local ptsCount = #Config.HeartToys
            
            if ptsCount > 0 then
                for i = 1, ptsCount do
                    local angle = (i - 1) * (2 * math.pi / ptsCount)
                    local p = Instance.new("Part")
                    p.CanCollide = false
                    p.Anchored = true
                    p.Transparency = 1
                    p.Size = Vector3.new(4, 1, 4)
                    p.Parent = workspace
                    Config.HeartPoints[i] = {angle = angle, part = p, assigned = nil}
                end

                Config.HeartAssigned = {}
                for i = 1, math.min(#Config.HeartToys, #Config.HeartPoints) do
                    local toy = Config.HeartToys[i].model
                    local root = Config.HeartToys[i].part
                    if root then
                        for _, child in ipairs(toy:GetChildren()) do
                            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                                child.CanCollide = false
                                child.CanTouch = false
                                child.Anchored = false
                                if Config.CurrentMode == "Chaos Cyclone" then
                                    child.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        for _, c in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                            if c:IsA("BodyPosition") or c:IsA("BodyGyro") then c:Destroy() end
                        end

                        local BP = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                        local BG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                        BP.P = 30000
                        BP.D = 500
                        BP.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 1e10
                        BP.Parent = root
                        BG.P = 30000
                        BG.D = 500
                        BG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1) * 1e10
                        BG.Parent = root

                        local data = {
                            BG = BG,
                            BP = BP,
                            Root = root,
                            Model = toy,
                            baseAngle = Config.HeartPoints[i].angle
                        }
                        Config.HeartPoints[i].assigned = data
                        table.insert(Config.HeartAssigned, data)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if Config.ActiveConn then
            Config.ActiveConn:Disconnect()
        end

        Config.HeartTime = 0
        local t = 0

        Config.ActiveConn = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            t += dt
            Config.HeartTime += dt

            local Player = service.Players:FindFirstChild(Config.PlayerobjName) or localPlayer
            local char = Player.Character
            if not char then return end

            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            if not hrp then return end

            local basePos
            if Config.TrackMode == "Freeze" then
                basePos = Config.FreezePosition or hrp.Position
            else
                basePos = hrp.Position
            end

            local hrpAngle = math.atan2(hrp.CFrame.LookVector.X, hrp.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
            local rotationCFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, hrpAngle, 0)

            if Config.CurrentMode == "Rings" then
                for i, val in ipairs(Config.ActiveList) do
                    local p = val.part
                    if p and p.Parent then
                        local angle = ((math.pi * 2) / #Config.ActiveList) * i + (t * (Config.RingSpeed or 5))
                        local targetPos

                        if Config.SharedMode == "Horizontal" then
                            local x = math.cos(angle) * (Config.RingRadius or 15)
                            local z = math.sin(angle) * (Config.RingRadius or 15)
                            local offset = Vector3.new(x, Config.RingHeight or 0, z)
                            targetPos = basePos + rotationCFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(offset)
                        else
                            local x = math.cos(angle) * (Config.RingRadius or 15)
                            local y = math.sin(angle) * (Config.RingRadius or 15) + (Config.RingHeight or 0)
                            local offset = Vector3.new(x, y, 0)
                            targetPos = basePos + rotationCFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(offset)
                        end

                        p.CanCollide = false
                        p.CanTouch = false
                        p.CanQuery = false
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos, basePos)
                    end
                end
            elseif Config.CurrentMode == "Triangle" then
                local size = Config.TriangleSize or 15
                local height = Config.TriangleHeight or 0
                local p1, p2, p3
                if Config.SharedMode == "Horizontal" then
                    p1 = Vector3.new(0, height, size)
                    p2 = Vector3.new(-size, height, -size)
                    p3 = Vector3.new(size, height, -size)
                else
                    p1 = Vector3.new(0, height + size, 0)
                    p2 = Vector3.new(-size, height - size, 0)
                    p3 = Vector3.new(size, height - size, 0)
                end

                for i, val in ipairs(Config.ActiveList) do
                    local p = val.part
                    if p and p.Parent then
                        local progress = ((i / #Config.ActiveList) + (t * (Config.TriangleSpeed or 5) * 0.1)) % 1
                        local sideProgress, sidePos

                        if progress < 0.333 then
                            sideProgress = progress / 0.333
                            sidePos = p1 + (p2 - p1) * sideProgress
                        elseif progress < 0.666 then
                            sideProgress = (progress - 0.333) / 0.333
                            sidePos = p2 + (p3 - p2) * sideProgress
                        else
                            sideProgress = (progress - 0.666) / 0.334
                            sidePos = p3 + (p1 - p3) * sideProgress
                        end

                        local targetPos = basePos + rotationCFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(sidePos)
                        p.CanCollide = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CanQuery = false
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos, basePos)
                    end
                end
            elseif Config.CurrentMode == "Square" then
                local size = Config.SquareSize or 15
                local height = Config.SquareHeight or 0
                local p1, p2, p3, p4
                if Config.SharedMode == "Horizontal" then
                    p1 = Vector3.new(-size, height, size)
                    p2 = Vector3.new(size, height, size)
                    p3 = Vector3.new(size, height, -size)
                    p4 = Vector3.new(-size, height, -size)
                else
                    p1 = Vector3.new(-size, height + size, 0)
                    p2 = Vector3.new(size, height + size, 0)
                    p3 = Vector3.new(size, height - size, 0)
                    p4 = Vector3.new(-size, height - size, 0)
                end

                for i, val in ipairs(Config.ActiveList) do
                    local p = val.part
                    if p and p.Parent then
                        local progress = ((i / #Config.ActiveList) + (t * (Config.SquareSpeed or 5) * 0.1)) % 1
                        local sideProgress, sidePos

                        if progress < 0.25 then
                            sideProgress = progress / 0.25
                            sidePos = p1 + (p2 - p1) * sideProgress
                        elseif progress < 0.5 then
                            sideProgress = (progress - 0.25) / 0.25
                            sidePos = p2 + (p3 - p2) * sideProgress
                        elseif progress < 0.75 then
                            sideProgress = (progress - 0.5) / 0.25
                            sidePos = p3 + (p4 - p3) * sideProgress
                        else
                            sideProgress = (progress - 0.75) / 0.25
                            sidePos = p4 + (p1 - p4) * sideProgress
                        end

                        local targetPos = basePos + rotationCFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(sidePos)
                        p.CanCollide = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CanQuery = false
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos, basePos)
                    end
                end
            elseif Config.CurrentMode == "Heart" then
                local pulse = math.sin(Config.HeartTime * (Config.HeartPulseSpeed or 5)) * (Config.HeartPulseAmplitude or 10)

                for _, point in ipairs(Config.HeartPoints) do
                    local toy = point.assigned
                    if toy and toy.BP and toy.BG then
                        local ang = toy.baseAngle + (Config.HeartTime * (Config.HeartRotationSpeed or 20))
                        local s = ((Config.HeartSize or 15) / 20) * (Config.HeartScale or 1)
                        local hx = 16 * (math.sin(ang) ^ 3) * s
                        local hy = (13 * math.cos(ang) - 5 * math.cos(2*ang) - 2 * math.cos(3*ang) - math.cos(4*ang)) * s
                        
                        if (Config.HeartVerticalStretch or 1) > 1 then hy *= Config.HeartVerticalStretch end
                        if pulse > 0 then
                            local pf = 1 + pulse * 0.1
                            hx *= pf
                            hy *= pf
                        end

                        local h = (Config.HeartHeight or 10) + math.sin(ang * 2) * 1
                        local offset
                        if Config.SharedMode == "Horizontal" then
                            offset = Vector3.new(hx, h, hy)
                        else
                            offset = Vector3.new(hx, h + hy, 0)
                        end
                        local target = basePos + rotationCFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(offset)

                        toy.BP.Position = target
                        toy.BG.CFrame = CFrame.new(target, basePos)
                    end
                end
            elseif Config.CurrentMode == "Star" then
                for i, v in ipairs(Config.ActiveList) do
                    local p = v.part
                    if p and p.Parent then
                        local targetPos = getStarPosition(i, #Config.ActiveList, t, hrp)
                        if Config.TrackMode == "Freeze" then
                            local diff = targetPos - hrp.Position
                            targetPos = (Config.FreezePosition or hrp.Position) + diff
                        end

                        p.CanCollide = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CanQuery = false
                        p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        p.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos, basePos)
                    end
                end
            
            elseif Config.CurrentMode == "Chaos Cyclone" then
                for i, point in ipairs(Config.HeartPoints) do
                    local toy = point.assigned
                    if toy and toy.BP and toy.BG then
                        local rotationAngle = toy.baseAngle + (Config.HeartTime * (Config.RotationSpeed or 20.0))
                        local pulse = math.sin(Config.HeartTime * (Config.PulseSpeed or 5.0)) * (Config.PulseAmount or 10.0)
                        local currentRadius = (Config.Radius or 15.0) + pulse
                        local currentHeight = (Config.Height or 10.0) + math.sin(Config.HeartTime * 3 + i) * 5
                        
                        local chaosX = math.sin(Config.HeartTime * 2 + i) * 2
                        local chaosY = math.cos(Config.HeartTime * 2.5 + i) * 2
                        local chaosZ = math.sin(Config.HeartTime * 3 + i) * 2
                        
                        local x = math.cos(rotationAngle) * currentRadius + chaosX
                        local z = math.sin(rotationAngle) * currentRadius + chaosZ
                        local y = currentHeight + chaosY
                        
                        local offset = Vector3.new(x, y, z)
                        
                        local targetPos = basePos + rotationCFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(offset)
                        
                        if point.part then point.part.Position = targetPos end
                        toy.BP.Position = targetPos
                        toy.BG.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos, basePos)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
})
