ObjectsMANZI:AddToggle({
    Name = "Rainbow Crosshair",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        if Value then
            if not localPlayer then return end

            local oldGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Crosshairs")
            if oldGui then oldGui:Destroy() end
            local customGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("RainbowCrosshair")
            if customGui then customGui:Destroy() end
            local existingKurosu = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("kurosuheamanzi")
            if existingKurosu then existingKurosu:Destroy() end

            local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            ScreenGui.Name = "kurosuheamanzi"
            ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            ScreenGui.Parent = PlayerGui

            local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            TextLabel.Name = "FrostLmanzi"
            TextLabel.Text = "卍"
            TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            TextLabel.TextSize = 30
            TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
            TextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -30)
            TextLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TextLabel.Parent = ScreenGui

            local hue = 0
            Config.reinbookurosuhea = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                hue = (hue + deltaTime) % 1 
                local newColor = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1)

                TextLabel.TextColor3 = newColor
                service.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = newColor
                TextLabel.Rotation = TextLabel.Rotation + 180 * deltaTime 
            end)
        else
            if Config.reinbookurosuhea then
                Config.reinbookurosuhea:Disconnect()
                Config.reinbookurosuhea = nil
            end

            local existingKurosu = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("kurosuheamanzi")
            if existingKurosu then
                existingKurosu:Destroy()
            end
            
            service.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end    
})

MainAura:AddToggle({
    Name = "Grab Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        if Config.auraThread then
            task.cancel(Config.auraThread)
            Config.auraThread = nil
        end

        if not enabled then return end

        local function getPlayersInRange(center, radius)
            local playersInRange = {}
            for _, player in pairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if CanAuraTarget(player) then
                    local char = player.Character
                    if char then
                        local playerTorso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if playerTorso then
                            local distance = (playerTorso.Position - center.Position).Magnitude
                            if distance <= radius then
                                table.insert(playersInRange, player)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return playersInRange
        end

        Config.auraThread = task.spawn(function()
            while enabled do
                local character = localPlayer.Character
                if character then
                    local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if humanoidRootPart then
                        local targets = getPlayersInRange(humanoidRootPart, Config.auraRadius)
                        
                        for _, player in pairs(targets) do
                            task.spawn(function()
                                local playerCharacter = player.Character
                                if playerCharacter then
                                    local playerTorso = playerCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso") or playerCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    
                                    if playerTorso then
                                        if SetNetworkOwner then
                                            pcall(function() 
                                                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(playerTorso, playerCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame) 
                                            end)
                                        end

                                        local velocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                                        velocity.Parent = playerTorso
                                        velocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                        velocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                        service.Debris:AddItem(velocity, 0.1)
                                    end
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.01)
            end
        end)
    end
})

MainAura:AddToggle({
    Name = "Sit Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.SitAuraToggle = Value

        if Config.SitauraConnection then
            Config.SitauraConnection:Disconnect()
            Config.SitauraConnection = nil
        end

        if Value then
            Config.SitauraConnection = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not Config.SitAuraToggle then return end
                
                local rootPart = GetPlayerCharacter() and GetPlayerCharacter().HumanoidRootPart
                if not rootPart then return end

                for _, player in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if CanAuraTarget(player) then
                        local character = player.Character
                        if character then
                            local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                            if torso and humanoid and (torso.Position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude <= Config.auraRadius then
                                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(torso, rootPart.CFrame)
                                humanoid.Sit = true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})
MainAura:AddToggle({
    Name = "Ragdoll Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        Config.RagdollAuraToggle = v
        if Config.RagdollAuraThread then
            task.cancel(Config.RagdollAuraThread)
            Config.RagdollAuraThread = nil
        end

        if v then
            Config.RagdollAuraThread = task.spawn(function()
                while Config.RagdollAuraToggle do
                    local myRoot = GetPlayerCharacter() and GetPlayerCharacter().HumanoidRootPart
                    if myRoot then
                        for _, player in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if CanAuraTarget(player) then
                                local character = player.Character
                                if character then
                                    local targetRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if targetRoot and (targetRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude <= Config.auraRadius then
                                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(targetRoot, targetRoot.CFrame)
                                        targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -256, 0)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})

MainAura:AddToggle({
    Name = "Spin Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        Config.spinAuraToggle = enabled
        if Config.spinConnection then
            task.cancel(Config.spinConnection)
            Config.spinConnection = nil
        end

        if enabled then
            Config.spinConnection = task.spawn(function()
                while Config.spinAuraToggle do
                    local rootPart = GetPlayerCharacter() and GetPlayerCharacter().HumanoidRootPart
                    if rootPart then
                        for _, player in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if CanAuraTarget(player) ~= false then
                                local character = player.Character
                                if character then
                                    local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    if torso and (torso.Position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude <= Config.auraRadius then
                                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(torso, rootPart.CFrame)
                                        
                                        local spin = torso:FindFirstChild("SpinForce") or Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
                                        spin.Name = "SpinForce"
                                        spin.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(999999, 999999, 999999)
                                        spin.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, Config.spinSpeed, 0)
                                        spin.Parent = torso
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end)
        else
            for _, player in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local character = player.Character
                if character then
                    local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if torso then
                        local spin = torso:FindFirstChild("SpinForce")
                        if spin then
                            spin:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

MainAura:AddToggle({
    Name = "Bring Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Val)
        Config.BringAura = Val
        if Val then
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.BringAura do
                    for _, vas4 in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if CanAuraTarget(vas4) then
                            local CH = vas4.Character
                            if CH then
                                local HumT = CH:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local HumD = CH:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                local GPTWW = GetPlayerCharacter()
                                
                                if HumD and HumT and GPTWW and GPTWW:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                    local Vass2 = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(HumT.Position)
                                    if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and Vass2 <= 30 then
                                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(HumT, lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, HumT.Position))
                                    end
                                    HumD.Sit = false
                                    HumD.WalkSpeed = 25
                                    HumD:MoveTo(GPTWW.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                                end
                            end
                            
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

MainAura:AddToggle({
    Name = "Death Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        if Config.deathConnection then Config.deathConnection:Disconnect() end
        Config.deathAuraToggle = state
        if not state then return end

        Config.deathConnection = service.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            for _, plr in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if CanAuraTarget(plr) then
                    local char = plr.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local myHRP = GetPlayerCharacter() and GetPlayerCharacter().HumanoidRootPart
                    
                    if hrp and myHRP and (hrp.Position - myHRP.Position).Magnitude <= 25 then
                        pcall(function()
                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(hrp, hrp.CFrame)
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(hrp)
                            for _, bp in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                                if bp:IsA("BasePart") then bp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1e9, 1e9, -1e9) end
                            end
                            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if hum then
                                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
})

stickyaura:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Sticky Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AntiStickyAuraToggle = Value
        
        if Config.AntiStickyAurahread then
            task.cancel(Config.AntiStickyAurahread)
            Config.AntiStickyAurahread = nil
        end
     
        if not Config.AntiStickyAuraToggle then return end
     
        partname = { 
            "NinjaKunai", "NinjaShuriken", "NinjaKatana", 
            "ToolCleaver", "ToolDiggingForkRusty", 
            "ToolPencil", "ToolPickaxe" 
        }
     
        Config.AntiStickyAurahread = task.spawn(function()
            while Config.AntiStickyAuraToggle do
                local character = localPlayer.Character
                if character then
                    local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        for _, folder in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                            if folder:IsA("Folder") and folder.Name:match("SpawnedInToys$") and folder.Name ~= (localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys") then
                                local ownerName = folder.Name:gsub("SpawnedInToys$", "")
                                local ownerPlayer = service.Players:FindFirstChild(ownerName)
                                if ownerPlayer and CanAuraTarget(ownerPlayer) ~= false then
                                    for _, item in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                                        for _, partnme in ipairs(partname) do
                                            if item.Name == partnme and item:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                                                local sticky = item.StickyPart
                                                local basePart = item.PrimaryPart or sticky
                                                local dist = (basePart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                                                if dist <= 30 and SetNetworkOwner then
                                                    pcall(function() SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(sticky, sticky.CFrame) end)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end)
    end
})


FlingA:AddToggle({
    Name = "Fling Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(a7X9)
        Config.FlingAura = a7X9
        if a7X9 then
            local function lookAt(r2M1, k8W4)
                local dadadada = (k8W4 - r2M1).Unit
                local dadadada2 = dadadada:Cross((Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)))
                local dadadada3 = dadadada2:Cross(dadadada)
                return CFrame.fromMatrix(r2M1, dadadada2, dadadada3)
            end

            local function CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(n3B8, z5P2)
                if typeof(n3B8) == "Instance" and (n3B8:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and n3B8.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name) then
                    return not z5P2 and true or n3B8.PartOwner
                end
            end

            local function SNOWshipOnce(x9V4)
                local v125_ = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(x9V4.Position)
                if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(x9V4) then
                        return true
                    end
                    if v125_ <= 30 then
                        if SetNetworkOwner then
                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(x9V4, lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, x9V4.Position))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(f6H2, t1C7)
                if typeof(f6H2) == "Instance" and (f6H2:IsA("Player") and f6H2.Character) and
                    (f6H2.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") and
                    (f6H2.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and
                    f6H2.Character.Head.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name))
                then
                    return not t1C7 and true or f6H2.Character.Head.PartOwner
                end
            end

            local function SNOWshipPlayer(m4K9, q8J3)
                if localPlayer.Character and
                    (localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and
                    (typeof(m4K9) == "Instance" and (m4K9:IsA("Player") and m4K9.Character)) and
                    m4K9.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                then
                    local jhu = m4K9.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    local v134_ = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(jhu.Position)
                    if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPlayer(m4K9) then
                        if type(q8J3) == "function" then
                            q8J3()
                        end
                        return true
                    end
                    if v134_ <= 30 then
                        if SetNetworkOwner then
                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(jhu, lookAt(localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, jhu.Position))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function GetPlayerCharacter()
                if localPlayer.Character and
                    (localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and
                    localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
                then
                    return localPlayer.Character
                end
            end

            local function GetPlayerRoot()
                local GPRCR = GetPlayerCharacter()
                if GPRCR then
                    return GPRCR.HumanoidRootPart
                end
            end

            local COAroundPParams = OverlapParams.new()
            COAroundPParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
                GetPlayerCharacter(),
                service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map"),
                service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots"),
                service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Waypoints"),
                service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Slots")
            }
            COAroundPParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.FlingAura do
                    if Config.Flingtype == 2 or Config.Flingtype == 3 then
                        local gtprroot = GetPlayerRoot()
                        if gtprroot then
                            local dadas2 = {}
                            local _2dq2 = service.Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(gtprroot.Position, 35, COAroundPParams)
                            
                            for b2D5, w7N1 in pairs(_2dq2) do
                                if w7N1 and w7N1.Parent and w7N1:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    local model = w7N1.Parent
                                    if model:IsA("Model") then
                                        local folder = model.Parent
                                        if folder then
                                            local isTarget = false
                                            
                                            if Config.Flingauratype == "Map Toys" then
                                                if table.find(MapToyFolders, folder.Name) then
                                                    isTarget = true
                                                end
                                            elseif Config.Flingauratype == "Player Toys" then
                                                if string.match(folder.Name, "SpawnedInToys$") then
                                                    isTarget = true
                                                end
                                            elseif Config.Flingauratype == "All Toys" then
                                                if table.find(MapToyFolders, folder.Name) or string.match(folder.Name, "SpawnedInToys$") then
                                                    isTarget = true
                                                end
                                            end
                                            
                                            if isTarget and not table.find(dadas2, model) then
                                                table.insert(dadas2, model)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end

                            for j9Y3, g4S1 in pairs(dadas2) do
                                local attempts = 0
                                if g4S1 then
                                    local hrd = g4S1:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                    for y1X8, c5V2 in pairs(g4S1:GetChildren()) do
                                        if c5V2:IsA("BasePart") and c5V2.CanQuery then
                                            local dadas = SNOWshipOnce(c5V2)
                                            local adas = GetPlayerRoot()
                                            if not dadas and hrd then
                                                dadas = CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(hrd)
                                            end
                                            
                                            if Config.ExcludeOwnToys and 
                                               (Config.Flingauratype == "Player Toys" or Config.Flingauratype == "All Toys") and 
                                               (Config.Flingtype == 2 or Config.Flingtype == 3) then
                                                if CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(c5V2) or (hrd and CheckNetworkOwnerShipOnPart(hrd)) then
                                                    continue
                                                end
                                            end

                                            if dadas and adas then
                                                if not c5V2:FindFirstChild("FlingAuraVelocity") then
                                                    local r232rd = lookAt(adas.Position, c5V2.Position)
                                                    local dasda = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", c5V2)
                                                    dasda.Name = "FlingAuraVelocity"
                                                    dasda.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                                    dasda.Velocity = Vector3.new(r232rd.lookVector.X, 0.5, r232rd.lookVector.Z) * math.clamp(Config.FlingPower, 400, 600)
                                                    service.Debris:AddItem(dasda, 0.15)
                                                end
                                                attempts = attempts + 1
                                            end
                                            if attempts >= 3 then break end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    if Config.Flingtype == 1 or Config.Flingtype == 3 then
                        local v873_ = service.Players
                        for h3F6, d9L2 in pairs(v873_:GetPlayers()) do
                            if d9L2 and d9L2 ~= localPlayer and d9L2.Character then
                                
                                if Config.WhitelistFriends then
                                    if isDeveloper(d9L2.UserId) then continue end
                                    if localPlayer:IsFriendsWith(d9L2.UserId) then continue end
                                    if table.find(kickAuraWhitelist or {}, d9L2.Name) then continue end
                                    if table.find(Config.Manualwhite or {}, d9L2.Name) then continue end
                                end
                                local v878_ = d9L2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local v879_ = SNOWshipPlayer(d9L2)
                                local v880_ = GetPlayerCharacter()
                                if v878_ and (v879_ and (v880_ and not v878_:FindFirstChild("FlingAuraVelocity"))) then
                                    local v881_ = lookAt(v880_.HumanoidRootPart.Position, v878_.Position)
                                    local v882_ = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", v878_)
                                    v882_.Name = "FlingAuraVelocity"
                                    v882_.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                    v882_.Velocity = Vector3.new(v881_.lookVector.X, 0.5, v881_.lookVector.Z) * Config.FlingPower
                                    service.Debris:AddItem(v882_, 0.15)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

    Name = "Exclude Own Toys",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)

            Config.Flingtype = 1
        elseif x1N9 == "Objects" then
            Config.Flingtype = 2
        elseif x1N9 == "Players and Objects" then
            Config.Flingtype = 3
        end
    end,
})

Autosp:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Slot",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AutoSlot = Value
        
        if Value then
            if Config.Connection then Config.Connection:Disconnect() end
            
            Config.Connection = SlotTime.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(remainingTime)
                Config.Time = remainingTime
                
                if Config.Time == 0 and Config.AutoSlot then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local root = GetRoot()
                        if not root then return end
                        
                        local originalPos = root.CFrame
                        
                        while Config.Time == 0 and Config.AutoSlot do
                            for _, slot in ipairs(service.Workspace.Slots:GetChildren()) do
                                if not Config.AutoSlot then break end
                                
                                local handle = slot:FindFirstChild("SlotHandle") and slot.SlotHandle:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                                if handle then
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    root.CFrame = handle.CFrame
                                    task.wait(0.2)
                                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(handle, root.CFrame)
                                end
                            end
                            task.wait()
                        end
                        
                        task.wait(1.5)
                        root.CFrame = originalPos
                    end)
                end
            end)
            
        else
            if Config.Connection then
                Config.Connection:Disconnect()
                Config.Connection = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

local TimeLabel = House:AddLabel("House-Time: 0")
local OwnerLabel = House:AddLabel("House-Owner: ?")

House:AddToggle({
    Name = "House Time",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.HouseTPauto = Value

        if not Value and Config.isTeleported and Config.savedPosition then
            local hrp = HRP()
            if hrp then
                hrp.CFrame = Config.savedPosition
            end
            Config.isTeleported = false
            Config.savedPosition = nil
        end
    end
})

House:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Select House",
    Default = Config.SelectedPlot,
    Options = {
        {Name = "Purple-House", Image = "rbxassetid://9909725942"},
        {Name = "Red-House", Image = "rbxassetid://8989326312"},
        {Name = "Green-House", Image = "rbxassetid://8989327148"},
        {Name = "Blue-House", Image = "rbxassetid://9909733734"},
        {Name = "Chinese-House", Image = "rbxassetid://9108988013"},
    },
    Callback = function(value)
        Config.SelectedPlot = value
    end
})

House:AddButton({
    Name = "House",
    Callback = function()
        local plot = GetPlot()
        if not plot then return end

        local isOwned = false
        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if sign then
            local ownersFolder = sign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
            if ownersFolder then
                local valueObject = ownersFolder:FindFirstChild("Value")
                if valueObject and valueObject:IsA("StringValue") then
                    if valueObject.Value and valueObject.Value ~= "" then
                        isOwned = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if isOwned then return end

        local claimSign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if not claimSign then return end

        for _, child in pairs(claimSign:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == "Sign" then
                local grab = child:FindFirstChild("Plus")
                    and child.Plus:FindFirstChild("PlusGrabPart")

                if grab then
                    local char = GetCharacter()
                    local cf = grab.CFrame * CFrame.new(-5, 0, -5)
                    if char and typeof(cf) == "CFrame" then
                        char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cf
                    end

                    for i = 1, 15 do
                        local currentChar = GetCharacter()
                        
                        if currentChar and grab and grab:IsA("BasePart") then
                            if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(grab.Position) <= 30 then
                                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(
                                    grab,
                                    lookAt(
                                        currentChar.HumanoidRootPart.Position,
                                        grab.Position
                                    )
                                )
                            end
                        end
                        
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

Barrier:AddButton({
    Name = "Break Barrier",
    Callback = function()
        if InPlot.Value and not InOwnedPlot.Value then 
            InPlot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
        end 
        if not CanSpawnToy.Value then 
            CanSpawnToy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
        end
        
        local playerCF = PLCF() or CFrame.new()
        local SpawnCF = playerCF * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
        task.spawn(SpawnToyRemoteFunction.InvokeServer, SpawnToyRemoteFunction, "FoodHamburger", SpawnCF, Vector3.zero)
        
        local Hamburger
        if InOwnedPlot.Value then 
            local Plot = CheckForHome()
            Hamburger = Plot and (Plot:FindFirstChild("FoodHamburger") or Plot:WaitForChild("FoodHamburger", 2.5))
        else 
            Hamburger = SpawnedInToys:FindFirstChild("FoodHamburger") or SpawnedInToys:WaitForChild("FoodHamburger", 2.5)
        end

        if not Hamburger then return end
        
        local HoldPart = Hamburger:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") or Hamburger:WaitForChild("HoldPart", 10)
        local GrabItemRemoteFunction = HoldPart and (HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction") or HoldPart:WaitForChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction", 10))
        
        if GrabItemRemoteFunction then 
            local oldCF = GetCharacter():GetPivot()
            
            GrabItemRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(Hamburger, GetCharacter())
            GetCharacter():PivotTo(workspace.Plots.Plot3.PlotArea.CFrame)
            task.wait(0.1)
            GetCharacter():PivotTo(oldCF)
            
            DestroyToy:FireServer(Hamburger)
        end 
    end    
})

Barrier:AddToggle({
    Name = "Barrier Noclip",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        local shouldCollide = not Value

        for _, v in ipairs(service.Workspace.Plots:GetChildren()) do
            local b = v:FindFirstChild("Barrier")
            if b then
                for _, p in ipairs(b:GetChildren()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                        p.CanCollide = shouldCollide
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end    
})

TrainTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Train Control",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        local function getMoveVector()
            return ControlModule:GetMoveVector()
        end
		function getToyFolder()
            return service.Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
		end

        local function getToyInFolder()
            local folder = getToyFolder()
            if folder then
                return folder:FindFirstChild(Config.TrainToy)
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function spawnToy(targetCFrame)
            if not SpawnToyRemoteFunction then return nil end
            local ok = pcall(function()
                SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(Config.TrainToy, targetCFrame, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
            end)
            if not ok then return nil end
            local start = tick()
            while tick() - start < Config.p4c9 do
                local toy = getToyInFolder()
                if toy then return toy end
                task.wait(0.01)
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function getHRP()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            return char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
        end

        local function forceSpawnToyAtHRP()
            local hrp = getHRP()
            if not hrp then return nil end
            local folder = getToyFolder()
            if folder then
                local old = folder:FindFirstChild(Config.TrainToy)
                if old then
                    pcall(function() old:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
            return spawnToy(hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(Config.r5e2))
        end

        local function ensureToy()
            local toy = getToyInFolder()
            if toy and toy.Parent then return toy end
            return forceSpawnToyAtHRP()
        end

        local function getAllTrainSeats()
            local seats = {}
            local map = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local ahto = map and map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
            local train = ahto and ahto:FindFirstChild("Train")
            local obj = train and train:FindFirstChild("Object")
            local objModel = obj and obj:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
            
            if objModel then
                for _, descendant in ipairs(objModel:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("Seat") or descendant:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
                        table.insert(seats, descendant)
                    end
                end
            end
            
            return seats
        end

        local function getFirstTrainSeat()
            local seats = getAllTrainSeats()
            return seats[1]
        end

        local function getTrainModel()
            local map = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local ahto = map and map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
            local train = ahto and ahto:FindFirstChild("Train")
            local obj = train and train:FindFirstChild("Object")
            return obj and obj:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
        end

        local function isTrainSeat(seatPart)
            if not seatPart then return false end
            local trainModel = getTrainModel()
            if not trainModel then return false end
            
            return seatPart:IsDescendantOf(trainModel)
        end

        local function sitTrain()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local seat = getFirstTrainSeat()
            if hum and seat then
                pcall(function()
                    seat:Sit(hum)
                end)
            end
        end

        local function getHum()
            local c = localPlayer.Character
            return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end

        local function enableCameraNoClip()
            if Config.z3x7 == nil then
                Config.z3x7 = localPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode
            end
            localPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode = Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam
        end

        local function restoreCameraNoClip()
            if Config.z3x7 ~= nil then
                localPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode = Config.z3x7
                Config.z3x7 = nil
            end
        end

        local function getAllPlayerEggs()
            local eggs = {}
            for _, player in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local egg = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
                if egg then
                    table.insert(eggs, egg)
                end
            end
            return eggs
        end

        local function startLoop()
            if Config.l5k2 then return end
            Config.l5k2 = task.spawn(function()
                while Config.f3v1 and not getHRP() do
                    task.wait(0.01)
                end
                if not Config.f3v1 then
                    Config.l5k2 = nil
                    return
                end

                while Config.f3v1 do
                    local cycleStart = tick()

                    local hrp = getHRP()
                    local eggs = getAllPlayerEggs()

                    if hrp and #eggs > 0 then
                        for _, egg in ipairs(eggs) do
                            local toy = ensureToy()
                            if toy and toy.Parent then
                                local holdFn = (toy:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") and toy.HoldPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")) or (MenuToys and MenuToys:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction"))
                                if holdFn then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        holdFn:InvokeServer(toy, egg)
                                    end)
                                end

                                task.wait(0.03)

                                local dropFn = (toy:FindFirstChild("HoldPart") and toy.HoldPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")) or (MenuToys and MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction"))
                                if dropFn then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        dropFn:InvokeServer(toy, hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(Config.r5e2), Vector3.new(0, -6.55, 0))
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    local elapsed = tick() - cycleStart
                    local remain = Config.k3b8 - elapsed
                    task.wait(remain > 0 and remain or 0.01)
                end

                Config.l5k2 = nil
            end)
        end

        local function stopLoop()
            Config.f3v1 = false
        end

        local function restoreNoClipState(modelRef, state)
            if modelRef then
                for part, st in pairs(state) do
                    if part and part.Parent and typeof(st) == "table" then
                        pcall(function()
                            part.CanCollide = st.CanCollide
                            part.CanQuery = st.CanQuery
                            part.CanTouch = st.CanTouch
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function restoreVehicleNoClip()
            restoreNoClipState(Config.y2u8, Config.p9l5)
            Config.y2u8 = nil
            Config.p9l5 = {}
        end

        local function restoreCharNoClip()
            restoreNoClipState(Config.i4o1, Config.k8j2)
            Config.i4o1 = nil
            Config.k8j2 = {}
        end

        local function applyNoClipToModel(model, stateTable)
            if not model then return end
            for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if not stateTable[d] then
                        stateTable[d] = {CanCollide = d.CanCollide, CanQuery = d.CanQuery, CanTouch = d.CanTouch}
                    end
                    d.CanCollide = false
                    d.CanQuery = false
                    d.CanTouch = false
                end
            end
        end

        local function claimNetworkOwnershipSingle(part)
            if not part then return end
            pcall(function()
                part:SetNetworkOwner(localPlayer)
            end)
        end

        local function claimNetworkOwnershipModel(model)
            if not model then return end
            pcall(function()
                for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pcall(function()
                            descendant:SetNetworkOwner(localPlayer)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local function claimNetworkOwnership(part)
            if not part then return end
            claimNetworkOwnershipSingle(part)
            local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
            if model then
                claimNetworkOwnershipModel(model)
            end
        end

        local function startOwnershipThread(threadId, playerPart)
            if Config.e7r3[threadId] then return end
            Config.e7r3[threadId] = task.spawn(function()
                while Config.TrainToggle and playerPart and playerPart.Parent do
                    claimNetworkOwnership(playerPart)
                    task.wait(Config.c2x8)
                end
                Config.e7r3[threadId] = nil
            end)
        end

        local function stopAllOwnershipThreads()
            for id, thread in pairs(Config.e7r3) do
                if thread then
                    pcall(function()
                        task.cancel(thread)
                    end)
                end
            end
            Config.e7r3 = {}
        end

        local function destroyVFly()
            if Config.b6n2 then 
                pcall(function() Config.b6n2:Destroy() end)
                Config.b6n2 = nil 
            end
            if Config.g4h9 then 
                pcall(function() Config.g4h9:Destroy() end)
                Config.g4h9 = nil 
            end
            Config.t3r7 = nil
            Config.a8s1 = false
            Config.w1q5 = 0
            stopAllOwnershipThreads()
        end

        local function getTrainRootPart()
            local trainModel = getTrainModel()
            if not trainModel then return nil end
            if trainModel.PrimaryPart then
                return trainModel.PrimaryPart
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(trainModel:GetDescendants()) do
                if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Size.Magnitude > 5 then
                    return child
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function startVFlyForTrain()
            destroyVFly()
            local part = getTrainRootPart()
            if not part then return end

            claimNetworkOwnership(part)

            Config.b6n2 = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            Config.b6n2.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            Config.b6n2.P = Config.g8q1
            Config.b6n2.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            Config.b6n2.Parent = part

            Config.g4h9 = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            Config.g4h9.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            Config.g4h9.P = Config.z4t6
            Config.g4h9.CFrame = part.CFrame
            Config.g4h9.Parent = part

            Config.t3r7 = part
            Config.a8s1 = true
            Config.w1q5 = tick()
            
            for i = 1, Config.j9b4 do
                startOwnershipThread(i, part)
            end
        end

        local function hardStopAll()
            Config.TrainToggle = false
            Config.s6e8 = 0
            Config.u2i4 = 0
            Config.o9p3 = false

            destroyVFly()
            stopLoop()
            restoreCameraNoClip()
            Config.m9a1 = false
            stopAllOwnershipThreads()
            
            if Config.Trainrende then Config.Trainrende:Disconnect() Config.Trainrende = nil end
            if Config.charTrain then Config.charTrain:Disconnect() Config.charTrain = nil end
        end

        if Value then
            if Config.Trainrende then Config.Trainrende:Disconnect() end
            if Config.charTrain then Config.charTrain:Disconnect() end

            Config.TrainToggle = true
            Config.s6e8 = tick()
            Config.u2i4 = 0
            Config.o9p3 = false
            Config.f3v1 = true
            startLoop()

            Config.Trainrende = service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                local hum = getHum()
                if not hum then
                    if Config.TrainToggle or Config.a8s1 or Config.f3v1 or Config.m9a1 then
                        hardStopAll()
                    end
                    return
                end

                local seatPart = hum.SeatPart

                if Config.TrainToggle then
                    if Config.o9p3 and not seatPart then
                        if Config.a8s1 then destroyVFly() end
                        if tick() - Config.u2i4 >= Config.h7y3 then
                            Config.u2i4 = tick()
                            for i = 1, 3 do
                                sitTrain()
                                task.wait(0.005)
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    end

                    if not seatPart then
                        if tick() - Config.s6e8 <= Config.n1m5 then
                            if tick() - Config.u2i4 >= Config.h7y3 then
                                Config.u2i4 = tick()
                                for i = 1, 3 do
                                    sitTrain()
                                    task.wait(0.005)
                                end
                            end
                            return
                        else
                            hardStopAll()
                            return
                        end
                    end

                    if not isTrainSeat(seatPart) then
                        if tick() - Config.s6e8 <= Config.n1m5 then
                            if tick() - Config.u2i4 >= Config.h7y3 then
                                Config.u2i4 = tick()
                                for i = 1, 3 do
                                    sitTrain()
                                    task.wait(0.005)
                                end
                            end
                            return
                        else
                            hardStopAll()
                            return
                        end
                    end

                    local justMounted = false
                    if not Config.o9p3 then justMounted = true end

                    Config.o9p3 = true
                    enableCameraNoClip()

                    if justMounted then forceSpawnToyAtHRP() end

                    if Config.m9a1 then
                        local trainModel = getTrainModel()
                        if trainModel then
                            if Config.y2u8 ~= trainModel then
                                restoreVehicleNoClip()
                                Config.y2u8 = trainModel
                            end
                            applyNoClipToModel(trainModel, Config.p9l5)
                        end

                        local cModel = localPlayer.Character
                        if cModel then
                            if Config.i4o1 ~= cModel then
                                restoreCharNoClip()
                                Config.i4o1 = cModel
                            end
                            applyNoClipToModel(cModel, Config.k8j2)
                        end
                    else
                        if Config.y2u8 then restoreVehicleNoClip() end
                        if Config.i4o1 then restoreCharNoClip() end
                    end

                    if not Config.a8s1 then startVFlyForTrain() end
                    
                    if Config.a8s1 and Config.t3r7 and tick() - Config.w1q5 > Config.c2x8 then
                        claimNetworkOwnership(Config.t3r7)
                        Config.w1q5 = tick()
                    end
                else
                    if Config.a8s1 or Config.f3v1 or Config.m9a1 then
                        hardStopAll()
                    end
                end

                if not Config.a8s1 then return end
                if not (Config.t3r7 and Config.t3r7.Parent and Config.b6n2 and Config.g4h9) then
                    hardStopAll()
                    return
                end

                local cam = service.Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not cam then return end

                local move = getMoveVector()
                local dir = Vector3.zero

                if move.Magnitude > 0 then
                    dir = cam.CFrame.RightVector * move.X + cam.CFrame.LookVector * (-move.Z)
                    if dir.Magnitude > 0 then dir = dir.Unit end
                end

                local speed = Config.x7f2 * Config.d2w7
                Config.b6n2.Velocity = dir * speed
                Config.g4h9.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Config.t3r7.Position, Config.t3r7.Position + cam.CFrame.LookVector, cam.CFrame.UpVector)
            end)

            Config.charTrain = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                task.wait(0.5)
                hardStopAll()
            end)

            for i = 1, 5 do
                sitTrain()
                task.wait(0.01)
            end
        else
            hardStopAll()
        end
    end    
})

TrainTab:AddSlider({
    Name = "Fly Speed",
    Min = 1,
    Max = 50,
    Default = 15,
    Increment = 1,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.x7f2 = Value
    end    
})

TrainTab:AddToggle({
    Name = "Noclip",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.m9a1 = Value
    end    
})

local PlayerSelectDropdown = selectloop:AddPlayersDropdown({
	Name = "Select Target",
	MultipleSelection = true, --複数選択有効
	Default = {},
	Callback = function(Values)
		if not Values or typeof(Values) ~= "table" or #Values == 0 then 
			Config.PlayerList = {}
			return 
		end

		local validPlayers = {}
		for _, name in ipairs(Values) do
			local player = service.Players:FindFirstChild(name)
			if player then
				table.insert(validPlayers, player.Name)
			end
		end
		Config.PlayerList = validPlayers
	end
})
selectloop:AddToggle({
    Name = "LoopKill<font color=\"rgb(255, 255, 0)\"><b>[BLOB]</b></font>",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.Loopkillblob = Value
        if Value then
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            
            local etc = {
                MyPCLD = nil,
                TargetPLR = nil,
                Root = nil,
                Hum = nil,
                LastBlob = nil,
            }

            local function FWD(parent, part, time)
                return parent:FindFirstChild(part) or parent:WaitForChild(part, time)
            end

            local function CFP(parent, part)
                return parent:FindFirstChild(part) ~= nil  
            end

            local function FindBlob()
                if etc["LastBlob"] == nil or not CFP(etc["LastBlob"], "VehicleSeat") or etc["LastBlob"].VehicleSeat.Occupant ~= hum then 
                    etc["LastBlob"] = hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart:FindFirstAncestor("CreatureBlobman")
                end 
                return etc["LastBlob"]
            end

            local function CheckForHome()
                if etc["LastHouse"] == nil or etc["LastPlotOwner"] == nil or etc["LastPlotOwner"].Parent == nil then 
                    for i = 1, 5 do 
                        local Plot = workspace.Plots["Plot"..i]
                        for _, v in pairs(Plot.PlotSign["ThisPlotsOwners"]:GetChildren()) do 
                            if v.Value == localPlayer.Name then 
                                etc["LastHouse"] = workspace.PlotItems["Plot"..i]
                                etc["LastPlotOwner"] = v 
                                return etc["LastHouse"]
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return etc["LastHouse"]
            end

            local function SpawnToy(ToyName: string)
                if InPlot.Value and not InOwnedPlot.Value then 
                    InPlot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
                end 
                if not CanSpawnToy.Value then 
                    CanSpawnToy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
                end

                local SpawnCF = (etc.MyPCLD or hrp).CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
                
                local Container = InOwnedPlot.Value and CheckForHome() or SpawnedInToys
                if not Container then return nil end

                local spawnedObject = nil
                local connection
                connection = Container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                    if child.Name == ToyName then
                        spawnedObject = child
                    end
                end)

                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(ToyName, SpawnCF, Vector3.zero)
                    end)
                end)

                local start = tick()
                repeat task.wait() until spawnedObject or (tick() - start) > 2.5

                connection:Disconnect()
                return spawnedObject
            end

            local function getHRP(targetChar)
                return targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            end

            local function ForceDeathNotG(targetHum)  
                targetHum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                targetHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                targetHum.Sit = false 
                targetHum.Jump = true  
            end

            _G.HeightLimit = 10000
            function _G.IsAboveLimit(Player)
                if Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    return Player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y > _G.HeightLimit
                end
                return true
            end

            local oldCF = char:GetPivot()
            local blob = FindBlob() or SpawnToy("CreatureBlobman")
            if not blob then return end
            
            FWD(blob, "VehicleSeat"):Sit(hum)
            
            local CreatureGrab = FWD(FWD(blob, "BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript"), "CreatureGrab")
            local CreatureRelease = FWD(FWD(blob, "BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript"), "CreatureRelease")
            local RightDetector = FWD(blob, "RightDetector")
            local RightWeld = RightDetector and FWD(RightDetector, "RightWeld")
            
            while not hum.SeatPart do task.wait() end 
            
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.Loopkillblob and task.wait() do  
                    if not Config.PlayerList or #Config.PlayerList == 0 then
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        continue
                    end

                    for _, playerName in ipairs(Config.PlayerList) do
                        if not Config.Loopkillblob then break end

                        local targetPlr = service.Players:FindFirstChild(playerName)
                        if not targetPlr or not targetPlr.Character then continue end

                        etc.TargetPLR = targetPlr
                        etc.Root = getHRP(targetPlr.Character)
                        etc.Hum = targetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                        if (not etc.Root or not etc.Hum) or _G.IsAboveLimit(targetPlr) or etc.Hum.Health == 0 then 
                            continue 
                        end 

                        oldCF = char:GetPivot() 

                        while not RightWeld.Attachment0 and Config.Loopkillblob do
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            etc.Root = getHRP(targetPlr.Character)
                            etc.Hum = targetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                            
                            if (not etc.Root or not etc.Hum) or _G.IsAboveLimit(targetPlr) or etc.Hum.Health == 0 then 
                                char:PivotTo(oldCF) 
                                break 
                            end 
                            
                            if not hum.SeatPart then  
                                SpawnedInToys:FindFirstChild("CreatureBlobman").VehicleSeat:Sit(hum)
                            end 
                            
                            char:PivotTo(etc.Root.CFrame)
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            CreatureGrab:FireServer(RightDetector, etc.Root, RightWeld)
                        end 

                        if not Config.Loopkillblob then break end

                        CreatureRelease:FireServer(RightWeld, etc.Root)
                        char:PivotTo(etc.Root.CFrame)
                        
                        for _ = 1, 10 do 
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            CreatureGrab:FireServer(RightDetector, etc.Root, RightWeld)
                            CreatureRelease:FireServer(RightWeld, etc.Root)
                            
                            if isnetworkowner(etc.Root) then  
                                ForceDeathNotG(etc.Hum)
                            end 
                            task.wait()
                        end 

                        char:PivotTo(oldCF)
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end    
})

Blob:AddDropdown({
    Name = "Kick Mode",
    Default = "Drift",
    Options = {"Drift", "Spam"},
    Callback = function(val)
        Config.KickMode = val
    end
})

Blob:AddToggle({
    Name = 'Kick<font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,0,0)">BLOBMAN</font>',
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(on)
        if on and (not Config.PlayerList or #Config.PlayerList == 0 or Config.PlayerList[1] == "") then
            FrostNotify("Select target first")
            return
        end

        if on then
            Config.AutoBlobSit = true
            
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                local startTime = tick()
                while tick() - startTime < 3 do
                    if hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" then
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end
            
            local currentSeat = hum and hum.SeatPart
            if not currentSeat or currentSeat.Parent.Name ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
                FrostNotify("Failed to sit on CreatureBlobman")
                Config.AutoBlobSit = false
                return
            end
        else
            Config.AutoBlobSit = false
        end
        if on then
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local seat = hum and hum.SeatPart
            
            if not seat or seat.Parent.Name ~= "CreatureBlobman" then
                FrostNotify("Must be sitting on Blobman")
                return
            end
            if Config.KickMode == "Drift" then
                Config.DRKickLo = true
                Config.KickspamT = false
                
                local targetName = Config.PlayerList[1]
                local target = targetName and service.Players:FindFirstChild(targetName)

                task.spawn(function()
                    local blob = seat.Parent
                    local blobRoot = blob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or blob.PrimaryPart
                    local scriptObj = blob:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                    local CG = scriptObj and scriptObj:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
                    local CD = scriptObj and scriptObj:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                    local R_Det = blob:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")
                    local SavedPos = blobRoot.CFrame
                    
                    while Config.DRKickLo do
                        if not target or not target.Parent then break end
                        
                        local TrueInitialPos = blobRoot.CFrame
                        local tChar = target.Character
                        local tRoot = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local tHum = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        
                        if not tRoot or not tHum or tHum.Health <= 0 then 
                            task.wait(0.5)
                            while Config.DRKickLo and (not target.Character or not target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or target.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0) do
                                task.wait(0.1)
                            end
                            continue 
                        end

                        local bringStart = os.clock()
                        while os.clock() - bringStart < 0.2 do
                            if not Config.DRKickLo then break end
                            if not target.Character or not target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or target.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                                break
                            end
                            tRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if tRoot and blobRoot then
                                blobRoot.CFrame = tRoot.CFrame
                                blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                pcall(function()
                                    if CG and R_Det then
                                        local weld = R_Det:FindFirstChild("RightWeld") or R_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                                        CG:FireServer(R_Det, tRoot, weld)
                                    end
                                    CreateGrabLine:FireServer(tRoot, Vector3.zero, tRoot.Position, false)
                                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(tRoot, blobRoot.CFrame)
                                end)
                            end
                            service.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                        
                        if Config.DRKickLo then
                            blobRoot.CFrame = TrueInitialPos
                            blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                            task.wait(0.05)
                        end
                        
                        local startY = TrueInitialPos.Position.Y
                        local centerPos = Vector3.new(TrueInitialPos.Position.X, startY + 24, TrueInitialPos.Position.Z)
                        local angle = 0
                        local radius = 34
                        local speed = 0.2
                        
                        while Config.DRKickLo do
                            if not target or not target.Parent then break end
                            
                            tChar = target.Character
                            tRoot = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            tHum = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                            
                            if not tRoot or not tHum or tHum.Health <= 0 then
                                break
                            end
                            
                            if tRoot and tHum and tHum.Health > 0 and blobRoot then
                                angle = angle + speed
                                
                                local offsetX = math.cos(angle) * radius
                                local offsetZ = math.sin(angle) * radius
                                local myPos = centerPos + Vector3.new(offsetX, 0, offsetZ)
                                
                                blobRoot.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, centerPos)
                                blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                
                                tRoot.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(centerPos, myPos)
                                tRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                tRoot.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                
                                pcall(function()
                                    tHum.PlatformStand = true
                                    tHum.Sit = true
                                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(tRoot, tRoot.CFrame)
                                    
                                    if R_Det and CD then
                                        local weld = R_Det:FindFirstChild("RightWeld") or R_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                                        if weld then
                                            CD:FireServer(weld)
                                        end
                                    end
                                    DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(tRoot)
                                    
                                    if R_Det and CG then
                                        local weld = R_Det:FindFirstChild("RightWeld") or R_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                                        CG:FireServer(R_Det, tRoot, weld)
                                    end
                                    CreateGrabLine:FireServer(tRoot, Vector3.zero, tRoot.Position, false)
                                end)
                            else
                                break
                            end
                            
                            task.wait(0.03)
                        end
                        
                        if not Config.DRKickLo then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    
                    Config.DRKickLo = false
                    if blobRoot then
                        blobRoot.CFrame = SavedPos
                        blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end)

            elseif Config.KickMode == "Spam" then
                Config.KickspamT = true
                Config.DRKickLo = false

                local targets = Config.PlayerList
                if not targets or #targets == 0 then
                    Config.KickspamT = false
                    return
                end

                task.spawn(function()
                    local blob = seat.Parent
                    local blobRoot = blob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or blob.PrimaryPart
                    local scriptObj = blob:FindFirstChild("BlobmanSeatAndOwnerScript")
                    local CG = scriptObj and scriptObj:FindFirstChild("CreatureGrab")
                    local CD = scriptObj and scriptObj:FindFirstChild("CreatureDrop")
                    local R_Det = blob:FindFirstChild("RightDetector")
                    local R_Weld = R_Det and (R_Det:FindFirstChild("RightWeld") or R_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld"))
                    local SavedPos = blobRoot.CFrame

                    for _, targetName in ipairs(targets) do
                        if not Config.KickspamT then break end
                        local target = service.Players:FindFirstChild(targetName) or Players:FindFirstChild(targetName)
                        if not target or not target.Character then continue end

                        local tChar = target.Character
                        local tRoot = tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if not tRoot or not blobRoot then continue end
                        
                        local bringStart = tick()
                        while tick() - bringStart < 0.35 do
                            if not Config.KickspamT then break end
                            
                            blobRoot.CFrame = tRoot.CFrame
                            blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                            
                            pcall(function()
                                if CG and R_Det then
                                    CG:FireServer(R_Det, tRoot, R_Weld)
                                end
                                CreateGrabLine:FireServer(tRoot, Vector3.zero, tRoot.Position, false)
                                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(tRoot, blobRoot.CFrame)
                            end)
                            service.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                        
                        blobRoot.CFrame = SavedPos
                        blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        
                        local packetTimer = 0
                        while Config.KickspamT do
                            if not target or not target.Parent or not target.Character then
                                break
                            end

                            local currentTChar = target.Character
                            local currentTRoot = currentTChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local currentTHum = currentTChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                            if currentTRoot and currentTHum and currentTHum.Health > 0 and blobRoot then
                                blobRoot.CFrame = SavedPos
                                blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                local lockPos = SavedPos * CFrame.new(0, 23, 0)
                                currentTRoot.CFrame = lockPos
                                currentTRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                currentTRoot.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                
                                if tick() - packetTimer > 0.05 then
                                    packetTimer = tick()
                                    pcall(function()
                                        currentTHum.PlatformStand = true
                                        currentTHum.Sit = true
                                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(currentTRoot, lockPos)
                                        if R_Det then
                                            local weld = R_Det:FindFirstChild("RightWeld") or R_Det:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                                            if weld then
                                                CD:FireServer(weld)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(currentTRoot)
                                        if R_Det then
                                            CG:FireServer(R_Det, currentTRoot, R_Weld)
                                        end
                                        CreateGrabLine:FireServer(currentTRoot, Vector3.zero, currentTRoot.Position, false)
                                    end)
                                end
                            else
                                blobRoot.CFrame = SavedPos
                                blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                break
                            end
                            
                            service.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                        end
                    end
                    
                    Config.KickspamT = false
                    if blobRoot then
                        blobRoot.CFrame = SavedPos
                        blobRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end)
            end
        else
            Config.DRKickLo = false
            Config.KickspamT = false
        end
    end
})

Bring:AddButton({
    Name = 'Bring   <font face="GothamBlack" color="rgb(255,255,255)">GRAB</font>',
    Callback = function()
        if not Config.PlayerList or #Config.PlayerList == 0 then
            return
        end

        local character = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local root = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        
        local oldCFrame = root.CFrame

        for _, playerName in ipairs(Config.PlayerList) do
            local Player = service.Players:FindFirstChild(playerName)
            
            if Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                local playerhend = Player.Character.Head

                for i = 1, 2 do
                    if not Player.Character or not Player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then break end
                    root.CFrame = playerhend.CFrame * CFrame.new(2, 0, 0)
                    local args = { [1] = playerhend, [2] = root.CFrame }
                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(unpack(args))
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end

                task.wait(0.1)
                
                root.CFrame = oldCFrame
                local front = oldCFrame.LookVector * 5
                playerhend.CFrame = CFrame.new(oldCFrame.Position + front)

                local destroyArgs = { [1] = playerhend }
                DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(unpack(destroyArgs))
                
            end
        end
    end
})

Antiinput:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Burger",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        local function FWD(parent, part, time)
