            return parent:FindFirstChild(part) or parent:WaitForChild(part, time)
        end

        local function disc(con)
            if Config.Contuuti and Config.Contuuti[con] then 
                Config.Contuuti[con]:Disconnect()
                Config.Contuuti[con] = nil
            end 
        end

        Config.Antibugerplayer = Value
        local char = GetCharacter()
        local hrp = HRP()
        
        if Value then
            if Config.PlayerList then
                for _, adada in ipairs(Config.PlayerList) do
                    local TargetInv = workspace:FindFirstChild(adada .. "SpawnedInToys")
                    if not TargetInv then continue end

                    for _, v in pairs(TargetInv:GetDescendants()) do 
                        if v.Name == "HoldPart" then 
                            local HoldFunction = v:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
                            local DropFunction = v:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
                            repeat 
                                HoldFunction = v.Parent and v:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
                                DropFunction = v.Parent and v:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
                                if not HoldFunction then break end
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    HoldFunction:InvokeServer(v.Parent, char)
                                end)
                            until not v or not v.Parent or not HoldFunction or (v["RigidConstraint"].Attachment1 and v["RigidConstraint"].Attachment1 == char["Left Arm"].LeftGripAttachment) or not Config.Antibugerplayer 
                            if DropFunction then 
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    DropFunction:InvokeServer(v.Parent)
                                end)
                            end
                            v.Name = "NotHoldPart"
                        end
                    end
                    
                    local ad = "StealBurger_" .. adada
                    disc(ad)
                    
                    Config.Contuuti[ad] = TargetInv.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                        if desc.Name == "HoldPart" then 
                            task.spawn(function()
                                local suc
                                FWD(desc, "HoldItemRemoteFunction"):InvokeServer(desc.Parent, char)
                                suc, _ = pcall(function() 
                                    FWD(desc, "HoldItemRemoteFunction"):InvokeServer(desc.Parent, char)
                                end)
                                if suc then 
                                    repeat  
                                        if not desc or not desc.Parent then break end
                                        if desc.AssemblyRootPart ~= hrp then 
                                            FWD(desc, "HoldItemRemoteFunction"):InvokeServer(desc.Parent, char)
                                        end
                                        if not desc or not desc.Parent then break end 
                                        if desc.AssemblyRootPart ~= hrp then continue end 
                                        suc, _ = pcall(function() FWD(desc, "HoldItemRemoteFunction"):InvokeServer(desc.Parent, char); end)
                                        task.wait()
                                    until not suc or not desc or not Config.Antibugerplayer
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        else 
            if Config.Contuuti then
                for k, _ in pairs(Config.Contuuti) do
                    if string.find(k, "StealBurger_") then
                        disc(k)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end    
})

AntiAntiKick:AddToggle({
    Name = "Anti-Anti-Kick",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.AntiAntiKick = Value
        if Config.AntiAntiKick then 
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.AntiAntiKick and task.wait() do 
                    local list = Config.PlayerList
                    if not list or #list == 0 then continue end

                    for _, targetName in ipairs(list) do
                        local TargetPLR = service.Players:FindFirstChild(targetName)
                        local TargetInv = workspace:FindFirstChild(targetName .. "SpawnedInToys")
                        
                        if not TargetPLR or not TargetInv then continue end
                        
                        local Root = TargetPLR.Character and TargetPLR.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if not Root then continue end 
                        
                        local FirePlayerPart = Root:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart")
                        if not FirePlayerPart then continue end
                        for _, v in pairs(TargetInv:GetChildren()) do 
                            local StickyPart = v:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
                            if StickyPart then 
                                if StickyPart.CanQuery then 
                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        for _, part in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do  
                                            if part:IsA("BasePart") then  
                                                part.CanCollide = false 
                                                part.CanTouch = false 
                                                part.CanQuery = false
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end)
                                end
                                
                                local hasOwner = StickyPart:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") ~= nil and StickyPart["PartOwner"].Value == localPlayer.Name
                                if not hasOwner then 
                                    SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(StickyPart)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end    
})

Rag:AddToggle({
    Name = 'Loop Banana',
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        Config.Bananaragodll = Value
        
        if Value then
            if not Config.PlayerList or #Config.PlayerList == 0 then
                Config.Bananaragodll = false
                return
            end
            
            task.spawn(function()
                char = localPlayer.Character
                hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                
                if not hrp then
                    Config.Bananaragodll = false
                    return
                end
                
                SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("FoodBanana", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 0), Vector3.new(0, 170, 0))
                task.wait(0.5)
                
                toysFolder = service.Workspace:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                Config.CurrrenBanana = toysFolder and toysFolder:FindFirstChild("FoodBanana")
                
                if not Config.CurrrenBanana then
                    Config.Bananaragodll = false
                    return
                end
                
                Config.CurrrenBanana.Name = "Rbanana"
                
                holdPart = Config.CurrrenBanana:FindFirstChild("HoldPart")
                holdRemote = holdPart and holdPart:FindFirstChild("HoldItemRemoteFunction")
                if holdRemote then
                    holdRemote:InvokeServer(Config.CurrrenBanana, char)
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
                
                UseRemote:FireServer(Config.CurrrenBanana)
                task.wait(3)
                
                dropRemote = holdPart and holdPart:FindFirstChild("DropItemRemoteFunction")
                if dropRemote then
                    dropRemote:InvokeServer(Config.CurrrenBanana, hrp.CFrame, Vector3.new(0, 170, 0))
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
                
                local bananaParam = Config.CurrrenBanana
                if not bananaParam then
                    Config.Currrrpeel = nil
                else
                    Config.Currrrpeel = bananaParam:FindFirstChild("BananaPeel")
                end
                
                hitboxPart = Config.CurrrenBanana:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
                
                if not Config.Currrrpeel then
                    Config.Bananaragodll = false
                    return
                end
                
                if hitboxPart then
                    local partParam = hitboxPart
                    if partParam and partParam.Parent then
                        pcall(function()
                            CreateGrabLine:FireServer(partParam, partParam.CFrame)
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            
                            ExtendGrabLine:FireServer(8.5)
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            
                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(partParam, partParam.CFrame)
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            
                            DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(partParam)
                        end)
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
                
                local peelParam = Config.Currrrpeel
                if peelParam then
                    for _, child in ipairs(peelParam:GetChildren()) do
                        if child.Name == "HitboxPart" and child:IsA("BasePart") then
                            child.Size = child.Size * Config.hitobox
                            child.Transparency = 0.8
                            child.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                while Config.Bananaragodll do
                    if not Config.PlayerList or #Config.PlayerList == 0 then
                        Config.Bananaragodll = false
                        break
                    end

                    for _, playerName in ipairs(Config.PlayerList) do
                        if not Config.Bananaragodll then break end

                        player = service.Players:FindFirstChild(playerName)
                        if player then
                            char = player.Character
                            if char then
                                PlayerHRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                
                                if PlayerHRP and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                                    if not Config.Currrrpeel or not Config.Currrrpeel.Parent or not Config.Bananaragodll then
                                        Config.Bananaragodll = false
                                        break
                                    end
                                    
                                    hitboxPart = Config.CurrrenBanana and Config.CurrrenBanana:FindFirstChild("HitboxPart")
                                    
                                    if hitboxPart then
                                        local partParam = hitboxPart
                                        if partParam and partParam.Parent then
                                            pcall(function()
                                                CreateGrabLine:FireServer(partParam, partParam.CFrame)
                                                task.wait(0.05)
                                                ExtendGrabLine:FireServer(8.5)
                                                task.wait(0.05)
                                                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(partParam, partParam.CFrame)
                                                task.wait(0.05)
                                                DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(partParam)
                                            end)
                                        end
                                    end
                                    
                                    if Config.Bananaragodll and Config.Currrrpeel then
                                        for _, child in ipairs(Config.Currrrpeel:GetChildren()) do
                                            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    child.CFrame = PlayerHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -2, 0)
                                                end)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    
                                    if hitboxPart and Config.Bananaragodll then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            hitboxPart.CFrame = PlayerHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -2, 0)
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
                
                if Config.CurrrenBanana and Config.CurrrenBanana.Parent then
                    DestroyToy:FireServer(Config.CurrrenBanana)
                end
                Config.CurrrenBanana = nil
                Config.Currrrpeel = nil
                
            end)
        else
            Config.Bananaragodll = false
            
            if Config.CurrrenBanana and Config.CurrrenBanana.Parent then
                DestroyToy:FireServer(Config.CurrrenBanana)
            end
            Config.CurrrenBanana = nil
            Config.Currrrpeel = nil
            
        end
    end
})

ShowB:AddToggle({
    Name = 'Loop SnowBall',
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(enabled)
        local playerCharacter = localPlayer.Character or localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()

        localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            playerCharacter = character
        end)

        local targetName = Config and Config.PlayerList and Config.PlayerList[1] or nil

        function showfli(min, max)
            return Vector3.new(math.random(min, max), math.random(min, max), math.random(min, max))
        end

        local function spswno(objectName)
            task.spawn(function()
                local rotation = Vector3.new(0, 9, 10)
                pcall(function()
                    SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(objectName,
                        playerCharacter.Head.CFrame, rotation)
                end)
            end)
        end

        function yukimasw(objectName)
            if not SpawnedInToys then
            end

            if SpawnedInToys then
                for _, item in pairs(SpawnedInToys:GetChildren()) do
                    if item.Name == objectName then
                        return item
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function tpsnow(item, Player)
            if item and Player and Player.Character then
                local playerPart = Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if playerPart then
                    local targetPos = playerPart.CFrame
                    item.CFrame = targetPos
                    item.AssemblyLinearVelocity = showfli(-100, 130)
                    item.AssemblyAngularVelocity = showfli(-500, 500)
                end
            end
        end

        function yukidamaspeed()
            while Config.autoshow do
                pcall(function()
                    local obj = yukimasw(Config.seobna)
                    if not obj then
                        spswno(Config.seobna)
                        task.wait(0.7)
                    else

                        local currentTargets = Config and Config.PlayerList or {}
                        
                        for _, currentTargetName in pairs(currentTargets) do
                            local Player = service.Players:FindFirstChild(currentTargetName)

                        if Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            for _, child in pairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
                                if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    tpsnow(child, Player)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                task.wait(0.3)
            end
        end

        function freeyukidama(enabled)
            Config.autoshow = enabled
            if enabled then
                task.spawn(yukidamaspeed)
            end
        end

        if enabled then
            freeyukidama(true)

            Config.mr34lAcon = coroutine.create(function()
                while true do
                    pcall(function()
                        local character = localPlayer.Character
                        if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            local root = character.HumanoidRootPart

                            for _, player in pairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if SpawnedInToys then
                                    for _, obj in pairs(SpawnedInToys:GetChildren()) do
                                        coroutine.wrap(function()
                                            if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "BallSnowball" then
                                                local soundPart = obj:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                                if soundPart and soundPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                                                    local dist = (soundPart.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                                                    if dist <= Config.aRadi then
                                                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame)
                                                        task.wait(0)
                                                        local velocity =
                                                            soundPart:FindFirstChild("l") or
                                                                Instance.new("BodyVelocity", soundPart)
                                                        velocity.Name = "l"
                                                        velocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                                        velocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                                                        service.Debris:AddItem(velocity, 0)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end)()
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)

                    if PlotItems then
                        for i = 1, 5 do
                            local pt = PlotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot" .. i)
                            if pt then
                                for _, obj in pairs(pt:GetChildren()) do
                                    coroutine.wrap(function()
                                        if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "BallSnowball" then
                                            local soundPart = obj:FindFirstChild("SoundPart")
                                            if soundPart and soundPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                                                local character = localPlayer.Character
                                                if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                                    local root = character.HumanoidRootPart
                                                    local dist = (soundPart.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                                                    if dist <= Config.aRadi then
                                                        SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(soundPart, soundPart.CFrame)
                                                        task.wait(0)
                                                        local velocity =
                                                            soundPart:FindFirstChild("byy") or Instance.new("BodyVelocity", soundPart)
                                                        velocity.Name = "veshoo"
                                                        velocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                                        velocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                                                        service.Debris:AddItem(velocity, 0)
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end)()
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
            coroutine.resume(Config.mr34lAcon)
        else
            freeyukidama(false)
            if Config.mr34lAcon then
                coroutine.close(Config.mr34lAcon)
                Config.mr34lAcon = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

KilKick:AddToggle({
    Name = 'Loop Kick',
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.LoopKickGrab = state
        if state then
            while Config.LoopKickGrab do
                Config.SavedCFrame = PLCF()
                for _, name in pairs(Config.PlayerList) do
                    local target = service.Players:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if IsValidTarget(target) then
                        local root = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if root then
                            for _ = 0, 50 do
                                NoclipON()
                                ROS(root)
                                
                                if not IsValidTarget(target) or not Config.LoopKickGrab or (CNWOSHIPOPl(target) or root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 500) then
                                    DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(root)
                                    wait()
                                    CSV(root)
                                    break
                                end
                                
                                wait()
                                if root.Position.Y <= -12 then
                                    TPPlayer(CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)))
                                else
                                    TPPlayer(CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                TPPlayer(Config.SavedCFrame)
                wait(0.2)
            end
            NoclipOFF()
            TPPlayer(Config.SavedCFrame)
        end
    end,
})

KilKick:AddToggle({
    Name = 'Loop Kill',
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.LoopKillGrab = state
        if state then
            while Config.LoopKillGrab do
                Config.SavedCFrame = PLCF()
                for _, name in pairs(Config.PlayerList) do
                    local target = service.Players:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if IsValidTarget(target) then
                        local root = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local hum = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        
                        if root and hum then
                            for _ = 0, 50 do
                                NoclipON()
                                ROS(root)
                                
                                if not IsValidTarget(target) or not Config.LoopKillGrab or (CNWOSHIPOPl(target) or root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 500) then
                                    DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(root)
                                    CSV(root)
                                    break
                                end
                                
                                task.wait()
                                if root.Position.Y <= -12 then
                                    TPPlayer(CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15)))
                                else
                                    TPPlayer(CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)))
                                end
                                
                                hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                                hum.Jump = true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                TPPlayer(Config.SavedCFrame)
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
            NoclipOFF()
            TPPlayer(Config.SavedCFrame)
        end
    end,
})

KI:AddToggle({
    Name = "Kick All",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.Kiclall = state
        if state then
            while Config.Kiclall do
                local oldPos = PLCF()
                for _, target in pairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if CanAttack(target) then
                        local root = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                        for _ = 0, 50 do
                            NoclipON()
                            ROS(root)
                            if not CanAttack(target) or not Config.Kiclall or CNWOSHIPOPl(target) or root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 500 then
                                CSV(root)
                                DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(root)
                                break
                            end
                            task.wait()
                            local offset = (root.Position.Y <= -12) and Vector3.new(0, 5, -15) or Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)
                            TPPlayer(CFrame.new(root.Position + offset))
                        end
                    end
                end
                TPPlayer(oldPos)
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
            NoclipOFF()
        end
    end
})

KIL:AddToggle({
    Name = "Kill All",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.Killall = state
        if state then
            while Config.Killall do
                local oldPos = PLCF()
                for _, target in pairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if CanAttack(target) then
                        local root = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                        local hum = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                        for _ = 0, 50 do
                            NoclipON()
                            ROS(root)

                            if not CanAttack(target) or not Config.Killall or CNWOSHIPOPl(target) or root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 500 then
                                CSV(root)
                                DestroyGrabLine:FireServer(root)
                                break
                            end
                            
                            task.wait()
                            local offset = (root.Position.Y <= -12) and Vector3.new(0, 5, -15) or Vector3.new(0, -10, -10)
                            TPPlayer(CFrame.new(root.Position + offset))
                            
                            hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                            hum.Jump, hum.Sit = true, false
                        end
                    end
                end
                TPPlayer(oldPos)
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
            NoclipOFF()
        end
    end
})

BR:AddToggle({
    Name = "Bring All",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.BringAllToggle = state
        
        if state then
            if Config.cameraAnchor then
                local camera = service.Workspace.CurrentCamera
                if camera and Config.originalCameraSubject then
                    camera.CameraSubject = Config.originalCameraSubject
                end
                Config.cameraAnchor:Destroy()
                Config.cameraAnchor = nil
                Config.originalCameraSubject = nil
            end
            
            coroutine.wrap(function()
                local rootPart = GetRoot()
                local playerCFrame = rootPart and rootPart.CFrame
                if not playerCFrame then return end

                local camCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
                    service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(-15, 15, 0),
                    playerCFrame.Position
                )
                service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = camCFrame

                while Config.BringAllToggle do
                    if not Config.freezePart then
                        Config.freezePart = Instance.new("Part")
                        Config.freezePart.Anchored = true
                        Config.freezePart.CanCollide = false
                        Config.freezePart.Transparency = 1
                        Config.freezePart.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                        Config.freezePart.Parent = service.Workspace
                    end
                    Config.freezePart.CFrame = camCFrame
                    service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Follow
                    service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Config.freezePart

                    for _, player in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        local isTargetValid = false
                        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then
                            local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                                if not IsPlayerInsideSafeZone(player) then
                                    local pRoot = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    local velMag = pRoot and pRoot.Velocity.Magnitude or 0
                                    if velMag < 20 then
                                        isTargetValid = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        if isTargetValid then
                            local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            local ragdolled = hum and hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled")
                            
                            if root and hum and ragdolled then
                                for _ = 1, 50 do
                                    if not Config.BringAllToggle then break end
                                    
                                    if not Config.floatConnection then
                                        Config.floatConnection = service.RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
                                            if (Config.BringAllToggle) and localPlayer.Character then
                                                for _, part in ipairs(localPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                                                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end)
                                    end
                                    
                                    local snowShipSuccess = false
                                    if root then
                                        if root:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and root.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                                            snowShipSuccess = true
                                        else
                                            local localRoot = GetRoot()
                                            if SetNetworkOwner and localRoot then
                                                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(root, CFrame.lookAt(localRoot.Position, root.Position))
                                            end
                                            for _ = 1, 5 do
                                                task.wait()
                                                if root:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and root.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                                                    snowShipSuccess = true
                                                    break
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end

                                    if CNWOSHIPOPl(player) then
                                        if not ragdolled.Value and player:DistanceFromCharacter(playerCFrame.Position) > 10 then
                                            root.CFrame = playerCFrame
                                        end
                                        
                                        local bp = root:FindFirstChild("BringBody")
                                        if not bp then
                                            bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                                            bp.Name = "BringBody"
                                            bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                                            bp.D = 5000
                                            bp.P = 1500000
                                            bp.Parent = root
                                        end
                                        bp.Position = typeof(playerCFrame) == "CFrame" and playerCFrame.Position or playerCFrame
                                        break
                                    end

                                    task.wait()
                                    local localRoot = GetRoot()
                                    if localRoot then
                                        if root.Position.Y <= -9 then
                                            localRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, -15))
                                        else
                                            localRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, -10))
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    local localRoot = GetRoot()
                    if localRoot then localRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(527, 123, -376) end
                    task.wait()
                end

                local hum = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum end
                service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                
                if Config.floatConnection then
                    Config.floatConnection:Disconnect()
                    Config.floatConnection = nil
                end
                
                local localRoot = GetRoot()
                if localRoot then localRoot.CFrame = playerCFrame end
            end)()
        else
            local hum = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum end
            service.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            
            if Config.floatConnection then
                Config.floatConnection:Disconnect()
                Config.floatConnection = nil
            end
        end
    end,
})

    Callback = function(state)
        Config.WhitelistFriends2 = state
    end

    Options = {"Aura", "TP"},
    Callback = function(selectedMode)
        Config.Autoownersipp = selectedMode
    end

AutoOwner:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Ownership",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(vass)
        Config.Autoownership = vass
        if vass then
            task.spawn(function()
                while Config.Autoownership do
                    for an3, an4 in pairs(Config.Anchorparts) do
                        if not Config.Autoownership then break end
                        
                        if typeof(an4.PartAnchored) == "Instance" and (not an3:GetAttribute("AnchorOwnership")) then
                            local shoooa = an4.PartAnchored
                            
                            if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                local hrp = localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                                local DFC = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(shoooa.Position)
                                
                                if shoooa:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") and shoooa.PartOwner.Value == localPlayer.Name then
                                    an3:SetAttribute("AnchorOwnership", true)
                                else
                                    if Config.Autoownersipp == "Aura" then
                                        if DFC <= 30 then
                                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(shoooa, lookAt(hrp.Position, shoooa.Position))
                                        end
                                    elseif Config.Autoownersipp == "TP" then
                                        if DFC <= 30 then
                                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(shoooa, lookAt(hrp.Position, shoooa.Position))
                                        else
                                            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(shoooa.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                                            task.wait(0.04)
                                            
                                            SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(shoooa, lookAt(hrp.Position, shoooa.Position))
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0.2)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

local function UpdatePlayerDropdown()
    local p_list = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(service.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= service.Players.LocalPlayer then
            local entry = string.format("%s (ID: %s)", player.DisplayName, player.Name)
            table.insert(p_list, entry)
        end
    end 
    
    AAGGG:Refresh(p_list, true)
end

service.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(UpdatePlayerDropdown)
service.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(UpdatePlayerDropdown)

local function RefreshDropdown()
    PlayerSelectDropdown:Refresh(GetPlayerListForDropdown(), true)
end

service.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(RefreshDropdown)
service.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function()
    task.wait()
    RefreshDropdown()
end)

workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
    if Config.PCLDToggle then
        local partname = {"partesp", "playercharacterlocationdetector"}
        local isTargetObj = false
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            for _, name in ipairs(partname) do
                if string.lower(obj.Name) == string.lower(name) then
                    isTargetObj = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        if isTargetObj then
        end
    end
end)

service.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child.Name ~= "GrabParts" then return end

    local grabPart = child:FindFirstChild("GrabPart")
    local playerPart = grabPart and grabPart.WeldConstraint.Part1
    
    if playerPart and Config.SupDADG then
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "SuperStrength"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        bv.Parent = playerPart

        task.spawn(function()
            local contextGui = localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ContextActionGui")
            if not contextGui then return end

            for _, v in pairs(contextGui:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("ImageLabel") and v.Image == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9603678090" then
                    local button = v.Parent
                    button.Active = true
                    
                    local connection
                    connection = button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
                        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        bv.Velocity = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.lookVector * Config.Strength
                    end)

                    child.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
                        if not child.Parent then connection:Disconnect() end
                    end)
                    break
                end
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            local connection
            connection = child:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
                if not child.Parent then
                    if service.UserInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
                        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        bv.Velocity = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.lookVector * Config.Strength
                        service.Debris:AddItem(bv, 1)
                    else
                        bv:Destroy()
                    end
                    connection:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
end)

local function alllabel()
    if not Config.AccountAgeDays then
        Config.AccountAgeDays = localPlayer.AccountAge
    end

    if not Config.GameName then
        local success, placeInfo = pcall(function()
            return service.MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(Config.PlaceId)
        end)
        if success and placeInfo then
            Config.GameName = placeInfo.Name
        else
            Config.GameName = "Unknown Game"
        end
    end

    local now = os.time()
    local timeString = os.date("%X", now)
    
    local diff = now - Config.exescripttime
    local execTimeString = string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(diff / 3600), math.floor((diff % 3600) / 60), diff % 60)

    Config.FrameFps = Config.FrameFps + 1
    local currentClock = os.clock()
    if currentClock - Config.lastfpsti >= 1 then
        Config.currfpsw = Config.FrameFps
        Config.FrameFps = 0
        Config.lastfpsti = currentClock
    end

    Config.CurrentPing = math.round(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 1000)

    pcall(function()
        if Label_Time and type(Label_Time.Set) == "function" then
            Label_Time:Set("Time: " .. timeString)
        end
        if Label_ExecTime and type(Label_ExecTime.Set) == "function" then
            Label_ExecTime:Set("Execution Time: " .. execTimeString)
        end
        if Label_PlayerName and type(Label_PlayerName.Set) == "function" then
            Label_PlayerName:Set(string.format("%s (ID: %s)", localPlayer.DisplayName, localPlayer.Name))
        end
        if Label_AccountAge and type(Label_AccountAge.Set) == "function" then
            Label_AccountAge:Set("AccountAge: " .. Config.AccountAgeDays .. "days")
        end
        if Label_PlayerId and type(Label_PlayerId.Set) == "function" then
            Label_PlayerId:Set(tostring(localPlayer.UserId))
        end
        
        if Label_Fps and type(Label_Fps.Set) == "function" then
            Label_Fps:Set(string.format("FPS: %d", Config.currfpsw))
        end
        if Label_Ping and type(Label_Ping.Set) == "function" then
            Label_Ping:Set(string.format("Ping: %dms", Config.CurrentPing))
        end
        
        if Label_GameInfo and type(Label_GameInfo.Set) == "function" then
            Label_GameInfo:Set(string.format("%s (%d)", Config.GameName, Config.PlaceId))
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        local plot = GetPlot()
        if not plot then continue end

        local timeObj = plot:FindFirstChild("TimeRemainingNum", true)

        if timeObj and timeObj:IsA("IntValue") then
            local timeValue = timeObj.Value

            pcall(function()
                if TimeLabel and type(TimeLabel.Set) == "function" then
                    TimeLabel:Set("House-Time: " .. timeValue)
                end
            end)

            local hrp = HRP()
            if hrp then
                if Config.HouseTPauto then
                    if timeValue <= 14 then
                        if not Config.isTeleported then
                            Config.savedPosition = hrp.CFrame
                            Config.isTeleported = true

                            if plot:FindFirstChild("Teleport") then
                                hrp.CFrame = plot.Teleport.CFrame
                            else
                                hrp.CFrame = plot:GetModelCFrame()
                            end
                        end
                    elseif timeValue > 15 then
                        if Config.isTeleported and Config.savedPosition then
                            hrp.CFrame = Config.savedPosition
                            Config.isTeleported = false
                            Config.savedPosition = nil
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local ownerName = nil
        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if sign then
            local ownersFolder = sign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
            if ownersFolder then
                local valueObject = ownersFolder:FindFirstChild("Value")
                if valueObject and valueObject:IsA("StringValue") then
                    ownerName = valueObject.Value
                end
            end
        end

        pcall(function()
            if OwnerLabel and type(OwnerLabel.Set) == "function" then
                if ownerName and ownerName ~= "" then
                    OwnerLabel:Set("House-Owner: " .. ownerName)
                else
                    OwnerLabel:Set("House-Owner: ?")
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

service.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(alllabel)
Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(GrabParts)
FrostNotify("Welcome"  .. localPlayer.Name .. "!")
